on:
  push:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build-and-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          dotnet-version: 10.0.x

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: lts/*

      - name: Build and test
        run: scripts/run-all.sh

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        if: always()
        with:
          name: artifacts
          path: artifacts/
          if-no-files-found: ignore

name: Deploy to velixir

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    concurrency:
      group: velixir-deploy
      cancel-in-progress: false
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Install the velixir CLI
        shell: bash
        run: |
          curl -fsSL https://velixir.net/install.sh | sh
          echo "$HOME/.velixir/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Deploy
        env:
          VELIXIR_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.VELIXIR_API_KEY }}
        run: |
          if [ -z "$VELIXIR_API_KEY" ]; then
            echo "::error::Add a repository secret named VELIXIR_API_KEY with a velixir API key that has Write permission (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions)."
            exit 1
          fi
          velixir deploy --app e6f606e7-3aba-4b3a-85df-945eb931dc97 --source-dir ./src/Chaos.Web

# chaos-velixir-run

A message board, a shared drawing canvas, a shared slot machine and a shared latency test that everyone sees live and nobody keeps. Blazor Server on .NET 10, on the [velixir](https://velixir.net) free plan (0.25 vCPU, 256 MB). Live at https://chaos.velixir.run/.

- Board: the server keeps the newest 50 messages; each browser keeps up to 10,000 in IndexedDB and offers its newest 50 back after a server restart. Dotted border means the server no longer has it. Your own posts show `Your message` (this browser only). Optional: after the poster opts in and grants geolocation, each post carries a collapsed `Details` list with the browser's location, time zone and device details. No lookups, no third parties, no IP stored.
- Canvas: one black pen on a shared square page, live for everyone, one reset button. Browsers keep finished strokes (10,000) and the latest reset, and offer back up to 500 strokes after a server restart.
- Slots: one shared machine. A press draws five symbols (A–Z, 0–9) on the server and locks the button for everyone until the reels stop. Three of a kind or a five-symbol straight wins. Four or five of a kind, or an unbroken sequence along `0-9A-Z` (`ABCDE`, `43210`), is a jackpot: everyone watching sees a celebration, and jackpots are never deleted, on the server or in the browser. Results are kept like board messages, plus the newest 500 winners. Tapping a winner shows its odds and the probability behind them. Ten successful presses unlock auto spin, which also runs in background tabs.
- Latency: one button times public endpoints from the browser and from the server. One test at a time, one per network every 5 minutes. Results are kept like board messages, without identity. Below them: the 10 fastest and slowest browser and server medians, then jackpots (a median under 10 ms wins, 100 ms or more loses). Browsers offer back their own 10 best and worst of each after a restart. Ten tests unlock auto run.
- Every page footer shows the ticking server time.
- No third-party code, no cookies, no accounts. Strict CSP with no inline scripts; `connect-src` also lists the latency endpoints.

## Requirements

- .NET SDK 10.0.100 or later in the 10.0 band
- Node.js 22 or later (JavaScript tests)
- Podman 4 or later (end-to-end tests)

## Commands

```
dotnet run --project src/Chaos.Web
scripts/run-all.sh [--skip-e2e] [--skip-js] [--skip-export]
```

`run-all.sh` builds with warnings as errors, runs the .NET and JavaScript tests with coverage, runs Playwright in Podman, then writes `docs/llm/dump.txt`. Output lands in `artifacts/`. Exit code 2 is a usage error, 127 a missing tool.

## Layout

| Path | What |
| --- | --- |
| `src/Chaos.Web` | The app. The only folder velixir builds. |
| `tests/Chaos.Web.Tests` | xUnit v3 unit and integration tests |
| `tests/Chaos.E2E` | Playwright tests, run in Podman |
| `tests/js` | Vitest tests for the hand-written modules |
| `scripts` | Build, test and export scripts |
| `docs/adr` | Architecture decision records |

## Deploy

Every push to `main` runs `.github/workflows/velixir.yml`, which uploads `src/Chaos.Web` only. It needs the `VELIXIR_API_KEY` repository secret. Every push also runs `.github/workflows/ci.yml`. Turn on the HTTP health probe (`/health`) under Scaling, Runtime in velixir.

Limits can be changed without a rebuild through environment variables, for example `Chaos__MaxConnections=60`. See `src/Chaos.Web/Infrastructure/ChaosOptions.cs`. `Chaos__SlotDrawSymbols=7` makes every spin a jackpot, for local testing only.

## License

AGPL-3.0-or-later. See `LICENSE` and `REUSE.toml`.

# 0001 Record decisions as ADRs

Status: accepted

Decisions that were ambiguous in the brief, or that trade one property for another, get a short record here. Each record states the decision and its cost. Records are updated when the code changes.

FILE:     docs/adr/0002-rendering-model.md
SIZE:     1.53 KB (1564 bytes)
LINES:    10
SHA256:   4b918c2f595f78d457afd26cf0c227bd7eb701701193d105695f26966d61c5dc

# 0002 Static SSR with interactive islands

Status: accepted

- Every page is static server-side rendering. Navigation uses Blazor enhanced navigation and needs no circuit.
- `/board`, `/canvas`, `/slots` and `/latency` each host one `InteractiveServer` island (`BoardIsland`, `CanvasIsland`, `SlotIsland`, `LatencyIsland`). Only they open a circuit. Blazor closes the circuit when no island remains on the page.
- The board, slots and latency feeds are rendered by the static page and then owned by the island's JavaScript module. It is marked `data-permanent` so enhanced navigation does not overwrite it. Up to 10,000 local messages never enter a server render tree.
- Enhanced navigation morphs elements of the old page into the new one by tag and position. JavaScript never writes into page markup outside an island or a `data-permanent` element: the board once announced into a trailing `<p>` that became the canvas page's note. Screen reader announcements go to one `#announcer` in the layout (`data-permanent`, cleared on `enhancedload`), and modules stop announcing once disposed or detached.
- The compose form and the reset button are Blazor-rendered. The character count is an empty island element that JavaScript fills on `input`; the island asks for a recount after a post clears the box. Drawing, IndexedDB and the slot machine controls are JavaScript; `SlotIsland` renders its markup once and never re-renders.
- No bUnit: components are thin; logic lives in services tested directly, and the pages are covered by integration and Playwright tests.

FILE:     docs/adr/0003-deploy-only-the-web-project.md

# 0003 Deploy only the web project

Status: accepted

velixir builds with a Nixpacks-style plan: `dotnet restore` and `dotnet publish -c Release -o out` at the upload root, then starts `./out/<first csproj>`. Uploading the repository root would restore, build and publish the test projects too, including the Playwright driver, onto a 1 GB disk.

- The deploy workflow runs `velixir deploy --app <id> --source-dir ./src/Chaos.Web`, the documented monorepo option.
- `Chaos.Web.csproj` is self-sufficient: target framework, nullable, implicit usings and runtime settings are in the project, not only in `Directory.Build.props`. `global.json`, central package management and analyzers do not reach the velixir build, which is fine because the project has no package references.
- The start command runs from the upload root, so the content root is the source folder, not `out/`. The web root is set to `wwwroot` next to the assembly when it exists, so published assets are served. The content root is left alone because `WebApplicationFactory` sets it.
- The app binds to `PORT` when set, like zen.
- Deploys are not gated on CI, matching zen. A failing velixir build keeps the previous release; the `/health` probe stops a release that does not start.

# 0004 UUIDv7 ids and handshake rules

Status: accepted

- The server creates every id with `Guid.CreateVersion7` from its own clock. Clients never mint ids. Timestamps are read from the id.
- The id string sorts in time order, so it is also the IndexedDB key and the sort key everywhere. Two posts in the same millisecond sort in random order; that is accepted.
- Handshake: the client offers its 50 newest local messages, newest first, in chunks under 24 KB so no SignalR message exceeds the 32 KB limit. The server considers at most 50 per circuit.
- An offered message is accepted only if its id is a valid UUIDv7, its time is not in the future, its time is before the server started, and its text is already normalized, and its `meta` is absent or already normalized (ADR 0022). Then it is merged; the server keeps the newest 50 and keeps its own copy on id ties.
- The start-time rule rejects forged recent messages: anything stamped after the server started came from this server, so if the server does not have it, it is forged or already evicted. It costs posts made to an old instance during a rolling deploy; those stay in local history.
- Posts: 500 UTF-16 code units after normalization, cut on grapheme boundaries; the same text as one of the 10 newest messages is refused.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0005-local-history.md
SIZE:     1.30 KB (1336 bytes)
LINES:    12
SHA256:   28bf69904c579c304f14befcb97e438bd13a0c9422a536c01bd42784791ad4c3
MODIFIED: 2026-10-31 02:28:55 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0005 IndexedDB local history

Status: accepted

- Database `chaos-board`, store `messages`, key path `id`, values `{ id, text, meta?, mine? }` (ADR 0022). Slots use `chaos-slots` (v2), store `spins`, values `{ id, text, mine }`, plus an uncapped `jackpots` store (ADR 0019), and latency uses `chaos-latency`, store `runs`, same shape, through the same `LocalHistory` exported by `BoardIsland.razor.js`. The canvas uses `chaos-canvas` (ADR 0006).
- Schema changes are numbered migrations in `BoardIsland.razor.js`; `onupgradeneeded` runs every step from the stored version. A database from a newer schema (`VersionError`) is deleted and recreated, since the server can refill it.
- Other tabs close their connection on `versionchange`, so upgrades and deletes are not blocked for long.
- Cap: 10,000 messages. After each write, the oldest are deleted with one key-range delete.
- The feed shows the server window plus 100 archived messages, with a button for 100 more. Items use `content-visibility: auto`.
- Live messages have a solid border, messages the server no longer has a dotted one.
- The About page deletes all four databases and the slot and latency counters without touching the theme. The next visit to the board recreates it from the server snapshot.
- If IndexedDB is unavailable the board still works, without history.

FILE:     docs/adr/0006-canvas-protocol.md
SIZE:     2.38 KB (2438 bytes)
LINES:    16
SHA256:   8fb3d00411c65256f5f8088a90a78400a044ca6aae9e954a7ac1b7d395b556db
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0006 Canvas coordinates, epochs and storage

Status: accepted

- The page is square (`aspect-ratio: 1`). Points are normalized to 0–1 and sent as integers 0–4095. The brush width is 0.6% of the canvas side, so every device draws the same picture.
- The backing store is CSS size times `devicePixelRatio`. A `ResizeObserver` and a `resolution` media query trigger a full redraw from stored points.
- Input: pointer events with pointer capture, `touch-action: none`, primary pointer only, left mouse button only, coalesced events when available, points closer than 3 units dropped.
- Palm rejection: touches wider or taller than 40 CSS px are ignored, and so is any touch within 1.5 s of pen input.
- Batches go out every 50 ms, at most 256 points, one call in flight. A stroke ends at 2,000 points.
- Every reset increments an epoch. Batches tagged with an old epoch are refused, so strokes in flight during a reset vanish.
- Late joiners get a snapshot of every stored stroke. The store keeps 20,000 points and evicts the oldest finished strokes first. Clients that already drew an evicted stroke keep showing it until the next reset.
- Every stroke and every reset gets a server UUIDv7 (`Uuid7.After` keeps them strictly increasing), so a stroke sorts after the reset it follows. `Draw` replies `"<result> <stroke id>"`.
- Browsers keep finished strokes in IndexedDB `chaos-canvas`: store `strokes` (`{ id, points: Uint16Array }`, cap 10,000, oldest deleted first) and store `meta` (`{ key: 'reset', id }`). A reset deletes every stroke up to its id; strokes older than the stored reset are never saved.
- Snapshots carry `reset`, `started` (server start, Unix ms) and `room` (free points). After each snapshot the browser offers its stored reset if newer than the server's and older than `started`, then its newest 500 strokes newer than both resets, older than `started`, unknown to the server and within `room`, in chunks under 24 KB.
- The server accepts an offered stroke only if it is a valid UUIDv7 from before the server started, newer than the current reset, unknown and well formed, and only while it fits: offers never evict. Accepted strokes go to everyone as a `merge` update. An adopted reset drops older strokes, bumps the epoch and sends everyone a fresh snapshot. A reset made on this server always wins over remembered ones.
- Per circuit: 500 offered strokes, `CanvasMaxPoints` offered points, 4 offered resets.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0007-realtime-delivery.md
SIZE:     1.14 KB (1165 bytes)
LINES:    10
SHA256:   423c4277a2bf0cebc1947e949c5718d379804e9fb3bb82cc1b96736ddac281e4
MODIFIED: 2026-10-29 20:03:06 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0007 Per-client outboxes over the circuit

Status: accepted

- Transport is the Blazor circuit itself: JS calls `[JSInvokable]` methods, the server calls the module's `apply`. No separate SignalR hub and no extra JavaScript client.
- Each island owns a bounded channel (`Outbox<T>`) drained by one pump that sends batches in order and waits for the browser to finish each batch. A full outbox, a failed call or a 20 s stall drops the subscriber.
- The pump waits until the island's module has been attached. JavaScript can call `Join` before `attach` has returned to .NET, and an earlier send would fault the outbox.
- Subscribing enqueues a snapshot under the same lock as updates, so nothing is missed or duplicated.
- Board deltas carry `version` and `previous`; a gap triggers a fresh snapshot. Canvas updates carry an offset per stroke; a gap triggers a fresh snapshot. Slots and latency deltas use the board rules; a latency `state` update only carries `running`, and latency snapshots and changed deltas carry `boards` (ADR 0020).
- The browser pings every 25 s and resubscribes when the server says it was dropped, and resubscribes after every reconnect.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0008-abuse-limits.md
SIZE:     1.99 KB (2033 bytes)
LINES:    32
SHA256:   a6cf021fd0f10b6ac37d37871e39d5af2fa4540b6e36f5aacd3c15ea857f2b46
MODIFIED: 2026-10-31 02:28:55 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0008 Rate and connection limits

Status: accepted

Defaults live in `ChaosOptions` and can be overridden with `Chaos__*` environment variables. velixir's edge limits sit in front of these.

| Limit | Default |
| --- | --- |
| HTTP requests per client | 120 burst, 10 per second |
| WebSocket connections per client | 8 |
| WebSocket connections in total | 80, then 503 |
| Posts per client | 3 burst, 1 every 2 s |
| Message length | 500 |
| Message details | 43 known keys, values 8–512 characters by key |
| Offered messages per circuit | 50 |
| Stroke points per circuit | 4,000 burst, 1,500 per second |
| Points per batch / stroke / canvas | 256 / 2,048 / 20,000 |
| Resets | 1 s global cooldown, 2 burst per client |
| Slot presses | one spin at a time (4 s), 3 burst, 1 every 2 s per client |
| Slot results kept | 50 recent, 500 winners, jackpots never evicted (offers stop at 10,000) |
| Slot winner and jackpot offers per circuit | 500 each |
| Canvas offers per circuit | 500 strokes, 20,000 points, 4 resets |
| Latency tests | one at a time, 10 s apart, one per client every 300 s, 30 s probe budget |
| Latency results kept | 50 recent, 50 jackpot winners, 50 losers, 10 fastest and slowest per side |
| Latency leader offers per circuit | 40 |

- A client is its IP after `X-Forwarded-For`, or its /64 for IPv6. Keys live in memory only and are never logged or shown. Devices behind one NAT are one client.
- The hub (`/_blazor`) only accepts WebSocket connections, so connection counts are real. Clients behind proxies that block WebSockets cannot use the board, canvas or slots.
- The total cap protects the 256 MB container: zen ran out of memory near 100 circuits.
- In-circuit limits use a `TimeProvider` token bucket so tests are deterministic. HTTP limits use ASP.NET Core's rate limiter.
- A press during a spin is refused before the per-client bucket, so waiting players are not penalized.
- There is no moderation. The About page says plainly that content spreads to every client and cannot be recalled.

SHA256:   eb1fb6b1bad94dadad098c659df71aa38dba86dea4b9c04e657b3e0ae0f637a1

# 0009 Security headers, CSP, origin check

Status: accepted

- CSP: `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self' <latency origins>; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'`. No nonce is needed.
- The latency origins come from `LatencyCatalog`, so the policy and the endpoint list cannot drift. They are allowed on every page because enhanced navigation keeps the first document's policy.
- `<ImportMap />` is left out because it emits an inline script. Modules load from fingerprinted URLs through `Assets[...]` instead.
- `Blazor.start` is called from `App.razor.js`, not an inline script. The reconnect UI is our own element, so Blazor injects no styles. No markup uses `style` attributes.
- Also sent: `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, `Permissions-Policy` (`camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(self), payment=(), usb=()`; geolocation for ADR 0022), `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy`, HSTS outside development. No `Server` header. `X-Frame-Options` is omitted as legacy.
- `/_blazor` requests with an `Origin` header must match the request host or `Chaos:AllowedOrigins`. Requests without `Origin` pass, since only browsers can be tricked into cross-site WebSockets.
- Headers are set in `OnStarting` so error pages keep them.

FILE:     docs/adr/0010-no-cookies.md
SIZE:     0.40 KB (414 bytes)

SHA256:   fb06ac2a3a103d90105360d32480212d4b33c33dd43b3142eab8ee12b745c7a6
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0010 No cookies

Status: accepted

Blazor issues an antiforgery cookie on every page that hosts an interactive component. This app has no forms that post to the server, so `CookielessAntiforgery` replaces `IAntiforgery`: it issues no tokens and rejects unsafe form posts. Data protection keys are in memory. The theme is in `localStorage`, board history in IndexedDB. With no cookies there is no consent banner.

FILE:     docs/adr/0011-theme.md
SIZE:     0.61 KB (623 bytes)
LINES:    8
SHA256:   b450c1dbcde8c28fbd73558e395d81c05aaf24333002c17bfed92c799710c768
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0011 Theme without a flash

Status: accepted

- Colors are `light-dark()` tokens on `:root`, which follow `color-scheme`. `data-theme="light"` or `"dark"` on `<html>` forces the scheme; without it the OS setting wins.
- `theme-init.js` is a tiny blocking script in `<head>` that applies the stored choice before first paint. It is an external file, so the CSP needs no hash or nonce.
- Enhanced navigation resets attributes on `<html>`; `App.razor.js` reapplies the theme on `enhancedload`, which fires in the same task as the DOM update.
- The toggle is a two-state button. Clearing site data returns to the OS setting.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0012-reconnect-and-cold-start.md
SIZE:     0.72 KB (735 bytes)
LINES:    9
SHA256:   6ec4a7165c7e01a9b6200650cfff103d464a4dc597b019fcf5c1de8bc2acd5f5
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0012 Reconnect, backoff, cold start

Status: accepted

- The free plan sleeps after 15 idle minutes and restarts without warning.
- Retries: first at once, then exponential with equal jitter (half fixed, half random), capped at 30 s, 30 attempts (about ten minutes).
- `ReconnectBanner` is a non-modal banner driven by Blazor's `components-reconnect-*` classes: "Reconnecting" on the first attempt, "Waking up the server" after it, then a retry button. Retrying also happens when the tab becomes visible again.
- A rejected reconnect means the server lost the circuit, usually a restart, so the page reloads. The islands then rebuild from the server and IndexedDB.
- An unhandled error shows a separate error bar with a reload link.

FILE:     docs/adr/0013-runtime-footprint.md
SIZE:     0.82 KB (840 bytes)
LINES:    12
SHA256:   1686f56d06b35458ad96d91248c108573da51f126ca4b1bcc3e1b21f6731a0e5
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0013 Memory and CPU settings

Status: accepted

- Workstation, non-concurrent GC, `ConserveMemory=7`, no tiered PGO, invariant globalization, English resources only.
- Disconnected circuits: 20 kept for one minute. Persisted circuits: 20 for five minutes. Two unacknowledged render batches per circuit.
- WebSocket compression is off: bandwidth is unmetered, CPU is a quarter core.
- Response compression (Brotli, gzip) at the fastest level for HTML; static assets are precompressed at build time.
- No ReadyToRun and no trimming: velixir runs a portable `dotnet publish` and its CPU architecture is not documented, the framework is already precompiled, and Blazor Server is not trim-safe.
- Logs at `Warning`, except host lifetime messages.
- `/health` is a built-in health check, exempt from rate limiting.
- Shutdown waits up to 10 s.

FILE:     docs/adr/0014-testing.md
SIZE:     1.52 KB (1560 bytes)
LINES:    10
SHA256:   456241424549cbd1b8800d5f033d099d5fa9c3ec1fca4480ee221275f56b9107
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:38:19 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0014 Test strategy

Status: accepted

- .NET: xUnit v3 (package `xunit.v3` 4.x) on Microsoft Testing Platform v2, enabled for `dotnet test` in `global.json`. Unit tests cover services and helpers; integration tests use `WebApplicationFactory` for pages, headers, cookies, the hub guard and compression.
- JavaScript: Vitest in Node tests the pure exports of each module. DOM, IndexedDB, canvas and WebSocket behaviour is covered by Playwright, so no DOM library is installed.
- End to end: Playwright .NET in `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright/dotnet:v<version>-noble`, which ships the .NET 10 SDK and browsers. The version comes from `Directory.Packages.props` only. The app is published and run inside the same container on localhost, in Production mode with relaxed limits and the server latency probe off. A second copy on port 5081 runs with `SlotDrawSymbols=7` so every spin is a jackpot (`CHAOS_RIGGED_URL`); browser requests to other sites are aborted by Playwright routes. It is started from the source copy, not its publish folder, like velixir starts it. The app log is printed when tests fail and kept in `artifacts/e2e/app.log`.
- End-to-end tests run serially (`[assembly: Parallelization(Mode = ParallelMode.None)]`) because they share one server's board, canvas and slot machine. `CollectionBehavior.DisableTestParallelization` is obsolete in xUnit v3 4.x.
- Coverage is reported (Cobertura for .NET and JavaScript) with no threshold.
- Test projects use the default analyzer set; the app uses the recommended set. Warnings are errors everywhere.

# 0015 Dependencies and versions

Status: accepted

- Runtime: none beyond the ASP.NET Core shared framework.
- Tests: `xunit.v3`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing`, `Microsoft.Testing.Extensions.CodeCoverage`, `Microsoft.Playwright`, `vitest`, `@vitest/coverage-v8`.
- NuGet versions are central in `Directory.Packages.props`; npm versions are exact with a lockfile.
- `global.json` pins `10.0.100` with `latestFeature`, so any 10.0 SDK works and the newest installed one is used. CI installs the latest `10.0.x`.
- Dependabot opens weekly grouped updates for NuGet, npm and GitHub Actions. The SDK is left out because bumping `global.json` would stop older 10.0 SDKs, such as distribution packages, from building.

# 0016 AGPL and REUSE

Status: accepted

- AGPL-3.0-or-later, full text in `LICENSE`.
- SPDX headers would be comments, which this project avoids. `REUSE.toml` declares `SPDX-License-Identifier` and `SPDX-FileCopyrightText` for every file instead, which REUSE-aware tools read.
- AGPL section 13: every page links to the source in the footer, and the About page explains the license.

# 0017 Scripts and CI

Status: accepted

- All logic is in `scripts/`. Each script uses `set -euo pipefail`, can be rerun, and cleans its own output in `artifacts/`.
- `run-all.sh` runs build, unit, JavaScript, end-to-end and export in that order, with `--skip-e2e`, `--skip-js` and `--skip-export`. Exit codes: 0 success, 2 usage, 127 missing tool, otherwise the failing step's code.
- Podman runs with `--security-opt label=disable` so the repository is not relabelled on SELinux hosts. The repository is mounted read-only and copied inside the container; NuGet packages are cached in a named volume.
- `ci.yml` installs .NET and Node, runs `run-all.sh` on every push, and uploads `artifacts/`. `velixir.yml` deploys on every push to `main`.

# 0018 Export dump rules

Status: accepted

- `scripts/export.sh` writes `docs/llm/dump.txt` from files git tracks or would track.
- Excluded: everything under `docs/llm/`, build and tool folders (`bin`, `obj`, `node_modules`, `artifacts`, `.velixir`, ...), binary files, `.env` files, `package-lock.json` (generated, third-party metadata) and `LICENSE` (third-party text).
- Included: all source, config, scripts, workflows and the rest of `docs/`, including these ADRs.
- Nothing is redacted. The repository holds no secrets; the deploy key is a GitHub secret.

FILE:     docs/adr/0019-slots.md
SIZE:     3.82 KB (3907 bytes)
LINES:    19
SHA256:   8551ff3a1e865728ce39827203bff685eb45cc5a6f5d2197c3c8e0f227062a38
MODIFIED: 2026-10-19 05:30:14 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0019 Shared slot machine

Status: accepted

- `/slots` hosts `SlotIsland`. Results are `{ id, text }` like board messages: UUIDv7 from the server clock, five symbols from `0-9A-Z`, newest 50 kept, same offer, merge and delta rules (`MessageWindow`).
- The server draws the result with `RandomNumberGenerator` when it accepts a press, then refuses every press until `SpinMilliseconds` (default 4,000) has passed. Busy presses are checked before the per-client bucket.
- `SlotDrawSymbols` (default all 36) limits the symbols drawn. Tests only: `7` makes every spin `77777`. Invalid values fail options validation; the service falls back to all symbols.
- The accepting delta and late-joiner snapshots carry `spin: { id, remaining }`. Clients animate for `remaining` ms from receipt, then add the result to the feed. Because receipt is later than the server clock, a client's button only re-enables after the server lock has expired.
- A spin that arrives while an older animation runs finishes the older one at once.
- Winners: three or more of one symbol anywhere, or five strictly ascending or descending in ASCII order (digits before letters). 1,201,320 of 36⁵ = 60,466,176, about 1 in 50.3. Ascending or descending runs of neighbours in `0-9A-Z` with no gaps (`ABCDE`, `789AB`, `43210`) are sequences, not straights: 32 each way, 64 total, 1 in 944,784. No wraparound (`YZ012` loses). Straights are the remaining 376,960 each way. Rules exist in `SlotRules.cs` and `SlotIsland.razor.js`; both are tested with the same cases.
- Winners window: newest `SlotWinnerCapacity` (default 500). Snapshots and deltas carry `winnersAdded` and `winnersRemoved` beside `added` and `removed`, under the same version. Clients hold back the spinning result from the winners list until its reels stop.
- Jackpots: four of a kind, five of a kind or a sequence, 6,400 results, about 1 in 9,448. Kept in a third list that never evicts. Drawn jackpots are always added. Offered jackpots are added only while the list holds fewer than `SlotJackpotCapacity` (default 10,000), so forged offers cannot grow it without bound. Snapshots carry every jackpot in `jackpotsAdded`; deltas carry new ones. There is no `jackpotsRemoved`.
- A jackpot that settles on screen adds `jackpot` to the machine for 8 s: verdict `Jackpot! <kind>`, cabinet glow, fast lights, CSS confetti (24 prerendered spans). Reduced motion keeps the glow and verdict only. Screen readers hear `Jackpot!`. Late joiners and instant finishes do not celebrate.
- After restarts, clients offer their newest 500 local winners through `OfferWinners` (winners and jackpots only) and up to 500 local jackpots through `OfferJackpots` (jackpots only), each with its own per-circuit budget. Chunks stay under 24 KB.
- Local storage: IndexedDB `chaos-slots` v2. `spins` is capped at 10,000 like the board. `jackpots` (added by migration 2) is never pruned. On each visit jackpots found in `spins` are copied into it, which also migrates v1 data.
- Every winner is a `<details>`. Its odds panel is built in the browser on first open from `lesson()` in `SlotIsland.razor.js`, the only place the probability text lives. Counts are formulas checked against an exhaustive search over a contiguous six-symbol alphabet (`89ABCD`), so sequences occur.
- No identity. `Press` returns the new id, `busy` or `slow` to the caller only. The browser marks its own results as `mine` in IndexedDB.
- Successful presses are counted in `localStorage` (`chaos-slots-presses`). At 10 the auto spin toggle appears; it presses 200–1,000 ms after each settle, keeps going while the tab is hidden (browsers may throttle its timers there), and is off on every page load.
- Reels are DOM strips moved with the Web Animations API by percentage of their own height, so no layout reads. Reduced motion swaps symbols without movement. Nothing uses inline style attributes, so the CSP is unchanged.

SHA256:   11e90e37991f0be3804a157273eb11d3dd276ea8dde3c68c5da0bae31a200a17

# 0020 Shared latency test

Status: accepted

- `/latency` hosts `LatencyIsland`. `LatencyCatalog` lists the endpoints: this site's `/time` (browser only), large public sites and 17 AWS DynamoDB `/ping` regions. All are anonymous GETs.
- `Begin` is refused (`busy`) while a test runs or within `LatencyGapSeconds` of the last start, then per client (`wait <seconds>`) for `LatencyCooldownSeconds`. A client is a network address (ADR 0008), so devices behind one NAT share the cooldown; the browser says so. Browser storage cannot enforce a limit, so `chaos-latency-next` is only a countdown hint. Accepted starts reply `ok <id> <cooldown>` and broadcast `running`.
- The server probes in the background (`HttpLatencyProbe`): one warm-up request, then the fastest of 3 time-to-headers, 6 endpoints at a time, 4 s per request, `LatencyBudgetSeconds` overall. `LatencyServerProbe=false` turns it off.
- The browser does the same with `fetch` (`no-cors`, `no-store`, no credentials, no referrer) and sends `Finish(id, ms[])`, -1 for failures. The server waits up to the budget plus 15 s, then publishes either way.
- A result is `{ id, text }` with text `key=browser,server;…` in integer ms, empty for none. Offers must re-serialize to the same text. Server keeps 50, browsers 10,000 (`chaos-latency`), same merge rules as slots.
- Ranking: a run's browser or server median counts only when at least half its endpoints answered on that side (`LatencyRun.Rank`, `rank()` in JS, tested with the same cases).
- Leaderboards (`LatencyLeaders`): jackpot winners (a counted median under 10 ms) and losers (100 ms or more), newest `LatencyJackpotCapacity` (default 50) each; and the `LatencyRankCapacity` (default 10) lowest and highest counted medians per side, ties to the older run. Every measured or offered run is a candidate.
- Snapshots carry `boards`: six ordered id lists (`fastJackpots`, `slowJackpots`, `browserFastest`, `browserSlowest`, `serverFastest`, `serverSlowest`) and `runs`, the texts the client has not seen. Deltas carry `boards` only when a list changes, with only new texts. Browsers save leader runs to local history.
- After a restart, browsers read every local run and offer their 10 best and 10 worst counted medians per side through `OfferLeaders` (40 per circuit, boards only, same validation as offers). Offers are trusted like slot jackpots; the caps bound forgeries.
- The page lists Results, then Leaderboard, then Jackpots.
- No identity: results hold only timings. The browser marks its own as `mine`. Test count (`chaos-latency-runs`) and next allowed time (`chaos-latency-next`) are in `localStorage`; 10 tests unlock auto run, which tests whenever allowed, including in hidden tabs.

FILE:     docs/adr/0021-server-clock.md
SIZE:     0.74 KB (759 bytes)
LINES:    7
SHA256:   cbca6ae4f1d54d85d6f2ccfdf3634e20be5f6a2599634e51ff4933d809f601ea
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0021 Server clock in the footer

Status: accepted

- `MainLayout` renders the server's local time, Unix ms and UTC offset into `<time id="server-clock" data-permanent>`. No circuit is needed.
- `App.razor.js` turns that into an offset from `performance.now()`, using the navigation timing window (`requestStart`–`responseStart`) as the error bound, and ticks once per second. The device's wall clock is never shown.
- It resyncs from `GET /time` (`{ unixMs, offsetMinutes }`, `no-store`) on load, every 10 minutes, when the tab becomes visible and when wall time and monotonic time drift apart by over 2 s (sleep). A sample replaces the current one if its error is smaller than the current error plus 100 ppm of its age; forced resyncs always replace it.

FILE:     docs/adr/0022-message-details.md
SIZE:     2.06 KB (2110 bytes)
LINES:    14
SHA256:   2e5fe0afd6aba40624f4ca0749d028b6b125c4b8ed3f1e280646ee5d8336501d
MODIFIED: 2026-10-31 02:28:55 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0022 Optional location and browser details on board messages

Status: accepted

- Opt-in. `#compose-share` appears only when `navigator.geolocation` exists. Ticking it calls `getCurrentPosition`; the browser prompt is the consent. Without permission nothing changes.
- Default state: on when the Permissions API reports `granted` and `localStorage` `chaos-board-share` is not `off`; off when denied. Unticking stores `off`. A permission change to `denied` unticks it. Clearing local history removes the key.
- On send, `BoardIsland` asks the module for `meta()` (15 s server timeout, `maximumAge` 60 s, 10 s position timeout). Failure sends the message without details.
- `collectMeta` reads only the browser: coordinates, accuracy, altitude, heading, speed, fix time, `Intl` time zone, locale, calendar, numbering system, UTC offset, local time, languages, user agent, client hints brands, platform, mobile, vendor, webdriver, Do Not Track, GPC, PDF viewer, screen, available screen, viewport, pixel ratio, color depth, orientation, threads, memory, touch points, pointer, color scheme, reduced motion, contrast, online, connection type, downlink, RTT, save data.
- No reverse geocoding, no third-party requests. The server adds nothing: no IP, no headers.
- Wire and storage: `BoardMessage.meta` is an ordered list of `{ key, value }` strings, omitted when null. `MessageMeta` keeps known keys only, in catalog order, first occurrence wins, strips control characters, trims, cuts to a per-key length and range-checks numeric keys. Without valid latitude and longitude the list is dropped. Offers carry `meta` and must already be normalized.
- Values are self-reported and unverified.
- Labels live in `MessageMeta.Fields` and `META_FIELDS`; tests pin the same key order in both.
- `Submit` returns the new message; the island calls `own(id)` so the browser marks it `mine` in IndexedDB and shows `Your message`. The server keeps no ownership. Older messages are unaffected.
- Rendering: collapsed `<details class="meta">` under the text, timestamp-sized and muted, both in the prerender and in the module.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

export DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1
export DOTNET_NOLOGO=1
export TESTINGPLATFORM_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1

port=5080
rigged=5081
work=/work
app=/tmp/app

mkdir -p "$work"
tar -C /src \
    --exclude=./.git --exclude=./artifacts \
    --exclude='*/bin' --exclude='*/obj' --exclude='*/node_modules' \
    -cf - . | tar -C "$work" -xf -
cd "$work"

dotnet publish src/Chaos.Web --configuration Release --output "$app"

start() {
    env \
        ASPNETCORE_URLS="http://127.0.0.1:$1" \
        ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Production \
        Chaos__BoardCapacity=5 \
        Chaos__PostBurst=1000 \
        Chaos__PostsPerSecond=1000 \
        Chaos__ResetBurst=1000 \
        Chaos__ResetsPerSecond=1000 \
        Chaos__PressBurst=1000 \
        Chaos__PressesPerSecond=1000 \
        Chaos__SpinMilliseconds=1500 \
        Chaos__LatencyServerProbe=false \
        Chaos__LatencyCooldownSeconds=1 \
        Chaos__LatencyGapSeconds=0 \
        Chaos__LatencyBudgetSeconds=5 \
        Chaos__MaxConnectionsPerClient=50 \
        Chaos__RequestBurst=100000 \
        Chaos__RequestsPerSecond=100000 \
        "${@:3}" \
        dotnet "$app/Chaos.Web.dll" >"/out/$2" 2>&1 &
}

wait_for() {
    for _ in $(seq 1 60); do
        if (exec 3<>"/dev/tcp/127.0.0.1/$1") 2>/dev/null; then
            return 0
        fi
        sleep 1
    done
    printf 'error: the app on port %s did not start\n' "$1" >&2
    cat "/out/$2" >&2
    return 1
}

start "$port" app.log
server=$!
start "$rigged" rigged.log Chaos__SlotDrawSymbols=7
rigged_server=$!
trap 'kill "$server" "$rigged_server" 2>/dev/null || true' EXIT

wait_for "$port" app.log
wait_for "$rigged" rigged.log

status=0
CHAOS_BASE_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$port/" \
CHAOS_RIGGED_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$rigged/" \
    dotnet test --project tests/Chaos.E2E --configuration Release --results-directory /out/results || status=$?

if [ "$status" -ne 0 ]; then
    printf '\n==> Last 50 lines of the app log\n' >&2
    tail -n 50 /out/app.log >&2
    printf '\n==> Last 50 lines of the rigged app log\n' >&2
    tail -n 50 /out/rigged.log >&2
fi

exit "$status"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"

PROJECT_PATH="${1:-$SCRIPT_DIR/..}"
OUTPUT_FILE="${2:-docs/llm/dump.txt}"

INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS="cs csproj slnx props targets json razor js mjs css html svg yml yaml sh md toml"
INCLUDE_NAMES=".gitignore .gitattributes .editorconfig"
EXCLUDE_NAMES="package-lock.json LICENSE"
EXCLUDE_DIRS="bin obj .vs .git node_modules .vscode .idea TestResults artifacts coverage .velixir"
EXCLUDE_PREFIXES="docs/llm/"

PROJECT_PATH="$(cd "$PROJECT_PATH" && pwd)"
cd "$PROJECT_PATH"

case "$OUTPUT_FILE" in
    /*) OUTPUT_PATH="$OUTPUT_FILE" ;;
    *)  OUTPUT_PATH="$PROJECT_PATH/$OUTPUT_FILE" ;;
esac
OUTPUT_DIR="$(dirname "$OUTPUT_PATH")"
mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"

OUTPUT_REL="${OUTPUT_PATH#"$PROJECT_PATH"/}"

if [ -t 1 ]; then
    GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[0;33m'; CYAN='\033[0;36m'; NC='\033[0m'
else
    GREEN=''; YELLOW=''; CYAN=''; NC=''
fi
log() { printf "%b%s%b\n" "$1" "$2" "$NC"; }

sha256_of() {
    if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
    elif command -v shasum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        shasum -a 256 "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
    else
        printf 'unavailable'
    fi
}

mod_time_utc_of() {
    local epoch
    if epoch="$(stat -c '%Y' "$1" 2>/dev/null)"; then
        date -u -d "@$epoch" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
    else
        epoch="$(stat -f '%m' "$1")"
        date -u -r "$epoch" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
    fi
}

line_count_of() {
    awk 'END { print NR }' "$1"
}

is_included_file() {
    local name="${1##*/}" ext entry
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_NAMES; do
        [ "$name" = "$entry" ] && return 1
    done
    case "$name" in
        .env|.env.*) return 1 ;;
    esac
    for entry in $INCLUDE_NAMES; do
        [ "$name" = "$entry" ] && return 0
    done
    ext="${name##*.}"
    [ "$ext" != "$name" ] || return 1
    ext="$(printf '%s' "$ext" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
    for entry in $INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS; do
        [ "$ext" = "$entry" ] && return 0
    done
    return 1
}

is_excluded_path() {
    local path="$1" entry
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_PREFIXES; do
        case "$path" in
            "$entry"*) return 0 ;;
        esac
    done
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_DIRS; do
        case "$path" in
            "$entry"/*|*/"$entry"/*) return 0 ;;
        esac
    done
    return 1
}

is_text() {
    [ ! -s "$1" ] || grep -Iq . "$1"
}

in_git_repo() { git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; }

render_tree() {
    local -a parts prev
    local rel n common i j indent
    prev=()
    echo "."
    while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
        IFS='/' read -r -d '' -a parts < <(printf '%s\0' "$rel") || true
        n=${#parts[@]}

        common=0
        while [ "$common" -lt $((n - 1)) ] && [ "$common" -lt "${#prev[@]}" ] \
              && [ "${parts[$common]}" = "${prev[$common]}" ]; do
            common=$((common + 1))
        done

        for ((i = common; i < n; i++)); do
            indent="    "
            for ((j = 0; j < i; j++)); do indent="$indent    "; done
            if [ "$i" -lt $((n - 1)) ]; then
                printf '%s%s/\n' "$indent" "${parts[$i]}"
            else
                printf '%s%s\n' "$indent" "${parts[$i]}"
            fi
        done

        if [ "$n" -gt 1 ]; then
            prev=("${parts[@]:0:$((n - 1))}")
        else
            prev=()
        fi
    done < "$FILE_LIST"
}

log "$GREEN" "Starting project export..."
log "$YELLOW" "Project Path: $PROJECT_PATH"
log "$YELLOW" "Output File:  $OUTPUT_PATH"

RAW_LIST="$(mktemp)"
FILE_LIST="$(mktemp)"
TMP_OUTPUT=""
cleanup() { rm -f "$RAW_LIST" "$FILE_LIST" ${TMP_OUTPUT:+"$TMP_OUTPUT"}; }
trap cleanup EXIT

if in_git_repo; then
    log "$CYAN" "Listing files via git..."
    git ls-files -z --cached --others --exclude-standard > "$RAW_LIST"
else
    log "$YELLOW" "Not a git repo; falling back to find..."
    find . -type f -print0 > "$RAW_LIST"
fi

while IFS= read -r -d '' file; do
    rel="${file#./}"
    [ "$rel" = "$OUTPUT_REL" ] && continue
    is_excluded_path "$rel" && continue
    is_included_file "$rel" || continue
    [ -f "$rel" ] || continue
    is_text "$rel" || continue
    printf '%s\0' "$rel"
done < "$RAW_LIST" | LC_ALL=C sort -z -u > "$FILE_LIST"

FILE_COUNT=0
while IFS= read -r -d '' _; do FILE_COUNT=$((FILE_COUNT + 1)); done < "$FILE_LIST"
log "$GREEN" "Found $FILE_COUNT files to export"

GIT_COMMIT="n/a"; GIT_BRANCH="n/a"; GIT_DIRTY="n/a"
if in_git_repo; then
    commit="$(git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -n "$commit" ] && GIT_COMMIT="$commit"
    branch="$(git branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -n "$branch" ] && GIT_BRANCH="$branch"

    status_args=(status --porcelain -- . ":(exclude)$OUTPUT_REL")
    if [ -n "$(git "${status_args[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)" ]; then
        GIT_DIRTY="yes (uncommitted changes are included below)"
    else
        GIT_DIRTY="no"
    fi
fi
DOTNET_VERSION="$(dotnet --version 2>/dev/null || echo 'not installed')"

TMP_OUTPUT="$(mktemp "$OUTPUT_DIR/.export.XXXXXX")"

{
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo "PROJECT EXPORT"
    echo "Generated (UTC): $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
    echo "Project Path:    $PROJECT_PATH"
    echo "Git Commit:      $GIT_COMMIT"
    echo "Git Branch:      $GIT_BRANCH"
    echo "Git Dirty:       $GIT_DIRTY"
    echo ".NET SDK:        $DOTNET_VERSION"
    echo "Files Exported:  $FILE_COUNT"
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo
    echo "DIRECTORY STRUCTURE (exported files only):"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo
    render_tree
    printf '\n\n'
    echo "FILE CONTENTS:"
    echo "=============="
    echo
} > "$TMP_OUTPUT"

TOTAL_BYTES=0
CURRENT=0
while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
    CURRENT=$((CURRENT + 1))
    full="$PROJECT_PATH/$rel"

    size="$(wc -c < "$full" | tr -d ' ')"
    lines="$(line_count_of "$full")"
    size_kb="$(awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.2f\", $size / 1024}")"
    hash="$(sha256_of "$full")"
    modified="$(mod_time_utc_of "$full")"
    TOTAL_BYTES=$((TOTAL_BYTES + size))

    log "$CYAN" "Processing ($CURRENT/$FILE_COUNT): $rel"

    {
        echo "================================================================================"
        echo "FILE:     $rel"
        echo "SIZE:     ${size_kb} KB (${size} bytes)"
        echo "LINES:    $lines"
        echo "SHA256:   $hash"
        echo "MODIFIED: $modified (UTC)"
        echo "================================================================================"
        echo
    } >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

    if [ -s "$full" ]; then
        cat "$full" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT" 2>/dev/null || echo "[ERROR READING FILE]" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
    else
        echo "[EMPTY FILE]" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
    fi

    printf '\n\n' >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
done < "$FILE_LIST"

TOTAL_MB="$(awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.2f\", $TOTAL_BYTES / 1048576}")"

{
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo "EXPORT COMPLETED (UTC): $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
    echo "Total Files Exported:   $FILE_COUNT"
    echo "Total Source Size:      ${TOTAL_MB} MB (${TOTAL_BYTES} bytes)"
    echo "Output File:            $OUTPUT_PATH"
    echo "==============================================================================="
} >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

DUMP_HASH="$(sha256_of "$TMP_OUTPUT")"
echo "DUMP SHA256 (of all lines above this one): $DUMP_HASH" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

chmod 0644 "$TMP_OUTPUT"
mv -f "$TMP_OUTPUT" "$OUTPUT_PATH"
TMP_OUTPUT=""

log "$GREEN" ""
log "$GREEN" "Export completed successfully!"
log "$YELLOW" "Output file: $OUTPUT_PATH"
log "$CYAN" "Total source size: ${TOTAL_MB} MB across ${FILE_COUNT} files"

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"

export DOTNET_NOLOGO=1
export TESTINGPLATFORM_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1

step() {
    printf '\n==> %s\n' "$*"
}

require() {
    local tool
    for tool in "$@"; do
        if ! command -v "$tool" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            printf 'error: %s is required but was not found on PATH\n' "$tool" >&2
            exit 127
        fi
    done
}

playwright_version() {
    local version
    version="$(sed -n 's/.*Include="Microsoft.Playwright" Version="\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$ROOT/Directory.Packages.props")"
    if [ -z "$version" ]; then
        printf 'error: no Microsoft.Playwright version in Directory.Packages.props\n' >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$version"
}

coverage_percent() {
    local rate
    rate="$(sed -n 's/.*<coverage[^>]* line-rate="\([0-9.]*\)".*/\1/p' "$1" | head -n 1)"
    if [ -n "$rate" ]; then
        awk -v rate="$rate" 'BEGIN { printf "%.1f%%\n", rate * 100 }'
    else
        printf 'unknown\n'
    fi
}

export const THEME_KEY = 'chaos-theme';
export const MAX_RETRIES = 30;

export function storedTheme(storage) {
    try {
        const value = storage?.getItem(THEME_KEY);
        return value === 'light' || value === 'dark' ? value : null;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

export function toggledTheme(current, prefersDark) {
    const effective = current ?? (prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
    return effective === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}

export function reconnectDelay(attempt, random = Math.random) {
    if (attempt <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    const ceiling = Math.min(30000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempt - 1));
    return Math.round(ceiling / 2 + random() * ceiling / 2);
}

export const CLOCK_SYNC_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
export const DRIFT_PER_MS = 0.0001;

export function zoneLabel(offsetMinutes) {
    if (!offsetMinutes) {
        return 'UTC';
    }
    const magnitude = Math.abs(offsetMinutes);
    const hours = String(Math.floor(magnitude / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
    const minutes = String(magnitude % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    return `UTC${offsetMinutes < 0 ? '-' : '+'}${hours}:${minutes}`;
}

export function clockLabel(serverMs, offsetMinutes) {
    const iso = new Date(serverMs + offsetMinutes * 60000).toISOString();
    return `${iso.slice(0, 10)} ${iso.slice(11, 19)} ${zoneLabel(offsetMinutes)}`;
}

export function sample(serverMs, start, end, offsetMinutes = 0) {
    const low = Math.min(start, end);
    const high = Math.max(start, end);
    return { offset: serverMs - (low + high) / 2, error: (high - low) / 2, at: high, zone: offsetMinutes };
}

export function uncertainty(current, now) {
    return current ? current.error + Math.max(0, now - current.at) * DRIFT_PER_MS : Infinity;
}

export function better(candidate, current, now) {
    return !current || candidate.error <= uncertainty(current, now);
}

export function untilNextSecond(serverMs) {
    return 1000 - (((Math.floor(serverMs) % 1000) + 1000) % 1000) + 5;
}

export class ServerClock {
    constructor(win) {
        this.win = win;
        this.doc = win.document;
        this.current = null;
        this.timer = 0;
        this.lastPerf = 0;
        this.lastWall = 0;
        this.syncing = null;
        this.syncedAt = -Infinity;
    }

    element() {
        return this.doc.getElementById('server-clock');
    }

    start() {
        const element = this.element();
        const now = Number(element?.dataset.now);
        if (Number.isFinite(now) && now > 0) {
            const navigation = this.win.performance.getEntriesByType?.('navigation')?.[0];
            const start = navigation?.requestStart || 0;
            const end = navigation?.responseStart || this.win.performance.now();
            this.current = sample(now, start, end, Number(element.dataset.offset) || 0);
        }
        this.doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (this.doc.visibilityState === 'visible') {
                this.sync(true);
            }
        });
        this.lastPerf = this.win.performance.now();
        this.lastWall = Date.now();
        this.tick();
        this.sync(false);
    }

    async sync(force) {
        const perf = this.win.performance;
        if (this.syncing || (!force && perf.now() - this.syncedAt < CLOCK_SYNC_MS && this.current)) {
            return;
        }
        this.syncing = (async () => {
            try {
                const start = perf.now();
                const response = await this.win.fetch(`/time?n=${Math.round(start)}`, { cache: 'no-store', credentials: 'omit' });
                const end = perf.now();
                if (!response.ok) {
                    return;
                }
                const body = await response.json();
                if (!Number.isFinite(body?.unixMs)) {
                    return;
                }
                const candidate = sample(body.unixMs, start, end, Number(body.offsetMinutes) || 0);
                if (force || better(candidate, this.current, end)) {
                    this.current = candidate;
                } else {
                    this.current = { ...this.current, zone: candidate.zone };
                }
                this.syncedAt = end;
                this.render();
            } catch {
            } finally {
                this.syncing = null;
            }
        })();
        await this.syncing;
    }

    now() {
        return this.current ? this.win.performance.now() + this.current.offset : null;
    }

    render() {
        const element = this.element();
        const now = this.now();
        if (!element || now === null) {
            return now;
        }
        element.textContent = clockLabel(now, this.current.zone);
        element.dateTime = new Date(now).toISOString();
        return now;
    }

    tick() {
        const perf = this.win.performance.now();
        const wall = Date.now();
        if (Math.abs((wall - this.lastWall) - (perf - this.lastPerf)) > 2000) {
            this.sync(true);
        }
        this.lastPerf = perf;
        this.lastWall = wall;
        if (perf - this.syncedAt >= CLOCK_SYNC_MS) {
            this.sync(false);
        }
        const now = this.render();
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.timer);
        this.timer = this.win.setTimeout(() => this.tick(), now === null ? 1000 : untilNextSecond(now));
    }
}

export function boot(win) {
    const doc = win.document;
    const darkQuery = win.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');

    const applyTheme = theme => {
        if (theme) {
            doc.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
        } else {
            delete doc.documentElement.dataset.theme;
        }
        const dark = theme ? theme === 'dark' : darkQuery.matches;
        for (const button of doc.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-toggle]')) {
            button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(dark));
        }
    };

    const applyStoredTheme = () => applyTheme(storedTheme(win.localStorage));

    const toggleTheme = () => {
        const next = toggledTheme(doc.documentElement.dataset.theme ?? null, darkQuery.matches);
        try {
            win.localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, next);
        } catch {
        }
        applyTheme(next);
    };

    const retryWhenVisible = () => {
        if (doc.visibilityState === 'visible') {
            retry();
        }
    };

    const retry = async () => {
        doc.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        try {
            if (await win.Blazor.reconnect()) {
                return;
            }
            if (await win.Blazor.resumeCircuit()) {
                return;
            }
            win.location.reload();
        } catch {
            doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        }
    };

    const resume = async () => {
        try {
            if (await win.Blazor.resumeCircuit()) {
                return;
            }
        } catch {
        }
        win.location.reload();
    };

    const clearHistory = async button => {
        const output = doc.getElementById(button.dataset.clearHistory);
        button.disabled = true;
        try {
            const [
                { DB_NAME: BOARD_DB, SHARE_KEY, deleteDatabase },
                { DB_NAME: SLOTS_DB, PRESS_KEY },
                { DB_NAME: CANVAS_DB },
                { DB_NAME: LATENCY_DB, RUNS_KEY, NEXT_KEY },
            ] = await Promise.all([
                import('./Islands/BoardIsland.razor.js'),
                import('./Islands/SlotIsland.razor.js'),
                import('./Islands/CanvasIsland.razor.js'),
                import('./Islands/LatencyIsland.razor.js'),
            ]);
            const outcomes = await Promise.all([BOARD_DB, SLOTS_DB, CANVAS_DB, LATENCY_DB].map(name => deleteDatabase(win.indexedDB, name)));
            for (const key of [SHARE_KEY, PRESS_KEY, RUNS_KEY, NEXT_KEY]) {
                try {
                    win.localStorage.removeItem(key);
                } catch {
                }
            }
            if (output) {
                output.textContent = outcomes.includes('blocked')
                    ? 'Close any other board, canvas, slots or latency tabs to finish clearing.'
                    : 'Local history cleared.';
            }
        } catch {
            if (output) {
                output.textContent = 'Could not clear the local history.';
            }
        } finally {
            button.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    const actions = {
        themeToggle: toggleTheme,
        reconnectRetry: retry,
        reconnectResume: resume,
        reconnectReload: () => win.location.reload(),
        clearHistory,
    };

    doc.addEventListener('click', event => {
        const button = event.target instanceof win.Element ? event.target.closest('button') : null;
        if (!button) {
            return;
        }
        for (const [name, action] of Object.entries(actions)) {
            if (name in button.dataset) {
                action(button);
                return;
            }
        }
    });

    doc.getElementById('components-reconnect-modal')?.addEventListener('components-reconnect-state-changed', event => {
        const state = event.detail?.state;
        if (state === 'rejected') {
            win.location.reload();
        } else if (state === 'hide') {
            win.dispatchEvent(new win.CustomEvent('chaos:reconnected'));
        } else if (state === 'failed') {
            doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        }
    });

    darkQuery.addEventListener('change', applyStoredTheme);
    applyStoredTheme();

    const clock = new ServerClock(win);
    clock.start();

    const afterNavigation = () => {
        applyStoredTheme();
        const announcer = doc.getElementById('announcer');
        if (announcer) {
            announcer.textContent = '';
        }
        clock.render();
    };

    return win.Blazor.start({
        circuit: {
            reconnectionOptions: {
                maxRetries: MAX_RETRIES,
                retryIntervalMilliseconds: attempt => reconnectDelay(attempt),
            },
        },
    }).then(() => win.Blazor.addEventListener('enhancedload', afterNavigation));
}

if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.Blazor) {
    boot(window);
}

@implements IAsyncDisposable
@inject BoardService Board
@inject IJSRuntime JS
@inject ILogger<BoardIsland> Logger

<form class="compose" @onsubmit="Send">
    <label class="visually-hidden" for="compose-text">Message</label>
    <textarea id="compose-text" @bind="_draft" maxlength="@MessageText.MaxLength" rows="3" required aria-describedby="compose-count"
              placeholder="Write something everyone here will see" disabled="@(!_ready)"></textarea>
    <div class="actions">
        <output class="status" for="compose-text">@_status</output>
        <span id="compose-count" class="count"></span>
        <span class="hint">Ctrl+Enter sends</span>
        <button type="submit" disabled="@(!_ready || _sending)">Send</button>
    </div>
    <div id="compose-share-box" class="share" hidden>
        <label><input type="checkbox" id="compose-share" /> Attach my location and browser details to my messages</label>
        <span id="compose-share-status" class="share-status" role="status"></span>
    </div>
</form>

@code {
    private static readonly TimeSpan MetaTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15);
    private readonly TaskCompletionSource _attached = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
    private DotNetObjectReference<BoardIsland>? _self;
    private IJSObjectReference? _module;
    private IJSObjectReference? _client;
    private Outbox<BoardUpdate>? _outbox;
    private Subscriber? _subscriber;
    private string? _draft;
    private string? _status;
    private bool _ready;
    private bool _sending;
    private bool _recount;
    private int _offered;

    [Parameter, EditorRequired]
    public string Client { get; set; } = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    protected override async Task OnAfterRenderAsync(bool firstRender)
    {
        if (!firstRender)
        {
            if (_recount && _client is not null)
            {
                _recount = false;
                await JsCleanup.InvokeAsync(_client, "recount");
            }

            return;
        }

        _self = DotNetObjectReference.Create(this);
        _module = await JS.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("import", ModulePath.Of(Assets["Components/Islands/BoardIsland.razor.js"]));
        _client = await _module.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("attach", _self);
        _attached.TrySetResult();
        _ready = true;
        StateHasChanged();
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public int Offer(string[] ids, string[] texts, MetaEntry[]?[]? metas)
    {
        if (ids is null || texts is null || ids.Length != texts.Length || (metas is not null && metas.Length != ids.Length))
        {
            return 0;
        }

        var count = Math.Min(BoardService.MaxOffers - _offered, ids.Length);
        if (count <= 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        _offered += count;
        var offered = new List<BoardMessage>(count);
        for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (Guid.TryParseExact(ids[i], "D", out var id) && texts[i] is { } text)
            {
                offered.Add(new BoardMessage(id, text, metas?[i]));
            }
        }

        return Board.Offer(offered);
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public async Task Join()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        var outbox = new Outbox<BoardUpdate>(64, 16, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20), SendAsync, Logger);
        _outbox = outbox;
        _subscriber = new Subscriber(outbox);
        Board.Subscribe(_subscriber);
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public bool Ping() => _subscriber is not null && _outbox is { IsFaulted: false } && Board.IsSubscribed(_subscriber);

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_client, "dispose");
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_module, null);
        _self?.Dispose();
    }

    private async Task Send()
    {
        if (_sending)
        {
            return;
        }

        _sending = true;
        try
        {
            var meta = await ReadMetaAsync();
            var reply = Board.Submit(_draft, Client, meta);
            _status = reply.Result switch
            {
                PostResult.Empty => "Type a message first.",
                PostResult.TooFast => "Slow down. You can post again in a moment.",
                PostResult.Repeat => "That message was just posted.",
                _ => null,
            };

            if (reply.Message is { } message)
            {
                _draft = null;
                _recount = true;
                await OwnAsync(message.Id);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            _sending = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task<MetaEntry[]?> ReadMetaAsync()
    {
        if (_client is null || MessageText.Normalize(_draft).Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        try
        {
            using var timeout = new CancellationTokenSource(MetaTimeout);
            return await _client.InvokeAsync<MetaEntry[]?>("meta", timeout.Token);
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
            return null;
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
            return null;
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private async Task OwnAsync(Guid id)
    {
        if (_client is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            await _client.InvokeVoidAsync("own", id.ToString("D"));
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
        }
    }

    private async Task SendAsync(IReadOnlyList<BoardUpdate> batch, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _attached.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
        await InvokeAsync(() => _client is null
            ? Task.FromException(new InvalidOperationException("The board module is not attached."))
            : _client.InvokeVoidAsync("apply", cancellationToken, batch).AsTask());
    }

    private async Task StopAsync()
    {
        if (_subscriber is not null)
        {
            Board.Unsubscribe(_subscriber);
            _subscriber = null;
        }

        if (_outbox is not null)
        {
            await _outbox.DisposeAsync();
            _outbox = null;
        }
    }

    private sealed class Subscriber(Outbox<BoardUpdate> outbox) : IBoardSubscriber
    {
        public bool Deliver(BoardUpdate update) => outbox.TryPost(update);
    }
}

export const DB_NAME = 'chaos-board';
export const DB_VERSION = 1;

export const OFFER_BYTES = 24000;

export const PING_MS = 25000;
export const SHARE_KEY = 'chaos-board-share';
