import { LocalHistory, OFFER_LIMIT, PAGE_SIZE, announce, chunkOffers, isoOf, labelOf, needsResync, newestFirst } from './BoardIsland.razor.js';

export const DB_NAME = 'chaos-latency';
export const DB_VERSION = 1;
export const STORE = 'runs';
export const LOCAL_CAP = 10000;
export const PING_MS = 25000;
export const RUNS_KEY = 'chaos-latency-runs';
export const NEXT_KEY = 'chaos-latency-next';
export const AUTO_UNLOCK = 10;
export const SAMPLES = 3;
export const PARALLEL = 6;
export const REQUEST_MS = 4000;
export const MAX_MS = 60000;
export const LEADER_OFFERS = 10;
export const FAST_BELOW = 10;
export const SLOW_FROM = 100;
export const SIDES = Object.freeze(['browser', 'server']);
export const SIDE_LABELS = Object.freeze({ browser: 'Browser', server: 'Server' });

export const BOARDS = Object.freeze([
    { key: 'fastJackpots', id: 'latency-jackpot-winners', jackpot: 'fast' },
    { key: 'slowJackpots', id: 'latency-jackpot-losers', jackpot: 'slow' },
    { key: 'browserFastest', id: 'latency-browser-fastest', side: 'browser' },
    { key: 'browserSlowest', id: 'latency-browser-slowest', side: 'browser' },
    { key: 'serverFastest', id: 'latency-server-fastest', side: 'server' },
    { key: 'serverSlowest', id: 'latency-server-slowest', side: 'server' },
]);

export const migrations = [
    db => db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: 'id' }),
];

export const SCHEMA = Object.freeze({ name: DB_NAME, version: DB_VERSION, store: STORE, migrations, cap: LOCAL_CAP });

const KEY = /^[a-z0-9-]{1,32}$/;
const VALUE = /^(0|[1-9][0-9]{0,4})?$/;

function value(text) {
    if (!VALUE.test(text)) {
        return undefined;
    }
    if (text === '') {
        return null;
    }
    const number = Number.parseInt(text, 10);
    return number <= MAX_MS ? number : undefined;
}

export function parseRun(text) {
    if (typeof text !== 'string' || text.length === 0 || text.length > 4096) {
        return null;
    }
    const entries = text.split(';');
    if (entries.length > 64) {
        return null;
    }
    const keys = new Set();
    const results = [];
    for (const entry of entries) {
        const equals = entry.indexOf('=');
        if (equals <= 0) {
            return null;
        }
        const key = entry.slice(0, equals);
        const values = entry.slice(equals + 1).split(',');
        if (!KEY.test(key) || keys.has(key) || values.length !== 2) {
            return null;
        }
        const browser = value(values[0]);
        const server = value(values[1]);
        if (browser === undefined || server === undefined) {
            return null;
        }
        keys.add(key);
        results.push({ key, browser, server });
    }
    return results;
}

export function median(values) {
    const sorted = values.filter(item => Number.isInteger(item)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (sorted.length === 0) {
        return null;
    }
    const middle = Math.floor(sorted.length / 2);
    return sorted.length % 2 === 1 ? sorted[middle] : Math.round((sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2);
}

export function summarize(results) {
    return {
        browser: median(results.map(result => result.browser)),
        server: median(results.map(result => result.server)),
        answered: results.filter(result => result.browser !== null || result.server !== null).length,
        total: results.length,
    };
}

export function rank(results) {
    const quorum = Math.ceil(results.length / 2);
    const counted = values => (values.filter(item => Number.isInteger(item)).length >= quorum ? median(values) : null);
    return {
        browser: counted(results.map(result => result.browser)),
        server: counted(results.map(result => result.server)),
    };
}

export function isFast(value) {
    return Number.isInteger(value) && value < FAST_BELOW;
}

export function isSlow(value) {
    return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= SLOW_FROM;
}

export function qualifies(value, jackpot) {
    return jackpot === 'fast' ? isFast(value) : isSlow(value);
}

function byId(a, b) {
    return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0;
}

export function leadersOf(records, limit = LEADER_OFFERS) {
    const scored = [];
    for (const record of records) {
        const results = typeof record?.id === 'string' ? parseRun(record.text) : null;
        if (results) {
            scored.push({ id: record.id, record, rank: rank(results) });
        }
    }
    const chosen = new Map();
    for (const side of SIDES) {
        const ranked = scored.filter(item => item.rank[side] !== null);
        const fastest = [...ranked].sort((a, b) => a.rank[side] - b.rank[side] || byId(a, b));
        const slowest = [...ranked].sort((a, b) => b.rank[side] - a.rank[side] || byId(a, b));
        for (const item of [...fastest.slice(0, limit), ...slowest.slice(0, limit)]) {
            chosen.set(item.id, item.record);
        }
    }
    return [...chosen.values()];
}

export function boardIds(boards) {
    return Object.fromEntries(BOARDS.map(board => [board.key, Array.isArray(boards?.[board.key]) ? boards[board.key] : []]));
}

export function readAll(db, store) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const request = db.transaction(store, 'readonly').objectStore(store).getAll();
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    });
}

export function ms(value) {
    return Number.isInteger(value) ? `${value} ms` : '–';
}

export function parseStartReply(reply) {
    if (reply === 'busy') {
        return { kind: 'busy' };
    }
    const parts = typeof reply === 'string' ? reply.split(' ') : [];
    if (parts[0] === 'wait' && parts.length === 2) {
        const seconds = Number.parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        return Number.isInteger(seconds) && seconds > 0 ? { kind: 'wait', seconds } : { kind: 'error' };
    }
    if (parts[0] === 'ok' && parts.length === 3 && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(parts[1])) {
        const seconds = Number.parseInt(parts[2], 10);
        return { kind: 'ok', id: parts[1], seconds: Number.isInteger(seconds) && seconds > 0 ? seconds : 300 };
    }
    return { kind: 'error' };
}

export function clockText(milliseconds) {
    const total = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(milliseconds / 1000));
    return `${Math.floor(total / 60)}:${String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function progressText(runs, unlock = AUTO_UNLOCK) {
    if (runs >= unlock) {
        return `${runs} tests from this browser.`;
    }
    const left = unlock - runs;
    return `${left} more ${left === 1 ? 'test unlocks' : 'tests unlock'} auto run.`;
}

export function readNumber(storage, key) {
    try {
        const parsed = Number.parseInt(storage?.getItem(key) ?? '0', 10);
        return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : 0;
    } catch {
        return 0;
    }
}

function writeNumber(storage, key, number) {
    try {
        storage?.setItem(key, String(number));
    } catch {
    }
}

export function fetchOptions(url, signal) {
    const options = { cache: 'no-store', credentials: 'omit', referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer', redirect: 'follow', signal };
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) {
        options.mode = 'no-cors';
    }
    return options;
}

export function cacheBusted(url, nonce) {
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) {
        return url;
    }
    return `${url}${url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'}n=${nonce}`;
}

export function defaults(win) {
    return {
        fetch: (url, options) => win.fetch(url, options),
        now: () => win.performance.now(),
        setTimeout: (callback, delay) => win.setTimeout(callback, delay),
        clearTimeout: handle => win.clearTimeout(handle),
        AbortController: win.AbortController,
    };
}

export async function timeOnce(url, deps, parent, nonce = 0) {
    const controller = new deps.AbortController();
    const onAbort = () => controller.abort();
    parent?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort);
    const timer = deps.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), REQUEST_MS);
    try {
        if (parent?.aborted) {
            return null;
        }
        const start = deps.now();
        await deps.fetch(cacheBusted(url, nonce), fetchOptions(url, controller.signal));
        return Math.max(0, Math.round(deps.now() - start));
    } catch {
        return null;
    } finally {
        deps.clearTimeout(timer);
        parent?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
    }
}

export async function measure(url, deps, parent, samples = SAMPLES) {
    let nonce = 0;
    if (await timeOnce(url, deps, parent, nonce++) === null) {
        return null;
    }
    let best = null;
    for (let index = 0; index < samples; index += 1) {
        const elapsed = await timeOnce(url, deps, parent, nonce++);
        if (elapsed === null) {
            break;
        }
        best = best === null ? elapsed : Math.min(best, elapsed);
    }
    return best === null ? null : Math.min(best, MAX_MS);
}

export async function measureAll(urls, deps, budgetMs, onResult = () => {}, parallel = PARALLEL) {
    const results = urls.map(() => null);
    const controller = new deps.AbortController();
    const deadline = deps.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), budgetMs);
    let next = 0;
    const worker = async () => {
        while (next < urls.length && !controller.signal.aborted) {
            const index = next;
            next += 1;
            const result = await measure(urls[index], deps, controller.signal);
            results[index] = controller.signal.aborted ? null : result;
            onResult(index, results[index]);
        }
    };
    try {
        await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(parallel, urls.length) }, worker));
    } finally {
        deps.clearTimeout(deadline);
    }
    return results;
}

class LatencyClient {
    constructor(panel, dotnet, doc, win) {
        this.panel = panel;
        this.dotnet = dotnet;
        this.doc = doc;
        this.win = win;
        this.history = new LocalHistory(win.indexedDB, SCHEMA);
        this.rows = [...panel.querySelectorAll('tbody tr')];
        this.endpoints = this.rows.map(row => ({
            key: row.dataset.key,
            url: row.dataset.url,
            label: row.dataset.label,
            server: row.dataset.server === 'true',
            browserCell: row.querySelector('.browser'),
            serverCell: row.querySelector('.server'),
        }));
        this.labels = new Map(this.endpoints.map(endpoint => [endpoint.key, endpoint.label]));
        this.runButton = panel.querySelector('.run');
        this.autoButton = panel.querySelector('.auto');
        this.status = panel.querySelector('.status');
        this.progress = panel.querySelector('.progress');
        this.feed = doc.getElementById('latency-feed');
        this.empty = doc.getElementById('latency-empty');
        this.older = doc.getElementById('latency-older');
        this.boardLists = BOARDS.map(board => ({
            ...board,
            list: doc.getElementById(board.id),
            empty: doc.getElementById(`${board.id}-empty`),
        }));
        this.cooldown = (Number.parseInt(panel.dataset.cooldown, 10) || 300) * 1000;
        this.budget = (Number.parseInt(panel.dataset.budget, 10) || 30) * 1000;
        this.runs = readNumber(win.localStorage, RUNS_KEY);
        this.next = readNumber(win.localStorage, NEXT_KEY);
        this.retryAt = 0;
        this.version = -1;
        this.live = new Map();
        this.shown = new Map();
        this.mine = new Set();
        this.leaders = new Map();
        this.boards = boardIds(null);
        this.drawn = new Map();
        this.cursor = null;
        this.ready = false;
        this.running = false;
        this.testing = false;
        this.waiting = null;
        this.auto = false;
        this.joining = null;
        this.timer = 0;
        this.ticker = 0;
        this.disposed = false;
        this.onRun = () => this.press(true);
        this.onAuto = () => this.toggleAuto();
        this.onOlder = () => this.loadOlder();
        this.onReconnected = () => this.join();
    }

    async start() {
        this.runButton?.addEventListener('click', this.onRun);
        this.autoButton?.addEventListener('click', this.onAuto);
        this.older?.addEventListener('click', this.onOlder);
        this.win.addEventListener('chaos:reconnected', this.onReconnected);
        this.timer = this.win.setInterval(() => this.ping(), PING_MS);
        this.ticker = this.win.setInterval(() => this.tick(), 1000);
        this.updateControls();
        try {
            await this.offer();
        } catch {
        }
        try {
            await this.offerLeaders();
        } catch {
        }
        await this.join();
    }

    async offer() {
        const local = await this.history.newest(OFFER_LIMIT * 2);
        for (const record of local) {
            if (record.mine) {
                this.mine.add(record.id);
            }
        }
        for (const chunk of chunkOffers(local)) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Offer', chunk.map(record => record.id), chunk.map(record => record.text));
        }
    }

    async offerLeaders() {
        const db = await this.history.database();
        if (!db || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        const records = await readAll(db, STORE);
        for (const record of records) {
            if (record.mine) {
                this.mine.add(record.id);
            }
        }
        for (const chunk of chunkOffers(leadersOf(records), undefined, LEADER_OFFERS * 4)) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('OfferLeaders', chunk.map(record => record.id), chunk.map(record => record.text));
        }
    }

    join() {
        if (this.disposed) {
            return Promise.resolve();
        }
        this.version = -1;
        this.joining ??= this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Join')
            .catch(() => {})
            .finally(() => {
                this.joining = null;
            });
        return this.joining;
    }

    async ping() {
        if (this.disposed || this.joining) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            if (!(await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Ping'))) {
                await this.join();
            }
        } catch {
        }
    }

    async apply(updates) {
        for (const update of updates) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            this.running = update.running === true;
            if (update.kind === 'snapshot') {
                await this.snapshot(update);
                await this.board(update.boards, true);
            } else if (update.kind === 'delta' && this.version >= 0) {
                if (needsResync(this.version, update)) {
                    this.join();
                    return;
                }
                await this.delta(update);
                if (update.boards) {
                    await this.board(update.boards, false);
                }
            }
            this.updateControls();
        }
    }

    record(message) {
        return { id: message.id, text: message.text, mine: this.mine.has(message.id) };
    }

    async save(messages) {
        try {
            await this.history.save(messages.map(message => this.record(message)));
        } catch {
        }
    }

    async snapshot(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        this.live = new Map(update.added.map(message => [message.id, message]));
        await this.save(update.added);
        let archive = { items: [], last: null, done: true };
        try {
            archive = await this.history.page(null, PAGE_SIZE, this.live);
        } catch {
        }
        this.cursor = archive.last;
        this.render([...update.added, ...archive.items].sort(newestFirst), archive.done);
        for (const message of update.added) {
            this.landed(message);
        }
        if (!this.ready) {
            this.ready = true;
            this.panel.dataset.ready = '';
        }
    }

    async delta(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        for (const id of update.removed) {
            this.live.delete(id);
            this.shown.get(id)?.classList.add('archived');
        }
        const fresh = [];
        for (const message of update.added) {
            this.live.set(message.id, message);
            const existing = this.shown.get(message.id);
            if (existing) {
                existing.classList.remove('archived');
            } else {
                this.insert(message, true);
                fresh.push(message);
            }
            this.landed(message);
        }
        this.updateEmpty();
        if (fresh.length > 0 && !this.disposed && this.feed?.isConnected) {
            const newest = [...fresh].sort(newestFirst)[0];
            const summary = summarize(parseRun(newest.text) ?? []);
            announce(this.doc, `New latency result: browser median ${ms(summary.browser)}, server median ${ms(summary.server)}.`);
        }
        await this.save(update.added);
    }

    async board(boards, reset) {
        if (reset) {
            this.leaders.clear();
            this.drawn.clear();
        }
        const runs = Array.isArray(boards?.runs) ? boards.runs : [];
        for (const message of runs) {
            this.leaders.set(message.id, message);
        }
        this.boards = boardIds(boards);
        const keep = new Set(Object.values(this.boards).flat());
        for (const id of [...this.leaders.keys()]) {
            if (!keep.has(id)) {
                this.leaders.delete(id);
            }
        }
        this.renderBoards();
        await this.save(runs);
    }

    renderBoards() {
        for (const board of this.boardLists) {
            if (!board.list) {
                continue;
            }
            const ids = this.boards[board.key].filter(id => this.leaders.has(id));
            const signature = ids.map(id => `${id}${this.mine.has(id) ? '*' : ''}`).join();
            if (this.drawn.get(board.key) === signature) {
                continue;
            }
            this.drawn.set(board.key, signature);
            const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
            ids.forEach((id, index) => fragment.append(this.leader(this.leaders.get(id), board, index + 1)));
            board.list.replaceChildren(fragment);
            if (board.empty) {
                board.empty.hidden = ids.length > 0;
            }
        }
    }

    leader(message, board, place) {
        const element = this.item(message, false);
        element.classList.remove('archived');
        element.classList.add('leader');
        const ranks = rank(parseRun(message.text) ?? []);
        const headline = this.element('p', 'headline');
        if (board.side) {
            headline.append(this.element('span', 'place', String(place)), this.element('strong', null, ms(ranks[board.side])));
        } else {
            element.classList.add(board.jackpot);
            for (const side of SIDES) {
                if (qualifies(ranks[side], board.jackpot)) {
                    headline.append(this.element('strong', null, `${SIDE_LABELS[side]} ${ms(ranks[side])}`));
                }
            }
        }
        element.prepend(headline);
        return element;
    }

    landed(message) {
        if (message.id !== this.waiting) {
            return;
        }
        this.waiting = null;
        const results = new Map((parseRun(message.text) ?? []).map(result => [result.key, result]));
        for (const endpoint of this.endpoints) {
            const result = results.get(endpoint.key);
            if (endpoint.serverCell) {
                endpoint.serverCell.textContent = ms(result?.server ?? null);
            }
        }
        this.setStatus('Done. Your result is at the top of the list.');
    }

    clearTable() {
        for (const endpoint of this.endpoints) {
            if (endpoint.browserCell) {
                endpoint.browserCell.textContent = '…';
            }
            if (endpoint.serverCell) {
                endpoint.serverCell.textContent = endpoint.server ? '…' : '–';
            }
        }
    }

    canPress(now = Date.now()) {
        return this.ready && !this.testing && !this.running && !this.disposed && now >= this.next;
    }

    async press(manual) {
        const now = Date.now();
        if (!this.ready || this.testing || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        if (now < this.next) {
            if (manual) {
                this.setStatus(`You can run the next test in ${clockText(this.next - now)}.`);
            }
            return;
        }
        if (this.running) {
            if (manual) {
                this.setStatus('Someone else is testing right now. Try again in a few seconds.');
            }
            return;
        }
        this.testing = true;
        this.updateControls();
        let reply = { kind: 'error' };
        try {
            reply = parseStartReply(await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Begin'));
        } catch {
        }
        if (this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        if (reply.kind === 'ok') {
            await this.test(reply);
        } else if (reply.kind === 'busy') {
            this.retryAt = Date.now() + 10000 + Math.round(Math.random() * 20000);
            this.setStatus(manual ? 'Someone else is testing right now. Try again in a few seconds.' : '');
        } else if (reply.kind === 'wait') {
            this.setNext(Date.now() + reply.seconds * 1000);
            this.setStatus(`A test ran from your network recently. Devices sharing an internet connection share this limit. The next one is possible in ${clockText(reply.seconds * 1000)}.`);
        } else {
            this.setStatus('Could not reach the server.');
        }
        this.testing = false;
        this.updateControls();
    }

    async test(reply) {
        this.mine.add(reply.id);
        this.waiting = reply.id;
        this.runs += 1;
        writeNumber(this.win.localStorage, RUNS_KEY, this.runs);
        this.setNext(Date.now() + reply.seconds * 1000);
        this.clearTable();
        let done = 0;
        this.setStatus(`Testing from your browser: 0 of ${this.endpoints.length}.`);
        const results = await measureAll(this.endpoints.map(endpoint => endpoint.url), defaults(this.win), this.budget, (index, result) => {
            done += 1;
            const cell = this.endpoints[index].browserCell;
            if (cell) {
                cell.textContent = ms(result);
            }
            this.setStatus(`Testing from your browser: ${done} of ${this.endpoints.length}.`);
        });
        if (this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        let accepted = false;
        try {
            accepted = await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Finish', reply.id, results.map(result => (result === null ? -1 : result)));
        } catch {
        }
        if (this.waiting === reply.id) {
            this.setStatus(accepted ? 'Your browser is done. Waiting for the server.' : 'The server stopped waiting for this browser. Its own numbers still count.');
        }
    }

    setNext(at) {
        this.next = at;
        writeNumber(this.win.localStorage, NEXT_KEY, at);
    }

    toggleAuto() {
        if (this.runs < AUTO_UNLOCK) {
            return;
        }
        this.auto = !this.auto;
        this.setStatus(this.auto ? 'Auto run is on. It runs a test whenever one is allowed.' : 'Auto run is off.');
        this.updateControls();
    }

    tick() {
        if (this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        const now = Date.now();
        if (this.auto && this.canPress(now) && now >= this.retryAt) {
            this.press(false);
            return;
        }
        this.updateControls();
    }

    updateControls() {
        const now = Date.now();
        if (this.runButton) {
            this.runButton.disabled = !this.ready || this.testing || this.running || now < this.next;
            this.runButton.textContent = this.testing ? 'Testing…' : now < this.next ? `Next test in ${clockText(this.next - now)}` : 'Run test';
        }
        if (this.autoButton) {
            this.autoButton.hidden = this.runs < AUTO_UNLOCK;
            this.autoButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(this.auto));
        }
        if (this.progress) {
            this.progress.textContent = this.running && !this.testing && !this.waiting
                ? 'Someone is running a test right now.'
                : progressText(this.runs);
        }
    }

    setStatus(text) {
        if (this.status) {
            this.status.textContent = text;
        }
    }

    async loadOlder() {
        if (!this.older) {
            return;
        }
        this.older.disabled = true;
        try {
            const archive = await this.history.page(this.cursor, PAGE_SIZE, this.live);
            this.cursor = archive.last ?? this.cursor;
            for (const record of archive.items) {
                if (record.mine) {
                    this.mine.add(record.id);
                }
                if (!this.shown.has(record.id)) {
                    this.insert(record, false);
                }
            }
            this.older.hidden = archive.done;
        } catch {
            this.older.hidden = true;
        } finally {
            this.older.disabled = false;
        }
    }

    element(tag, className, text) {
        const element = this.doc.createElement(tag);
        if (className) {
            element.className = className;
        }
        if (text !== undefined) {
            element.textContent = text;
        }
        return element;
    }

    item(message, fresh) {
        const results = parseRun(message.text) ?? [];
        const summary = summarize(results);
        const mine = this.mine.has(message.id) || message.mine === true;
        const element = this.element('li', 'msg');
        element.classList.toggle('mine', mine);
        element.classList.toggle('archived', !this.live.has(message.id));
        element.classList.toggle('fresh', fresh);
        element.dataset.id = message.id;
        const time = this.element('time', null, labelOf(message.id));
        time.dateTime = isoOf(message.id);
        const line = this.element('p', 'summary');
        for (const [label, value] of [['Browser median ', ms(summary.browser)], ['Server median ', ms(summary.server)]]) {
            const span = this.element('span', null, label);
            span.append(this.element('strong', null, value));
            line.append(span);
        }
        line.append(this.element('span', null, `${summary.answered} of ${summary.total} answered`));
        element.append(time, line);
        if (mine) {
            const tags = this.element('p', 'tags');
            tags.append(this.element('span', 'yours', 'Your test'));
            element.append(tags);
        }
        const details = this.element('details');
        details.append(this.element('summary', null, 'All endpoints'));
        const wrap = this.element('div', 'table-wrap');
        const table = this.element('table');
        const head = this.element('thead');
        const headRow = this.element('tr');
        for (const title of ['Endpoint', 'Browser', 'Server']) {
            const cell = this.element('th', null, title);
            cell.scope = 'col';
            headRow.append(cell);
        }
        head.append(headRow);
        const body = this.element('tbody');
        for (const result of results) {
            const row = this.element('tr');
            const name = this.element('th', null, this.labels.get(result.key) ?? result.key);
            name.scope = 'row';
            row.append(name, this.element('td', null, ms(result.browser)), this.element('td', null, ms(result.server)));
            body.append(row);
        }
        table.append(head, body);
        wrap.append(table);
        details.append(wrap);
        element.append(details);
        return element;
    }

    insert(message, fresh) {
        const element = this.item(message, fresh);
        let next = this.feed.firstElementChild;
        while (next && next.dataset.id > message.id) {
            next = next.nextElementSibling;
        }
        this.feed.insertBefore(element, next);
        this.shown.set(message.id, element);
    }

    render(messages, done) {
        this.shown.clear();
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const message of messages) {
            if (message.mine) {
                this.mine.add(message.id);
            }
            const element = this.item(message, false);
            this.shown.set(message.id, element);
            fragment.append(element);
        }
        this.feed.replaceChildren(fragment);
        if (this.older) {
            this.older.hidden = done;
        }
        this.updateEmpty();
    }

    updateEmpty() {
        if (this.empty) {
            this.empty.hidden = this.shown.size > 0;
        }
    }

    dispose() {
        this.disposed = true;
        this.win.clearInterval(this.timer);
        this.win.clearInterval(this.ticker);
        this.runButton?.removeEventListener('click', this.onRun);
        this.autoButton?.removeEventListener('click', this.onAuto);
        this.older?.removeEventListener('click', this.onOlder);
        this.win.removeEventListener('chaos:reconnected', this.onReconnected);
    }
}

export function attach(panel, dotnet) {
    const client = new LatencyClient(panel, dotnet, document, window);
    client.start();
    return {
        apply: updates => client.apply(updates),
        dispose: () => client.dispose(),
    };
}

@implements IAsyncDisposable
@inject SlotService Slots
@inject IJSRuntime JS
@inject ILogger<SlotIsland> Logger

<section @ref="_machine" class="machine" aria-labelledby="machine-title" data-initial="@_face" data-spin="@((int)Slots.SpinTime.TotalMilliseconds)">
    <h2 id="machine-title" class="visually-hidden">Slot machine</h2>
    <div class="lights" aria-hidden="true">
        @for (var i = 0; i < Bulbs; i++)
        {
            <span></span>
        }
    </div>
    <div class="window" aria-hidden="true">
        @foreach (var symbol in _face)
        {
            <div class="reel"><div class="strip"><span>@SlotRules.Above(symbol)</span><span>@symbol</span><span>@SlotRules.Below(symbol)</span></div></div>
        }
        <div class="payline"></div>
    </div>
    <p class="verdict" aria-hidden="true"></p>
    <div class="confetti" aria-hidden="true">
        @for (var i = 0; i < Confetti; i++)
        {
            <span></span>
        }
    </div>
    <div class="controls">
        <button type="button" class="spin" disabled>Spin</button>
        <button type="button" class="auto" aria-pressed="false" hidden>Auto spin</button>
    </div>
    <output class="status"></output>
    <p class="progress"></p>
</section>

@code {
    private const int Bulbs = 18;
    private const int Confetti = 24;

    private ElementReference _machine;
    private readonly TaskCompletionSource _attached = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
    private DotNetObjectReference<SlotIsland>? _self;
    private IJSObjectReference? _module;
    private IJSObjectReference? _client;
    private Outbox<SlotUpdate>? _outbox;
    private Subscriber? _subscriber;
    private string _face = "CHAOS";
    private int _offered;
    private int _offeredWinners;
    private int _offeredJackpots;

    [Parameter, EditorRequired]
    public string Client { get; set; } = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    [Parameter]
    public string? Face { get; set; }

    protected override void OnParametersSet() => _face = SlotRules.IsValid(Face) ? Face : "CHAOS";

    protected override async Task OnAfterRenderAsync(bool firstRender)
    {
        if (!firstRender)
        {
            return;
        }

        _self = DotNetObjectReference.Create(this);
        _module = await JS.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("import", ModulePath.Of(Assets["Components/Islands/SlotIsland.razor.js"]));
        _client = await _module.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("attach", _machine, _self);
        _attached.TrySetResult();
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public int Offer(string[] ids, string[] texts) => Slots.Offer(Parse(ids, texts, SlotService.MaxOffers, ref _offered));

    [JSInvokable]
    public int OfferWinners(string[] ids, string[] texts) => Slots.OfferWinners(Parse(ids, texts, SlotService.MaxWinnerOffers, ref _offeredWinners));

    [JSInvokable]
    public int OfferJackpots(string[] ids, string[] texts) => Slots.OfferJackpots(Parse(ids, texts, SlotService.MaxJackpotOffers, ref _offeredJackpots));

    [JSInvokable]
    public async Task Join()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        var outbox = new Outbox<SlotUpdate>(64, 16, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20), SendAsync, Logger);
        _outbox = outbox;
        _subscriber = new Subscriber(outbox);
        Slots.Subscribe(_subscriber);
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public string Press() => Slots.Press(Client).Reply;

    [JSInvokable]
    public bool Ping() => _subscriber is not null && _outbox is { IsFaulted: false } && Slots.IsSubscribed(_subscriber);

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_client, "dispose");
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_module, null);
        _self?.Dispose();
    }

    private static List<BoardMessage> Parse(string[]? ids, string[]? texts, int limit, ref int used)
    {
        if (ids is null || texts is null || ids.Length != texts.Length)
        {
            return [];
        }

        var count = Math.Min(limit - used, ids.Length);
        if (count <= 0)
        {
            return [];
        }

        used += count;
        var offered = new List<BoardMessage>(count);
        for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (Guid.TryParseExact(ids[i], "D", out var id) && texts[i] is { } text)
            {
                offered.Add(new BoardMessage(id, text));
            }
        }

        return offered;
    }

    private async Task SendAsync(IReadOnlyList<SlotUpdate> batch, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _attached.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
        await InvokeAsync(() => _client is null
            ? Task.FromException(new InvalidOperationException("The slot module is not attached."))
            : _client.InvokeVoidAsync("apply", cancellationToken, batch).AsTask());
    }

    private async Task StopAsync()
    {
        if (_subscriber is not null)
        {
            Slots.Unsubscribe(_subscriber);
            _subscriber = null;
        }

        if (_outbox is not null)
        {
            await _outbox.DisposeAsync();
            _outbox = null;
        }
    }

    private sealed class Subscriber(Outbox<SlotUpdate> outbox) : ISlotSubscriber
    {
        public bool Deliver(SlotUpdate update) => outbox.TryPost(update);
    }
}

    .spin:not(:disabled) {
        animation: beckon 2.4s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
}

import { LocalHistory, OFFER_LIMIT, PAGE_SIZE, announce, chunkOffers, isoOf, labelOf, needsResync, newestFirst } from './BoardIsland.razor.js';

export const DB_NAME = 'chaos-slots';
export const DB_VERSION = 2;
export const STORE = 'spins';
export const JACKPOTS = 'jackpots';
export const LOCAL_CAP = 10000;
export const PING_MS = 25000;
export const PRESS_KEY = 'chaos-slots-presses';
export const AUTO_UNLOCK = 10;
export const SYMBOLS = '0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
export const LENGTH = 5;
export const SYMBOL_MS = 55;
export const WINNER_OFFERS = 500;
export const JACKPOT_OFFERS = 500;
export const SPIN_MS = 4000;
export const CELEBRATE_MS = 8000;

export const migrations = [
    db => db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: 'id' }),
    db => db.createObjectStore(JACKPOTS, { keyPath: 'id' }),
];

export const SCHEMA = Object.freeze({ name: DB_NAME, version: DB_VERSION, store: STORE, migrations, cap: LOCAL_CAP });

export const LABELS = Object.freeze({
    three: 'Three of a kind',
    four: 'Four of a kind',
    five: 'Five of a kind',
    up: 'Straight up',
    down: 'Straight down',
    sequenceUp: 'Sequence up',
    sequenceDown: 'Sequence down',
});

export const SEQUENCE_KINDS = Object.freeze(['sequenceUp', 'sequenceDown']);
export const JACKPOT_KINDS = Object.freeze(['four', 'five', ...SEQUENCE_KINDS]);

export function isValid(text) {
    return typeof text === 'string' && /^[0-9A-Z]{5}$/.test(text);
}

function tally(text) {
    const counts = new Map();
    for (const symbol of text) {
        counts.set(symbol, (counts.get(symbol) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    return counts;
}

function ordered(text, direction) {
    for (let index = 1; index < text.length; index += 1) {
        if ((text.charCodeAt(index) - text.charCodeAt(index - 1)) * direction <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

function consecutive(text, direction) {
    for (let index = 1; index < text.length; index += 1) {
        if (SYMBOLS.indexOf(text[index]) - SYMBOLS.indexOf(text[index - 1]) !== direction) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

export function classify(text) {
    if (!isValid(text)) {
        return 'none';
    }
    const most = Math.max(...tally(text).values());
    if (most >= 5) {
        return 'five';
    }
    if (most === 4) {
        return 'four';
    }
    if (most === 3) {
        return 'three';
    }
    if (consecutive(text, 1)) {
        return 'sequenceUp';
    }
    if (consecutive(text, -1)) {
        return 'sequenceDown';
    }
    if (ordered(text, 1)) {
        return 'up';
    }
    return ordered(text, -1) ? 'down' : 'none';
}

export function isJackpot(text) {
    return JACKPOT_KINDS.includes(classify(text));
}

export function hits(text) {
    const win = classify(text);
    if (win === 'none') {
        return Array(LENGTH).fill(false);
    }
    if (win === 'up' || win === 'down' || SEQUENCE_KINDS.includes(win)) {
        return Array(LENGTH).fill(true);
    }
    const [top] = [...tally(text)].reduce((best, entry) => (entry[1] > best[1] ? entry : best));
    return [...text].map(symbol => symbol === top);
}

export function choose(n, k) {
    if (k < 0 || k > n) {
        return 0;
    }
    let result = 1;
    for (let index = 1; index <= k; index += 1) {
        result = (result * (n - k + index)) / index;
    }
    return Math.round(result);
}

export function waysOf(kind, symbols = SYMBOLS.length) {
    switch (kind) {
        case 'five':
            return symbols;
        case 'four':
            return symbols * choose(LENGTH, 4) * (symbols - 1);
        case 'three':
            return symbols * choose(LENGTH, 3) * (symbols - 1) ** 2;
        case 'sequenceUp':
        case 'sequenceDown':
            return Math.max(0, symbols - LENGTH + 1);
        case 'up':
        case 'down':
            return choose(symbols, LENGTH) - waysOf('sequenceUp', symbols);
        default:
            return 0;
    }
}

export const OUTCOMES = SYMBOLS.length ** LENGTH;
export const WINNING = Object.keys(LABELS).reduce((sum, kind) => sum + waysOf(kind), 0);
export const JACKPOT_WAYS = JACKPOT_KINDS.reduce((sum, kind) => sum + waysOf(kind), 0);
export const SEQUENCES = SEQUENCE_KINDS.reduce((sum, kind) => sum + waysOf(kind), 0);
export const FULL_HOUSE = SYMBOLS.length * choose(LENGTH, 3) * (SYMBOLS.length - 1);
export const ALL_DIFFERENT = Array.from({ length: LENGTH }, (_, index) => SYMBOLS.length - index).reduce((product, value) => product * value, 1);

const NUMBER = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US');

export function grouped(value) {
    return NUMBER.format(value);
}

export function oneIn(ways, outcomes = OUTCOMES) {
    const ratio = outcomes / ways;
    return ratio < 100 ? NUMBER.format(Math.round(ratio * 10) / 10) : NUMBER.format(Math.round(ratio));
}

export function percent(fraction) {
    return `${Number((fraction * 100).toPrecision(3))}%`;
}

export function medianWait(chance) {
    return Math.ceil(Math.log(0.5) / Math.log1p(-chance));
}

export function headsInARow(chance) {
    return Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.log2(1 / chance)));
}

export function isFullHouse(text) {
    return classify(text) === 'three' && tally(text).size === 2;
}

const UNITS = [
    ['year', 31_557_600_000],
    ['day', 86_400_000],
    ['hour', 3_600_000],
    ['minute', 60_000],
    ['second', 1000],
];

export function duration(ms) {
    const [name, size] = UNITS.find(([, length]) => ms >= length) ?? UNITS.at(-1);
    const value = ms / size;
    const shown = value >= 10 ? Math.round(value) : Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
    return `${NUMBER.format(shown)} ${name}${shown === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
}

function counting(kind) {
    const n = SYMBOLS.length;
    switch (kind) {
        case 'five':
            return `The first reel can be anything. Each of the other four must match it, 1 chance in ${n} each: ${n} × ${n} × ${n} × ${n} = ${grouped(n ** 4)}, so 1 in ${grouped(n ** 4)}.`;
        case 'four':
            return `${n} symbols × ${choose(LENGTH, 4)} places for the odd reel × ${n - 1} choices for it = ${grouped(waysOf('four'))}.`;
        case 'three':
            return `${n} symbols × ${choose(LENGTH, 3)} ways to pick its three reels × ${n - 1} × ${n - 1} for the other two = ${grouped(waysOf('three'))}.`;
        case 'sequenceUp':
            return `A rising sequence is fixed by its first symbol, which can be 0 to ${SYMBOLS[n - LENGTH]}: ${grouped(waysOf(kind))} ways, ${SYMBOLS.slice(0, LENGTH)} to ${SYMBOLS.slice(-LENGTH)}. It does not wrap from Z to 0.`;
        case 'sequenceDown':
            return `A falling sequence is fixed by its first symbol, which can be Z down to ${SYMBOLS[LENGTH - 1]}: ${grouped(waysOf(kind))} ways, ${[...SYMBOLS.slice(-LENGTH)].reverse().join('')} to ${[...SYMBOLS.slice(0, LENGTH)].reverse().join('')}. It does not wrap from 0 to Z.`;
        default:
            return `Pick any ${LENGTH} different symbols: ${n} choose ${LENGTH} = ${grouped(choose(n, LENGTH))} ways. Each set ${kind === 'up' ? 'climbs in exactly one order, 0 to 9 then A to Z' : 'falls in exactly one order, Z to A then 9 to 0'}. The ${grouped(waysOf('sequenceUp'))} sets with no gaps are sequences, which are jackpots, leaving ${grouped(waysOf(kind))}.`;
    }
}

function idea(kind) {
    switch (kind) {
        case 'five':
            return 'Multiplication rule: the chance that independent things all happen is the product of their chances. Four matches at 1 in 36 each is (1/36)⁴.';
        case 'four':
            return `Counting: when every result is equally likely, chance = ways to get it ÷ all results = ${grouped(waysOf('four'))} ÷ ${grouped(OUTCOMES)}.`;
        case 'three':
            return `Combinations: "5 choose 3" counts ways to pick 3 reels of 5 when order does not matter: (5 × 4 × 3) ÷ (3 × 2 × 1) = 10. Some repeat is common: ${percent(1 - ALL_DIFFERENT / OUTCOMES)} of spins have at least two matching symbols, because five reels form 10 pairs. That is the birthday problem.`;
        case 'sequenceUp':
        case 'sequenceDown':
            return `A sequence is a straight with no gaps. Of the ${grouped(choose(SYMBOLS.length, LENGTH))} sets of ${LENGTH} different symbols, only ${grouped(waysOf(kind))} have no gaps, so either direction comes up 1 in ${oneIn(SEQUENCES)}, rarer than five of a kind (1 in ${oneIn(waysOf('five'))}).`;
        default:
            return `Order versus choice: ${LENGTH} different symbols can be lined up 5! = 120 ways, and only one climbs and one falls. Even so, straights (${grouped(waysOf('up') * 2)} ways both directions) are more common than three or more of a kind (${grouped(waysOf('three') + waysOf('four') + waysOf('five'))}).`;
    }
}

export function lesson(text, spinMs = SPIN_MS) {
    const kind = classify(text);
    if (kind === 'none') {
        return null;
    }
    const ways = waysOf(kind);
    const chance = ways / OUTCOMES;
    const mean = OUTCOMES / ways;
    const heads = headsInARow(chance);
    const facts = [
        ['Ways to get it', `${grouped(ways)} of ${grouped(OUTCOMES)}`],
        ['Chance per spin', percent(chance)],
        ['Average wait', `${oneIn(ways)} spins, about ${duration(mean * spinMs)} spinning nonstop, one spin every ${duration(spinMs)}`],
        ['Half the time within', `${grouped(medianWait(chance))} spins`],
        ['About as likely as', `${heads} coin ${heads === 1 ? 'flip' : 'flips'} all heads (1 in ${grouped(2 ** heads)})`],
        ['Share of all wins', percent(ways / WINNING)],
    ];
    if (SEQUENCE_KINDS.includes(kind)) {
        facts.push(['Either direction', `${grouped(SEQUENCES)} ways, 1 in ${oneIn(SEQUENCES)}`]);
    }
    if (isJackpot(text)) {
        facts.push(['Any jackpot', `${grouped(JACKPOT_WAYS)} ways, 1 in ${oneIn(JACKPOT_WAYS)}`]);
    }
    if (isFullHouse(text)) {
        facts.push(['Three with a pair', `${grouped(FULL_HOUSE)} ways, 1 in ${oneIn(FULL_HOUSE)}`]);
    }
    return {
        kind,
        headline: `1 in ${oneIn(ways)}`,
        summary: `${LABELS[kind]} comes up about once every ${oneIn(ways)} spins.`,
        facts,
        sections: [
            ['How it is counted', counting(kind)],
            ['The idea', idea(kind)],
            ['This exact result', `${text} had a 1 in ${grouped(OUTCOMES)} chance, the same as every other result. What makes a kind rare is how few results belong to it.`],
            ['Nothing is ever due', 'Spins are independent, so a dry spell does not make a win more likely. Waits follow a geometric distribution: mostly short, sometimes very long, which is why half of them end well before the average.'],
            ['Any win', `${grouped(WINNING)} of ${grouped(OUTCOMES)} results win, about 1 in ${oneIn(WINNING)}.`],
        ],
    };
}

export function newestWinners(records, limit = WINNER_OFFERS) {
    return records
        .filter(record => isValid(record?.text) && classify(record.text) !== 'none')
        .sort(newestFirst)
        .slice(0, limit);
}

export function verdictText(text) {
    const kind = classify(text);
    if (kind === 'none') {
        return '';
    }
    return isJackpot(text) ? `Jackpot! ${LABELS[kind]}` : LABELS[kind];
}

export function newestJackpots(records, limit = JACKPOT_OFFERS) {
    return records
        .filter(record => isJackpot(record?.text))
        .sort(newestFirst)
        .slice(0, limit);
}

export function readAll(db, store) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const request = db.transaction(store, 'readonly').objectStore(store).getAll();
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    });
}

export function putAll(db, store, records) {
    if (records.length === 0) {
        return Promise.resolve();
    }
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const transaction = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');
        const objects = transaction.objectStore(store);
        for (const record of records) {
            objects.put(record);
        }
        transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
        transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
        transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error);
    });
}

export function above(symbol) {
    return SYMBOLS[(SYMBOLS.indexOf(symbol) + 1) % SYMBOLS.length];
}

export function below(symbol) {
    return SYMBOLS[(SYMBOLS.indexOf(symbol) + SYMBOLS.length - 1) % SYMBOLS.length];
}

export function stopTimes(duration, reels = LENGTH) {
    return Array.from({ length: reels }, (_, index) => Math.round(duration * (0.42 + 0.11 * index)));
}

export function stripFor(current, target, spins, random = Math.random) {
    const filler = Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, spins) }, () => SYMBOLS[Math.floor(random() * SYMBOLS.length)]);
    return [above(target), target, below(target), ...filler, above(current), current, below(current)];
}

export function spinFrames(cells) {
    const step = 100 / cells;
    const start = -(cells - 3) * step;
    return [
        { transform: `translateY(${start}%)`, easing: 'ease-in' },
        { transform: `translateY(${start - step * 0.35}%)`, offset: 0.07, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.32,0,.18,1)' },
        { transform: `translateY(${step * 0.3}%)`, offset: 0.92, easing: 'ease-out' },
        { transform: 'translateY(0%)' },
    ];
}

export function readPresses(storage) {
    try {
        const value = Number.parseInt(storage?.getItem(PRESS_KEY) ?? '0', 10);
        return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 ? value : 0;
    } catch {
        return 0;
    }
}

export function autoDelay(random = Math.random) {
    return 200 + Math.round(random() * 800);
}

export function parseReply(reply) {
    if (reply === 'busy' || reply === 'slow') {
        return { kind: reply, id: null };
    }
    if (typeof reply === 'string' && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(reply)) {
        return { kind: 'accepted', id: reply };
    }
    return { kind: 'error', id: null };
}

export function progressText(presses, unlock = AUTO_UNLOCK) {
    if (presses >= unlock) {
        return `${presses} successful presses from this browser.`;
    }
    const left = unlock - presses;
    return `${left} more successful ${left === 1 ? 'press unlocks' : 'presses unlock'} auto spin.`;
}

class SlotClient {
    constructor(machine, dotnet, doc, win) {
        this.machine = machine;
        this.dotnet = dotnet;
        this.doc = doc;
        this.win = win;
        this.history = new LocalHistory(win.indexedDB, SCHEMA);
        this.reels = [...machine.querySelectorAll('.reel')];
        this.strips = this.reels.map(reel => reel.querySelector('.strip'));
        this.spinButton = machine.querySelector('.spin');
        this.autoButton = machine.querySelector('.auto');
        this.status = machine.querySelector('.status');
        this.verdict = machine.querySelector('.verdict');
        this.progress = machine.querySelector('.progress');
        this.feed = doc.getElementById('slots-feed');
        this.empty = doc.getElementById('slots-empty');
        this.older = doc.getElementById('slots-older');
        this.winnersList = doc.getElementById('slots-winners');
        this.winnersEmpty = doc.getElementById('slots-winners-empty');
        this.jackpotsList = doc.getElementById('slots-jackpots');
        this.jackpotsEmpty = doc.getElementById('slots-jackpots-empty');
        this.spinMs = Number.parseInt(machine.dataset.spin ?? '', 10) || SPIN_MS;
        this.faces = [...(isValid(machine.dataset.initial) ? machine.dataset.initial : 'CHAOS')];
        this.motion = win.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
        this.presses = readPresses(win.localStorage);
        this.version = -1;
        this.live = new Map();
        this.shown = new Map();
        this.winners = new Map();
        this.crowned = new Map();
        this.jackpots = new Map();
        this.enshrined = new Map();
        this.mine = new Set();
        this.cursor = null;
        this.spin = null;
        this.ready = false;
        this.pressing = false;
        this.auto = false;
        this.autoTimer = 0;
        this.party = 0;
        this.joining = null;
        this.timer = 0;
        this.disposed = false;
        this.onSpin = () => this.press(true);
        this.onAuto = () => this.toggleAuto();
        this.onOlder = () => this.loadOlder();
        this.onReconnected = () => this.join();
        this.onToggle = event => this.explain(event.target);
    }

    async start() {
        this.spinButton?.addEventListener('click', this.onSpin);
        this.autoButton?.addEventListener('click', this.onAuto);
        this.older?.addEventListener('click', this.onOlder);
        this.win.addEventListener('chaos:reconnected', this.onReconnected);
        this.doc.addEventListener('toggle', this.onToggle, true);
        this.timer = this.win.setInterval(() => this.ping(), PING_MS);
        this.updateControls();
        try {
            await this.offer();
        } catch {
        }
        try {
            await this.offerWinners();
        } catch {
        }
        try {
            await this.offerJackpots();
        } catch {
        }
        await this.join();
    }

    async offer() {
        const local = await this.history.newest(OFFER_LIMIT * 2);
        for (const record of local) {
            if (record.mine) {
                this.mine.add(record.id);
            }
        }
        for (const chunk of chunkOffers(local)) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Offer', chunk.map(record => record.id), chunk.map(record => record.text));
        }
    }

    async offerWinners() {
        const db = await this.history.database();
        if (!db || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        const records = await readAll(db, STORE);
        await putAll(db, JACKPOTS, newestJackpots(records, Infinity));
        const winners = newestWinners(records);
        for (const record of winners) {
            if (record.mine) {
                this.mine.add(record.id);
            }
        }
        await this.send('OfferWinners', winners, WINNER_OFFERS);
    }

    async offerJackpots() {
        const db = await this.history.database();
        if (!db || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        const jackpots = newestJackpots(await readAll(db, JACKPOTS), Infinity);
        for (const record of jackpots) {
            if (record.mine) {
                this.mine.add(record.id);
            }
            this.jackpots.set(record.id, record);
        }
        await this.send('OfferJackpots', jackpots, JACKPOT_OFFERS);
    }

    async send(method, records, limit) {
        for (const chunk of chunkOffers(records, undefined, limit)) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync(method, chunk.map(record => record.id), chunk.map(record => record.text));
        }
    }

    join() {
        if (this.disposed) {
            return Promise.resolve();
        }
        this.version = -1;
        this.joining ??= this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Join')
            .catch(() => {})
            .finally(() => {
                this.joining = null;
            });
        return this.joining;
    }

    async ping() {
        if (this.disposed || this.joining) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            if (!(await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Ping'))) {
                await this.join();
            }
        } catch {
        }
    }

    async apply(updates) {
        for (const update of updates) {
            if (this.disposed) {
                return;
            }
            if (update.kind === 'snapshot') {
                await this.snapshot(update);
            } else if (update.kind === 'delta' && this.version >= 0) {
                if (needsResync(this.version, update)) {
                    this.join();
                    return;
                }
                await this.delta(update);
            }
        }
    }

    record(message) {
        return { id: message.id, text: message.text, mine: this.mine.has(message.id) };
    }

    async save(messages) {
        try {
            await this.history.save(messages.map(message => this.record(message)));
        } catch {
        }
        await this.keep(messages);
    }

    async keep(messages) {
        const jackpots = messages.filter(message => isJackpot(message.text)).map(message => this.record(message));
        if (jackpots.length === 0) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            const db = await this.history.database();
            if (db) {
                await putAll(db, JACKPOTS, jackpots);
            }
        } catch {
        }
    }

    async snapshot(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        this.live = new Map(update.added.map(message => [message.id, message]));
        this.winners = new Map((update.winnersAdded ?? []).map(message => [message.id, message]));
        for (const message of update.jackpotsAdded ?? []) {
            this.jackpots.set(message.id, message);
        }
        const spinning = update.spin && this.live.has(update.spin.id) ? update.spin : null;
        if (this.spin && this.spin.message.id !== spinning?.id) {
            this.finish(true);
        }
        const settled = update.added.filter(message => message.id !== spinning?.id);
        await this.save(settled);
        await this.keep((update.jackpotsAdded ?? []).filter(message => message.id !== spinning?.id));
        let archive = { items: [], last: null, done: true };
        try {
            archive = await this.history.page(null, PAGE_SIZE, this.live);
        } catch {
        }
        this.cursor = archive.last;
        this.render([...settled, ...archive.items].sort(newestFirst), archive.done);
        this.renderWinners(spinning?.id ?? this.spin?.message.id);
        this.renderJackpots(spinning?.id ?? this.spin?.message.id);
        if (spinning && !this.spin) {
            this.begin(this.live.get(spinning.id), spinning.remaining);
        } else if (!this.spin && settled.length > 0) {
            this.show(settled.sort(newestFirst)[0].text);
        }
        if (!this.ready) {
            this.ready = true;
            this.machine.dataset.ready = '';
        }
        this.updateControls();
        this.scheduleAuto();
    }

    async delta(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        for (const id of update.removed) {
            this.live.delete(id);
            this.shown.get(id)?.classList.add('archived');
        }
        const settled = [];
        let spinning = null;
        for (const message of update.added) {
            this.live.set(message.id, message);
            if (update.spin && message.id === update.spin.id) {
                spinning = message;
                continue;
            }
            const existing = this.shown.get(message.id);
            if (existing) {
                existing.classList.remove('archived');
            } else {
                this.insert(message, true);
            }
            settled.push(message);
        }
        for (const id of update.winnersRemoved ?? []) {
            this.winners.delete(id);
            this.crowned.get(id)?.remove();
            this.crowned.delete(id);
        }
        for (const message of update.winnersAdded ?? []) {
            this.winners.set(message.id, message);
            if (message.id !== update.spin?.id && message.id !== this.spin?.message.id) {
                this.crown(message, true);
            }
        }
        const kept = [];
        for (const message of update.jackpotsAdded ?? []) {
            this.jackpots.set(message.id, message);
            if (message.id !== update.spin?.id && message.id !== this.spin?.message.id) {
                this.enshrine(message, true);
                kept.push(message);
            }
        }
        this.updateEmpty();
        if (spinning) {
            this.begin(spinning, update.spin.remaining);
        }
        await this.save(settled);
        await this.keep(kept);
    }

    reducedMotion() {
        return this.motion.matches || typeof this.strips[0]?.animate !== 'function';
    }

    begin(message, remaining) {
        if (this.spin) {
            this.finish(true);
        }
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.autoTimer);
        this.autoTimer = 0;
        const duration = Math.max(0, Math.round(remaining));
        const spin = { message, timers: [], animations: [] };
        this.spin = spin;
        this.calm();
        this.machine.classList.remove('win');
        this.machine.classList.add('spinning');
        this.verdict.textContent = '';
        for (const reel of this.reels) {
            reel.classList.remove('hit', 'stopped');
        }
        const stops = stopTimes(duration);
        const reduced = this.reducedMotion();
        stops.forEach((stop, index) => {
            const target = message.text[index];
            if (!reduced && stop > 0) {
                this.roll(spin, index, target, stop);
            }
            spin.timers.push(this.win.setTimeout(() => this.stop(spin, index, target), stop));
        });
        spin.timers.push(this.win.setTimeout(() => this.finish(false), duration));
        this.updateControls();
    }

    roll(spin, index, target, time) {
        const strip = this.strips[index];
        const reel = this.reels[index];
        const symbols = stripFor(this.faces[index], target, Math.max(4, Math.round(time / SYMBOL_MS)));
        this.fill(strip, symbols);
        spin.animations.push(strip.animate(spinFrames(symbols.length), { duration: time, fill: 'forwards' }));
        reel.classList.add('blur');
        spin.timers.push(this.win.setTimeout(() => reel.classList.remove('blur'), Math.round(time * 0.72)));
    }

    stop(spin, index, target) {
        if (this.spin !== spin) {
            return;
        }
        const reel = this.reels[index];
        reel.classList.remove('blur', 'stopped');
        this.setFace(index, target);
        for (const animation of spin.animations) {
            if (animation.effect?.target === this.strips[index]) {
                animation.cancel();
            }
        }
        void reel.offsetWidth;
        reel.classList.add('stopped');
    }

    finish(instant) {
        const spin = this.spin;
        if (!spin) {
            return;
        }
        this.spin = null;
        for (const timer of spin.timers) {
            this.win.clearTimeout(timer);
        }
        for (const animation of spin.animations) {
            animation.cancel();
        }
        const text = spin.message.text;
        this.show(text);
        this.machine.classList.remove('spinning');
        const win = classify(text);
        const marks = hits(text);
        this.reels.forEach((reel, index) => reel.classList.toggle('hit', marks[index]));
        this.machine.classList.toggle('win', win !== 'none');
        this.verdict.textContent = verdictText(text);
        this.land(spin.message, !instant);
        if (!instant && isJackpot(text)) {
            this.celebrate();
        }
        this.updateControls();
        this.scheduleAuto();
    }

    land(message, fresh) {
        if (!this.shown.has(message.id)) {
            this.insert(message, fresh);
        }
        if (this.winners.has(message.id)) {
            this.crown(message, fresh);
        }
        if (isJackpot(message.text)) {
            this.jackpots.set(message.id, message);
            this.enshrine(message, fresh);
        }
        this.updateEmpty();
        this.announce(message);
        this.save([message]);
    }

    celebrate() {
        this.calm();
        this.machine.classList.add('jackpot');
        this.party = this.win.setTimeout(() => this.calm(), CELEBRATE_MS);
    }

    calm() {
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.party);
        this.party = 0;
        this.machine.classList.remove('jackpot');
    }

    show(text) {
        [...text].forEach((symbol, index) => this.setFace(index, symbol));
        for (const reel of this.reels) {
            reel.classList.remove('blur', 'hit');
        }
    }

    setFace(index, symbol) {
        this.faces[index] = symbol;
        this.fill(this.strips[index], [above(symbol), symbol, below(symbol)]);
    }

    fill(strip, symbols) {
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const symbol of symbols) {
            const cell = this.doc.createElement('span');
            cell.textContent = symbol;
            fragment.append(cell);
        }
        strip.replaceChildren(fragment);
    }

    async press(manual) {
        if (!this.ready || this.spin || this.pressing || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        this.pressing = true;
        this.updateControls();
        let reply = { kind: 'error', id: null };
        try {
            reply = parseReply(await this.dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Press'));
        } catch {
        }
        this.pressing = false;
        if (this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        if (reply.kind === 'accepted') {
            this.markMine(reply.id);
            this.presses += 1;
            try {
                this.win.localStorage.setItem(PRESS_KEY, String(this.presses));
            } catch {
            }
            this.setStatus('Your press worked. This spin is yours.');
        } else if (reply.kind === 'busy') {
            this.setStatus(manual ? 'Someone else pressed first. Wait for the reels to stop.' : '');
        } else if (reply.kind === 'slow') {
            this.setStatus('Slow down a little.');
        } else {
            this.setStatus('Could not reach the server.');
        }
        this.updateControls();
        this.scheduleAuto();
    }

    markMine(id) {
        this.mine.add(id);
        const existing = this.shown.get(id);
        const message = this.live.get(id);
        if (existing && message) {
            const element = this.item(message, existing.classList.contains('fresh'));
            existing.replaceWith(element);
            this.shown.set(id, element);
            this.save([message]);
        }
        const crowned = this.crowned.get(id);
        const winner = this.winners.get(id);
        if (crowned && winner) {
            const element = this.item(winner, crowned.classList.contains('fresh'), false);
            crowned.replaceWith(element);
            this.crowned.set(id, element);
        }
        const enshrined = this.enshrined.get(id);
        const jackpot = this.jackpots.get(id);
        if (enshrined && jackpot) {
            const element = this.item(jackpot, enshrined.classList.contains('fresh'), false);
            enshrined.replaceWith(element);
            this.enshrined.set(id, element);
        }
    }

    toggleAuto() {
        if (this.presses < AUTO_UNLOCK) {
            return;
        }
        this.auto = !this.auto;
        this.setStatus(this.auto ? 'Auto spin is on. It keeps going in background tabs.' : 'Auto spin is off.');
        this.updateControls();
        this.scheduleAuto();
    }

    scheduleAuto() {
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.autoTimer);
        this.autoTimer = 0;
        if (!this.auto || !this.ready || this.spin || this.pressing || this.disposed) {
            return;
        }
        this.autoTimer = this.win.setTimeout(() => {
            this.autoTimer = 0;
            this.press(false);
        }, autoDelay());
    }

    updateControls() {
        if (this.spinButton) {
            this.spinButton.disabled = !this.ready || this.spin !== null || this.pressing;
        }
        if (this.autoButton) {
            this.autoButton.hidden = this.presses < AUTO_UNLOCK;
            this.autoButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(this.auto));
        }
        if (this.progress) {
            this.progress.textContent = progressText(this.presses);
        }
    }

    setStatus(text) {
        if (this.status) {
            this.status.textContent = text;
        }
    }

    async loadOlder() {
        if (!this.older) {
            return;
        }
        this.older.disabled = true;
        try {
            const archive = await this.history.page(this.cursor, PAGE_SIZE, this.live);
            this.cursor = archive.last ?? this.cursor;
            for (const record of archive.items) {
                if (record.mine) {
                    this.mine.add(record.id);
                }
                if (!this.shown.has(record.id)) {
                    this.insert(record, false);
                }
            }
            this.older.hidden = archive.done;
        } catch {
            this.older.hidden = true;
        } finally {
            this.older.disabled = false;
        }
    }

    item(message, fresh, archived = !this.live.has(message.id)) {
        const win = classify(message.text);
        const marks = hits(message.text);
        const mine = this.mine.has(message.id) || message.mine === true;
        const element = this.doc.createElement('li');
        element.className = 'msg';
        element.classList.toggle('win', win !== 'none');
        element.classList.toggle('jackpot', isJackpot(message.text));
        element.classList.toggle('mine', mine);
        element.classList.toggle('archived', archived);
        element.classList.toggle('fresh', fresh);
        element.dataset.id = message.id;
        const time = this.doc.createElement('time');
        time.dateTime = isoOf(message.id);
        time.textContent = labelOf(message.id);
        const symbols = this.doc.createElement('p');
        symbols.className = 'symbols';
        [...message.text].forEach((symbol, index) => {
            const cell = this.doc.createElement('span');
            cell.textContent = symbol;
            if (marks[index]) {
                cell.className = 'hit';
            }
            symbols.append(cell);
        });
        let row = element;
        if (win !== 'none') {
            const details = this.doc.createElement('details');
            details.className = 'odds';
            details.dataset.text = message.text;
            row = this.doc.createElement('summary');
            row.className = 'row';
            details.append(row);
            element.append(details);
        }
        row.append(time, symbols);
        if (win !== 'none' || mine) {
            const tags = this.doc.createElement('p');
            tags.className = 'tags';
            if (isJackpot(message.text)) {
                const prize = this.doc.createElement('strong');
                prize.className = 'prize';
                prize.textContent = 'Jackpot';
                tags.append(prize);
            }
            if (win !== 'none') {
                const badge = this.doc.createElement('strong');
                badge.className = 'badge';
                badge.textContent = LABELS[win];
                tags.append(badge);
            }
            if (mine) {
                const yours = this.doc.createElement('span');
                yours.className = 'yours';
                yours.textContent = 'Your press';
                tags.append(yours);
            }
            if (win !== 'none') {
                const more = this.doc.createElement('span');
                more.className = 'more';
                more.textContent = 'Odds';
                tags.append(more);
            }
            row.append(tags);
        }
        return element;
    }

    insert(message, fresh) {
        const element = this.item(message, fresh);
        place(this.feed, element, message.id);
        this.shown.set(message.id, element);
    }

    crown(message, fresh) {
        if (!this.winnersList || this.crowned.has(message.id)) {
            return;
        }
        const element = this.item(message, fresh, false);
        place(this.winnersList, element, message.id);
        this.crowned.set(message.id, element);
    }

    enshrine(message, fresh) {
        if (!this.jackpotsList || this.enshrined.has(message.id)) {
            return;
        }
        const element = this.item(message, fresh, false);
        place(this.jackpotsList, element, message.id);
        this.enshrined.set(message.id, element);
    }

    renderJackpots(pending) {
        this.enshrined.clear();
        if (!this.jackpotsList) {
            return;
        }
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const message of [...this.jackpots.values()].sort(newestFirst)) {
            if (message.id === pending) {
                continue;
            }
            const element = this.item(message, false, false);
            this.enshrined.set(message.id, element);
            fragment.append(element);
        }
        this.jackpotsList.replaceChildren(fragment);
        this.updateEmpty();
    }

    renderWinners(pending) {
        this.crowned.clear();
        if (!this.winnersList) {
            return;
        }
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const message of [...this.winners.values()].sort(newestFirst)) {
            if (message.id === pending) {
                continue;
            }
            const element = this.item(message, false, false);
            this.crowned.set(message.id, element);
            fragment.append(element);
        }
        this.winnersList.replaceChildren(fragment);
        this.updateEmpty();
    }

    explain(details) {
        if (!details?.classList?.contains('odds') || !details.open || 'explained' in details.dataset) {
            return;
        }
        const facts = lesson(details.dataset.text, this.spinMs);
        if (!facts) {
            return;
        }
        details.dataset.explained = '';
        const body = this.doc.createElement('div');
        body.className = 'lesson';
        const headline = this.doc.createElement('p');
        headline.className = 'headline';
        const odds = this.doc.createElement('strong');
        odds.textContent = facts.headline;
        headline.append(odds, ` ${facts.summary}`);
        const list = this.doc.createElement('dl');
        for (const [term, value] of facts.facts) {
            const dt = this.doc.createElement('dt');
            dt.textContent = term;
            const dd = this.doc.createElement('dd');
            dd.textContent = value;
            list.append(dt, dd);
        }
        body.append(headline, list);
        for (const [title, text] of facts.sections) {
            const paragraph = this.doc.createElement('p');
            const label = this.doc.createElement('strong');
            label.textContent = `${title}. `;
            paragraph.append(label, text);
            body.append(paragraph);
        }
        details.append(body);
    }

    render(messages, done) {
        this.shown.clear();
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const message of messages) {
            if (message.mine) {
                this.mine.add(message.id);
            }
            const element = this.item(message, false);
            this.shown.set(message.id, element);
            fragment.append(element);
        }
        this.feed.replaceChildren(fragment);
        if (this.older) {
            this.older.hidden = done;
        }
        this.updateEmpty();
    }

    announce(message) {
        if (this.disposed || !this.feed?.isConnected) {
            return;
        }
        const win = classify(message.text);
        const spelled = [...message.text].join(' ');
        const prefix = isJackpot(message.text) ? 'Jackpot!' : 'Winner!';
        announce(this.doc, win === 'none' ? `Result: ${spelled}.` : `${prefix} ${spelled}. ${LABELS[win]}.`);
    }

    updateEmpty() {
        if (this.empty) {
            this.empty.hidden = this.shown.size > 0;
        }
        if (this.winnersEmpty) {
            this.winnersEmpty.hidden = this.crowned.size > 0;
        }
        if (this.jackpotsEmpty) {
            this.jackpotsEmpty.hidden = this.enshrined.size > 0;
        }
    }

    dispose() {
        this.disposed = true;
        this.win.clearInterval(this.timer);
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.autoTimer);
        this.calm();
        if (this.spin) {
            for (const timer of this.spin.timers) {
                this.win.clearTimeout(timer);
            }
            for (const animation of this.spin.animations) {
                animation.cancel();
            }
            this.spin = null;
        }
        this.spinButton?.removeEventListener('click', this.onSpin);
        this.autoButton?.removeEventListener('click', this.onAuto);
        this.older?.removeEventListener('click', this.onOlder);
        this.win.removeEventListener('chaos:reconnected', this.onReconnected);
        this.doc.removeEventListener('toggle', this.onToggle, true);
    }
}

function place(list, element, id) {
    let next = list.firstElementChild;
    while (next && next.dataset.id > id) {
        next = next.nextElementSibling;
    }
    list.insertBefore(element, next);
}

export function attach(machine, dotnet) {
    const client = new SlotClient(machine, dotnet, document, window);
    client.start();
    return {
        apply: updates => client.apply(updates),
        dispose: () => client.dispose(),
    };
}

FILE:     src/Chaos.Web/Components/Pages/About.razor
SIZE:     9.13 KB (9353 bytes)
