@page "/about"

<PageTitle>About chaos</PageTitle>

<h1>About</h1>
<p class="lede">Chaos is a small public experiment: a message board, a drawing canvas, a slot machine and a latency test that anyone can use, with no accounts and nothing kept for long.</p>

<h2>Why it exists</h2>
<p>To show how much a plain Blazor Server app can do on a free hosting plan with a quarter of a CPU and 256 MB of memory, with no database, no cookies and no third-party code. The HTML, the CSS and the small amount of JavaScript are all written by hand.</p>

<h2>What you can do</h2>
<dl>
    <dt><a href="board">Board</a></dt>
    <dd>Post a message of up to 500 characters. Everyone on the board sees it at once. The server keeps the newest 50. This browser keeps up to 10,000 on your device, so you still see messages after the server has forgotten them. Those have a dotted border. Your own messages are marked <em>Your message</em> in this browser only. You can choose to attach your location and some browser details to your messages; see <a href="about#location">Location and details</a>.</dd>
    <dt><a href="canvas">Canvas</a></dt>
    <dd>Draw with a mouse, a finger or a stylus. Everyone sees each stroke as it is drawn. Anyone can reset the page for everyone. This browser keeps up to 10,000 finished strokes, so after a server restart the drawing comes back from the browsers that saw it, unless someone had pressed reset since.</dd>
    <dt><a href="slots">Slots</a></dt>
    <dd>Press the button and the shared machine spins for everyone watching. Nobody can press again until the reels stop, and then whoever presses first goes next. Nobody knows who pressed, but you are told whether your own press worked. After ten successful presses from this browser you get an auto spin button that presses for you each time the reels stop, even while the tab is in the background. Four or five of a kind, or five symbols in an unbroken sequence like <code>ABCDE</code>, is a jackpot: the machine celebrates, and every jackpot is kept forever in its own list. The newest 500 winners stay in their own list below the results. Tap any winner to see how rare it was and the probability behind it. No money or prizes are involved; it is just for fun.</dd>
    <dt><a href="latency">Latency</a></dt>
    <dd>Press the button and your browser times how long a list of public endpoints takes to answer, while this server times the same endpoints from its data centre. Each endpoint gets one warm-up request and then the fastest of three is kept. Everyone sees every result, with no name or address attached. Each network can start a test every five minutes, so phones and computers on the same Wi-Fi share that limit. After ten tests from this browser an auto run button appears. Below the results, a leaderboard shows the ten fastest and slowest medians from browsers and from the server. At the bottom, a browser or server median under 10 ms is a jackpot winner and 100 ms or more a jackpot loser. A median only counts when at least half of the endpoints answered.</dd>
</dl>

<h2>Winners</h2>
<p>Each spin is five symbols, each one of the letters A to Z or the digits 0 to 9. A spin is a winner if:</p>
<ul>
    <li><strong>Three, four or five of a kind:</strong> the same symbol appears at least three times, anywhere. <code>7Q7A7</code> wins.</li>
    <li><strong>Sequence up or down:</strong> five neighbours in a row along 0 to 9 and then A to Z, with no gaps. <code>ABCDE</code>, <code>789AB</code>, <code>43210</code> and <code>VWXYZ</code> win. It does not wrap around, so <code>YZ012</code> does not.</li>
    <li><strong>Straight up or down:</strong> all five symbols climb or all five fall, with no repeats but with gaps, in the same order. <code>13AKZ</code> and <code>ZKA80</code> win; <code>ZK8A0</code> does not.</li>
</ul>
<p>Winners have a gold border and a star badge in the results, and their winning symbols are highlighted. About one spin in fifty wins: 1,201,320 of the 60,466,176 possible results. Straights are the most common win, 753,920 results. Five of a kind is 1 in 1,679,616. A sequence in either direction is 1 in 944,784: only 32 runs go up and 32 come down.</p>
<p>Four of a kind, five of a kind and sequences are <strong>jackpots</strong>: 6,400 results, about 1 in 9,448. Jackpots get a red border and a crown badge, and they are never removed: not by the server, not by your browser's 10,000 limit.</p>

<h2>How it works</h2>
<ul>
    <li>Pages are rendered on the server and arrive as plain HTML. Only the board, the canvas, the slot machine and the latency test open a live connection, over a WebSocket.</li>
    <li>The clock at the bottom of every page shows the server's time, not your device's. The page measures how far its own clock is from the server's and keeps ticking from there.</li>
    <li>Every message and every spin gets a UUIDv7 from the server clock, so its ID also records when it happened. Anything that claims to come from the future is rejected.</li>
    <li>The server can restart at any time and then remembers nothing. When you open the board or the slots, your browser offers its 50 newest entries back, and the server keeps the ones that fit among its newest 50. The slots page also offers its 500 newest winners back for the winners list and up to 500 jackpots for the jackpot list.</li>
    <li>The canvas sends strokes in small batches of points measured from 0 to 1 across a square page, so phones and desktops draw the same picture. Each reset starts a new round, and strokes still in flight from the old one are dropped. Every finished stroke and every reset gets a UUIDv7, so after a restart the server takes back only strokes drawn after the newest reset anyone remembers.</li>
    <li>The latency test from your browser uses plain requests whose answers it cannot read, only time. It cannot see anything those sites send back, and they see a request with no cookies and no referrer.</li>
    <li>The slot result is drawn on the server with a cryptographic random number generator the moment a press is accepted. The server then refuses every press until the spin time has passed, so only one spin runs at a time. Your browser plays the reels for the time the server says is left, then adds the result to the list; your own button stays off until your reels stop, so a spin always settles on your screen before you can press again.</li>
    <li>The reels are plain HTML animated with the Web Animations API. If your device asks for reduced motion, the symbols change without spinning.</li>
    <li>A strict Content Security Policy only allows scripts and styles from this site, and connections only to this site and the latency test endpoints. There are no inline scripts.</li>
</ul>

<h2>Before you post</h2>
<p class="warning">Anything you post or draw goes to every connected browser at once and is stored on their devices. It can't be edited, deleted or recalled. Nothing is moderated. Don't share anything private.</p>

<h2>Your data</h2>
<p>There are no accounts, no cookies and no tracking. Your theme choice, your count of successful slot presses and latency tests, and when your next test is allowed are kept in this browser's local storage. Board messages, canvas strokes, slot results and latency results are kept in this browser's IndexedDB, up to 10,000 of each, with the oldest removed first. Slot jackpots are kept separately and never removed unless you clear local history. Messages you posted and results you started are marked as yours only in this browser. Latency results carry only timings: no address, no name and no device details. Rate limits count by network address in the server's memory only; addresses are never saved or shown, and devices sharing one internet connection share one limit.</p>
<h3 id="location">Location and details</h3>
<p>This is optional and off unless you turn it on. The board works the same either way. If you tick <em>Attach my location and browser details</em> and your browser grants location permission, every message you send carries what your browser reports at that moment: latitude, longitude and accuracy, plus altitude, heading and speed when known, your time zone, UTC offset and local time, language, user agent, platform, screen and window size, CPU threads, memory, touch points, color scheme, motion and contrast settings, and connection type. Anyone can open it under the message with <em>Details</em>, and it is kept and offered back like the message itself. Nothing is looked up from it: no maps, no address, no third-party service. The server does not add your network address. Your browser reports these values and the server cannot check them. Untick the box, or block location for this site, to stop. Messages already sent keep their details.</p>
<p class="clear">
    <button type="button" data-clear-history="clear-status">Clear local history</button>
    <output id="clear-status"></output>
</p>
<p>This removes the board, canvas, slot and latency history and your counts on this device. The next time you open those pages, they fill up again from the server.</p>

<h2>Source code</h2>
<p>The code is on <a href="https://github.com/kusl/chaos-velixir-run">GitHub</a> under the GNU Affero General Public License, version 3 or later. If you run a modified copy for other people, you have to offer them its source too.</p>

@page "/board"
@inject BoardService Board
@inject TimeProvider Time

<PageTitle>Chaos board</PageTitle>

<h1>Board</h1>
<p class="lede">Say something. The server keeps the newest @Board.Capacity messages and this browser keeps what it has seen. A dotted border marks a message the server has already forgotten. If you let this site see your location, your messages also carry it and some details about your browser, which anyone can open under the message.</p>

<BoardIsland Client="@_client" @rendermode="InteractiveServer" />

<section class="feed" data-permanent="board-feed" aria-labelledby="feed-title">
    <h2 id="feed-title" class="visually-hidden">Messages</h2>
    <p id="board-empty" class="empty" hidden="@(_messages.Count > 0)">No messages yet. Write the first one.</p>
    <ol id="board-feed" class="messages">
        @foreach (var message in _messages)
        {
            <li @key="message.Id" class="msg" data-id="@message.Id">
                <div class="head"><time datetime="@MessageFormat.Iso(message.Id)">@MessageFormat.Label(message.Id, _now)</time></div>
                <p class="text">@message.Text</p>
                @if (message.Meta is { Count: > 0 } meta)
                {
                    <details class="meta">
                        <summary>Details</summary>
                        <dl>
                            @foreach (var entry in meta)
                            {
                                <div><dt>@MessageMeta.LabelOf(entry.Key)</dt><dd>@entry.Value</dd></div>
                            }
                        </dl>
                    </details>
                }
            </li>
        }
    </ol>
    <button type="button" id="board-older" class="older" hidden>Show older messages</button>
</section>

@code {
    private IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> _messages = [];
    private DateTimeOffset _now;
    private string _client = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    [CascadingParameter]
    private HttpContext? HttpContext { get; set; }

    protected override void OnInitialized()
    {
        _messages = Board.Messages;
        _now = Time.GetUtcNow();
        _client = ClientAddress.Key(HttpContext?.Connection.RemoteIpAddress);
    }
}

@page "/latency"
@inject LatencyService Latency
@inject TimeProvider Time

<PageTitle>Chaos latency</PageTitle>

<h1>Latency</h1>
<p class="lede">One button times how long @LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Count public endpoints take to answer, once from your browser and once from this server. Everyone sees every result. Each network can run a test every @Minutes minutes, so devices on the same Wi-Fi share that limit.</p>

<LatencyIsland Client="@_client" @rendermode="InteractiveServer" />

<section class="feed" data-permanent="latency-feed" aria-labelledby="runs-title">
    <h2 id="runs-title">Results</h2>
    <p id="latency-empty" class="empty" hidden="@(_runs.Count > 0)">No tests yet. Press the button.</p>
    <ol id="latency-feed" class="runs">
        @foreach (var run in _runs)
        {
            @Item(run, null, null, 0)
        }
    </ol>
    <button type="button" id="latency-older" class="older" hidden>Show older results</button>
</section>

<section class="feed" data-permanent="latency-leaders" aria-labelledby="leaders-title">
    <h2 id="leaders-title">Leaderboard</h2>
    <p class="note">The @Latency.RankCapacity lowest and highest medians seen. A median only counts when at least half of the endpoints answered on that side.</p>
    <div class="boards">
        @Board("latency-browser-fastest", "Fastest browsers", "No browser results yet.", _leaders.BrowserFastest, null, LatencySide.Browser)
        @Board("latency-browser-slowest", "Slowest browsers", "No browser results yet.", _leaders.BrowserSlowest, null, LatencySide.Browser)
        @Board("latency-server-fastest", "Fastest server runs", "No server results yet.", _leaders.ServerFastest, null, LatencySide.Server)
        @Board("latency-server-slowest", "Slowest server runs", "No server results yet.", _leaders.ServerSlowest, null, LatencySide.Server)
    </div>
</section>

<section class="feed" data-permanent="latency-jackpots" aria-labelledby="jackpots-title">
    <h2 id="jackpots-title">Jackpots</h2>
    <p class="note">A browser or server median under @LatencyLeaders.FastBelow ms wins; @LatencyLeaders.SlowFrom ms or more loses. The newest @Latency.JackpotCapacity of each are kept.</p>
    <div class="boards">
        @Board("latency-jackpot-winners", "Winners", "Nobody yet. Probably nobody ever.", _leaders.FastJackpots, "fast", null)
        @Board("latency-jackpot-losers", "Losers", "Nobody yet. Hopefully nobody ever.", _leaders.SlowJackpots, "slow", null)
    </div>
</section>

@code {
    private IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> _runs = [];
    private LatencyLeaders _leaders = LatencyLeaders.Empty;
    private DateTimeOffset _now;
    private string _client = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    private int Minutes => Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(Latency.Cooldown.TotalMinutes));

    [CascadingParameter]
    private HttpContext? HttpContext { get; set; }

    protected override void OnInitialized()
    {
        _runs = Latency.Messages;
        _leaders = Latency.Leaders;
        _now = Time.GetUtcNow();
        _client = ClientAddress.Key(HttpContext?.Connection.RemoteIpAddress);
    }

    private static string SideLabel(LatencySide side) => side == LatencySide.Browser ? "Browser" : "Server";

    private RenderFragment Board(string id, string title, string empty, IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> runs, string? jackpot, LatencySide? side) =>
        @<section class="board" aria-labelledby="@(id + "-title")">
            <h3 id="@(id + "-title")">@title</h3>
            <p id="@(id + "-empty")" class="empty" hidden="@(runs.Count > 0)">@empty</p>
            <ol id="@id" class="runs leaders">
                @for (var i = 0; i < runs.Count; i++)
                {
                    @Item(runs[i], jackpot, side, i + 1)
                }
            </ol>
        </section>;

    private RenderFragment Item(BoardMessage run, string? jackpot, LatencySide? side, int place) => __builder =>
    {
        if (!LatencyRun.TryParse(run.Text, out var results))
        {
            return;
        }

        var summary = LatencyRun.Summarize(results);
        var rank = LatencyRun.Rank(results);
        var css = jackpot is not null ? $"msg leader {jackpot}" : side is not null ? "msg leader" : "msg";
        <li @key="run.Id" class="@css" data-id="@run.Id">
            @if (side is { } ranked)
            {
                <p class="headline"><span class="place">@place</span><strong>@LatencyRun.Milliseconds(LatencyLeaders.Of(rank, ranked))</strong></p>
            }
            else if (jackpot is not null)
            {
                <p class="headline">
                    @foreach (var each in Sides)
                    {
                        var median = LatencyLeaders.Of(rank, each);
                        if (jackpot == "fast" ? LatencyLeaders.IsFast(median) : LatencyLeaders.IsSlow(median))
                        {
                            <strong>@SideLabel(each) @LatencyRun.Milliseconds(median)</strong>
                        }
                    }
                </p>
            }
            <time datetime="@MessageFormat.Iso(run.Id)">@MessageFormat.Label(run.Id, _now)</time>
            <p class="summary">
                <span>Browser median <strong>@LatencyRun.Milliseconds(summary.Browser)</strong></span>
                <span>Server median <strong>@LatencyRun.Milliseconds(summary.Server)</strong></span>
                <span>@summary.Answered of @summary.Total answered</span>
            </p>
            <details>
                <summary>All endpoints</summary>
                <div class="table-wrap">
                    <table>
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th scope="col">Endpoint</th>
                                <th scope="col">Browser</th>
                                <th scope="col">Server</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var result in results)
                            {
                                <tr>
                                    <th scope="row">@LatencyCatalog.LabelOf(result.Key)</th>
                                    <td>@LatencyRun.Milliseconds(result.Browser)</td>
                                    <td>@LatencyRun.Milliseconds(result.Server)</td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </details>
        </li>
    };

    private static readonly LatencySide[] Sides = [LatencySide.Browser, LatencySide.Server];
}

    [Parameter, EditorRequired]
    public string Text { get; set; } = "";

    [Parameter]
    public DateTimeOffset Now { get; set; }

    private string Classes => _win switch
    {
        SlotWin.None => "msg",
        _ when SlotRules.IsJackpot(_win) => "msg win jackpot",
        _ => "msg win",
    };

    {
        _win = SlotRules.Classify(Text);
        _hits = SlotRules.Hits(Text);
    }

    private RenderFragment Row => __builder =>
    {

        <p class="symbols">
            @for (var i = 0; i < Text.Length; i++)
            {
                <span class="@(_hits[i] ? "hit" : null)">@Text[i]</span>
            }
        </p>
        @if (_win is not SlotWin.None)
        {
            <p class="tags">
                @if (SlotRules.IsJackpot(_win))
                {
                    <strong class="prize">Jackpot</strong>
                }
                <strong class="badge">@SlotRules.Label(_win)</strong><span class="more">Odds</span>

        }
    };

<PageTitle>Chaos slots</PageTitle>

<h1>Slots</h1>
<p class="lede">One shared machine and one big button. Whoever presses first spins for everyone, and nobody can press again until the reels stop. Three or more of a kind, or five climbing or falling, is a winner. Four or five of a kind, or an unbroken sequence like ABCDE or 43210, is a jackpot. Tap a winner to see how rare it was and why. Nothing is at stake.</p>

<SlotIsland Client="@_client" Face="@_face" @rendermode="InteractiveServer" />

<section class="feed" data-permanent="slots-jackpots-feed" aria-labelledby="jackpots-title">

    <h2 id="winners-title">Winners</h2>
    <p class="note">The newest @Slots.WinnerCapacity winning spins, kept after they leave the results.</p>
    <p id="slots-winners-empty" class="empty" hidden="@(_winners.Count > 0)">No winners yet.</p>
    <ol id="slots-winners" class="spins">

@code {
    private IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> _results = [];
    private IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> _winners = [];
    private IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> _jackpots = [];
    private DateTimeOffset _now;
    private string _client = ClientAddress.Unknown;
    private string? _face;

    [CascadingParameter]
    private HttpContext? HttpContext { get; set; }

    protected override void OnInitialized()
    {
        var spinning = Slots.Spinning?.Id;
        _results = [.. Slots.Messages.Where(result => result.Id != spinning)];
        _winners = [.. Slots.Winners.Where(result => result.Id != spinning)];
        _jackpots = [.. Slots.Jackpots.Where(result => result.Id != spinning)];
        _face = _results.Count > 0 ? _results[0].Text : null;
        _now = Time.GetUtcNow();
        _client = ClientAddress.Key(HttpContext?.Connection.RemoteIpAddress);
    }
}

FILE:     src/Chaos.Web/Drawing/CanvasService.cs
SIZE:     10.88 KB (11136 bytes)
LINES:    416

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Drawing;

internal sealed class CanvasService
{
    public const int MaxCoordinate = 4095;
    public const int MaxBatchPoints = 256;
    public const int MaxStrokePoints = 2048;
    public const int MaxOfferedStrokes = 500;

    public static readonly TimeSpan ResetCooldown = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1);

    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private readonly int _maxPoints;
    private readonly double _pointBurst;
    private readonly double _pointsPerSecond;
    private readonly KeyedTokenBuckets _resets;
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly LinkedList<Stroke> _strokes = new();
    private readonly HashSet<Guid> _ids = [];
    private readonly HashSet<ICanvasSubscriber> _subscribers = [];
    private int _epoch = 1;
    private int _points;
    private long? _lastReset;
    private Guid _resetId;
    private Guid _lastId;

    public CanvasService(TimeProvider time, IOptions<ChaosOptions> options)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(options);
        var limits = options.Value;
        _time = time;
        _maxPoints = limits.CanvasMaxPoints;
        _pointBurst = limits.StrokePointBurst;
        _pointsPerSecond = limits.StrokePointsPerSecond;
        _resets = new KeyedTokenBuckets(limits.ResetBurst, limits.ResetsPerSecond, time);
        StartedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; }

    public int MaxPoints => _maxPoints;

    public int Epoch
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _epoch;
            }
        }
    }

    public Guid ResetId
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _resetId;
            }
        }
    }

    public int PointCount
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _points;
            }
        }
    }

    public int StrokeCount
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _strokes.Count;
            }
        }
    }

    public CanvasSession OpenSession(ICanvasSubscriber subscriber) =>
        new(subscriber, new TokenBucket(_pointBurst, _pointsPerSecond, _time));

    public DrawResult Draw(CanvasSession session, int epoch, int clientStroke, int[] points, bool end)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(session);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(points);

        if (points.Length % 2 != 0 || points.Length > MaxBatchPoints * 2 || (points.Length == 0 && !end) || !InRange(points))
        {
            return DrawResult.Invalid;
        }

        var count = points.Length / 2;
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (epoch != _epoch)
            {
                return DrawResult.Stale;
            }

            var stroke = session.Open is { } open && open.Epoch == _epoch && open.ClientId == clientStroke ? open : null;
            if (stroke is null)
            {
                if (clientStroke <= session.LastClientId || count == 0)
                {
                    return DrawResult.Invalid;
                }
            }
            else if (stroke.Count + count > MaxStrokePoints)
            {
                FinishOpen(session);
                return DrawResult.TooLong;
            }

            if (!session.Bucket.TryTake(count))
            {
                return DrawResult.TooFast;
            }

            if (!MakeRoom(count))
            {
                return DrawResult.Full;
            }

            if (stroke is null)
            {
                FinishOpen(session);
                stroke = new Stroke(Mint(), _epoch, clientStroke);
                _strokes.AddLast(stroke);
                _ids.Add(stroke.Id);
                session.Open = stroke;
                session.LastClientId = clientStroke;
                session.LastStroke = stroke.Id;
            }

            var offset = stroke.Count;
            stroke.Append(points);
            _points += count;
            if (end)
            {
                stroke.Done = true;
                session.Open = null;
            }

            Broadcast(CanvasUpdate.ForStroke(_epoch, stroke.Id, offset, points, end), session.Subscriber);
            return DrawResult.Accepted;
        }
    }

    public ResetResult Reset(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var now = _time.GetTimestamp();
            if (_lastReset is { } last && _time.GetElapsedTime(last, now) < ResetCooldown)
            {
                return ResetResult.TooFast;
            }

            if (!_resets.TryTake(client))
            {
                return ResetResult.TooFast;
            }

            _lastReset = now;
            _epoch++;
            _resetId = Mint();
            _strokes.Clear();
            _ids.Clear();
            _points = 0;
            Broadcast(CanvasUpdate.ForReset(_epoch, _resetId), null);
            return ResetResult.Accepted;
        }
    }

    public bool OfferReset(Guid reset)
    {
        if (!MessageWindow.IsRestorable(reset, _time.GetUtcNow(), StartedAt))
        {
            return false;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (reset.CompareTo(_resetId) <= 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            _resetId = reset;
            if (reset.CompareTo(_lastId) > 0)
            {
                _lastId = reset;
            }

            var node = _strokes.First;
            while (node is not null)
            {
                var next = node.Next;
                if (node.Value.Id.CompareTo(reset) <= 0)
                {
                    _points -= node.Value.Count;
                    _ids.Remove(node.Value.Id);
                    _strokes.Remove(node);
                }
                else
                {
                    node.Value.Done = true;
                }

                node = next;
            }

            _epoch++;
            Broadcast(Snapshot(), null);
            return true;
        }
    }

    public int OfferStrokes(IEnumerable<StrokeOffer> offered)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(offered);
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var valid = offered
            .Take(MaxOfferedStrokes)
            .Where(offer => offer is not null && IsRestorable(offer, now))
            .DistinctBy(offer => offer.Id)
            .OrderByDescending(offer => offer.Id)
            .ToList();
        if (valid.Count == 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            var added = new List<StrokeSnapshot>();
            foreach (var offer in valid)
            {
                var count = offer.Points.Count / 2;
                if (offer.Id.CompareTo(_resetId) <= 0 || _ids.Contains(offer.Id) || _points + count > _maxPoints)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var stroke = new Stroke(offer.Id, _epoch, 0) { Done = true };
                stroke.Append(offer.Points);
                Insert(stroke);
                _points += count;
                if (offer.Id.CompareTo(_lastId) > 0)
                {
                    _lastId = offer.Id;
                }

                added.Add(new StrokeSnapshot(stroke.Id, stroke.ToArray(), true));
            }

            if (added.Count > 0)
            {
                Broadcast(CanvasUpdate.ForMerge(_epoch, added), null);
            }

            return added.Count;
        }
    }

    public void Abandon(CanvasSession session)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(session);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            FinishOpen(session);
        }
    }

    public void Subscribe(ICanvasSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(subscriber);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (subscriber.Deliver(Snapshot()))
            {
                _subscribers.Add(subscriber);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Unsubscribe(ICanvasSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _subscribers.Remove(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public bool IsSubscribed(ICanvasSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _subscribers.Contains(subscriber);
        }
    }

    private static bool InRange(IReadOnlyList<int> points)
    {
        foreach (var value in points)
        {
            if ((uint)value > MaxCoordinate)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private bool IsRestorable(StrokeOffer offer, DateTimeOffset now) =>
        offer.Points is { Count: > 0 } points
        && points.Count % 2 == 0
        && points.Count <= MaxStrokePoints * 2
        && InRange(points)
        && MessageWindow.IsRestorable(offer.Id, now, StartedAt);

    private CanvasUpdate Snapshot() =>
        CanvasUpdate.ForSnapshot(
            _epoch,
            [.. _strokes.Select(stroke => new StrokeSnapshot(stroke.Id, stroke.ToArray(), stroke.Done))],
            _resetId == Guid.Empty ? null : _resetId,
            StartedAt.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds(),
            Math.Max(0, _maxPoints - _points));

    private Guid Mint()
    {
        _lastId = Uuid7.After(_lastId, _time.GetUtcNow());
        return _lastId;
    }

    private void Insert(Stroke stroke)
    {
        var node = _strokes.First;
        while (node is not null && node.Value.Id.CompareTo(stroke.Id) < 0)
        {
            node = node.Next;
        }

        if (node is null)
        {
            _strokes.AddLast(stroke);
        }
        else
        {
            _strokes.AddBefore(node, stroke);
        }

        _ids.Add(stroke.Id);
    }

    private void FinishOpen(CanvasSession session)
    {
        if (session.Open is { } open && open.Epoch == _epoch && !open.Done)
        {
            open.Done = true;
            Broadcast(CanvasUpdate.ForStroke(_epoch, open.Id, open.Count, [], true), session.Subscriber);
        }

        session.Open = null;
    }

    private bool MakeRoom(int count)
    {
        var node = _strokes.First;
        while (_points + count > _maxPoints && node is not null)
        {
            var next = node.Next;
            if (node.Value.Done)
            {
                _points -= node.Value.Count;
                _ids.Remove(node.Value.Id);
                _strokes.Remove(node);
            }

            node = next;
        }

        return _points + count <= _maxPoints;
    }

    private void Broadcast(CanvasUpdate update, ICanvasSubscriber? except)
    {
        List<ICanvasSubscriber>? dropped = null;
        foreach (var subscriber in _subscribers)
        {
            if (!ReferenceEquals(subscriber, except) && !subscriber.Deliver(update))
            {
                (dropped ??= []).Add(subscriber);
            }
        }

        if (dropped is not null)
        {
            _subscribers.ExceptWith(dropped);
        }
    }
}

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class ChaosOptions
{
    public const string SectionName = "Chaos";

    public IList<string> AllowedOrigins { get; } = [];

    public int MaxConnectionsPerClient { get; set; } = 8;

    public int MaxConnections { get; set; } = 80;

    public int RequestsPerSecond { get; set; } = 10;

    public int RequestBurst { get; set; } = 120;

    public int BoardCapacity { get; set; } = 50;

    public double PostBurst { get; set; } = 3;

    public double PostsPerSecond { get; set; } = 0.5;

    public int CanvasMaxPoints { get; set; } = 20_000;

    public double StrokePointBurst { get; set; } = 4_000;

    public double StrokePointsPerSecond { get; set; } = 1_500;

    public int SlotJackpotCapacity { get; set; } = 10_000;

    public string SlotDrawSymbols { get; set; } = SlotRules.Symbols;

    public double PressBurst { get; set; } = 3;

    public double PressesPerSecond { get; set; } = 0.5;

    public int SpinMilliseconds { get; set; } = 4000;

    public int LatencyCapacity { get; set; } = 50;

    public int LatencyRankCapacity { get; set; } = 10;

    public int LatencyJackpotCapacity { get; set; } = 50;

    public int LatencyCooldownSeconds { get; set; } = 300;

    public int LatencyGapSeconds { get; set; } = 10;

    public int LatencyBudgetSeconds { get; set; } = 30;

        MaxConnectionsPerClient > 0
        && MaxConnections >= MaxConnectionsPerClient
        && RequestsPerSecond > 0
        && RequestBurst > 0
        && BoardCapacity > 0
        && PostBurst >= 1
        && PostsPerSecond > 0

        && StrokePointBurst >= CanvasService.MaxBatchPoints

        && ResetBurst >= 1

        && LatencyCapacity > 0
        && LatencyRankCapacity is >= 1 and <= 100

        && LatencyCooldownSeconds is >= 1 and <= 86_400

        && LatencyBudgetSeconds is >= 5 and <= 120;
}

using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class ClientAddress
{
    public const string Unknown = "unknown";

    public static string Key(IPAddress? address)
    {
        if (address is null)
        {
            return Unknown;
        }

        if (address.IsIPv4MappedToIPv6)
        {
            address = address.MapToIPv4();
        }

        if (address.AddressFamily != AddressFamily.InterNetworkV6)
        {
            return address.ToString();
        }

        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        address.TryWriteBytes(bytes, out _);
        bytes[8..].Clear();
        return new IPAddress(bytes) + "/64";
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal enum ConnectionAdmission
{
    Admitted,
    ClientFull,
    ServerFull,
}

internal sealed class ConnectionTracker(IOptions<ChaosOptions> options)
{
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, int> _perClient = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private int _total;

    public int Total
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _total;
            }
        }
    }

    public int CountFor(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client);
        }
    }

    public ConnectionAdmission TryAdmit(string client, out IDisposable? lease)
    {
        var limits = options.Value;
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (_total >= limits.MaxConnections)
            {
                lease = null;
                return ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull;
            }

            var count = _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client);
            if (count >= limits.MaxConnectionsPerClient)
            {
                lease = null;
                return ConnectionAdmission.ClientFull;
            }

            _perClient[client] = count + 1;
            _total++;
        }

        lease = new Lease(this, client);
        return ConnectionAdmission.Admitted;
    }

    private void Release(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var count = _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client) - 1;
            if (count > 0)
            {
                _perClient[client] = count;
            }
            else
            {
                _perClient.Remove(client);
            }

            _total--;
        }
    }

    private sealed class Lease(ConnectionTracker owner, string client) : IDisposable
    {
        private int _released;

        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _released, 1) == 0)
            {
                owner.Release(client);
            }
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class CookielessAntiforgery : IAntiforgery
{
    private static readonly AntiforgeryTokenSet NoTokens = new(null, null, "__RequestVerificationToken", null);

    public AntiforgeryTokenSet GetAndStoreTokens(HttpContext httpContext) => NoTokens;

    public AntiforgeryTokenSet GetTokens(HttpContext httpContext) => NoTokens;

    public Task<bool> IsRequestValidAsync(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(httpContext);
        return Task.FromResult(IsSafe(httpContext.Request.Method));
    }

    public Task ValidateRequestAsync(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(httpContext);
        return IsSafe(httpContext.Request.Method)
            ? Task.CompletedTask
            : Task.FromException(new AntiforgeryValidationException("This site accepts no form posts."));
    }

    public void SetCookieTokenAndHeader(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
    }

    private static bool IsSafe(string method) =>
        HttpMethods.IsGet(method) || HttpMethods.IsHead(method) || HttpMethods.IsOptions(method) || HttpMethods.IsTrace(method);
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static partial class HubGuard
{
    public static readonly PathString HubPath = new("/_blazor");

    public static IApplicationBuilder UseHubGuard(this IApplicationBuilder app)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(app);
        var tracker = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<ConnectionTracker>();
        var options = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<IOptions<ChaosOptions>>();
        var logger = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("Chaos.Web.HubGuard");

        return app.Use(async (context, next) =>
        {
            if (!context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(HubPath, out var remainder))
            {
                await next(context);
                return;
            }

            if (!OriginPolicy.IsAllowed(context.Request.Headers.Origin, context.Request.Host.Value, options.Value.AllowedOrigins))
            {
                await RejectAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden, "Origin not allowed.");
                return;
            }

            if (remainder.HasValue)
            {
                await next(context);
                return;
            }

            if (!context.WebSockets.IsWebSocketRequest)
            {
                await RejectAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest, "Only WebSocket connections are accepted.");
                return;
            }

            var admission = tracker.TryAdmit(ClientAddress.Key(context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress), out var lease);
            if (admission is not ConnectionAdmission.Admitted)
            {
                if (admission is ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull)
                {
                    LogServerFull(logger, tracker.Total);
                }

                context.Response.Headers.RetryAfter = "10";
                var status = admission is ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull
                    ? StatusCodes.Status503ServiceUnavailable
                    : StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
                await RejectAsync(context, status, "Too many connections.");
                return;
            }

            using (lease)
            {
                await next(context);
            }
        });
    }

    private static Task RejectAsync(HttpContext context, int status, string message)
    {
        context.Response.StatusCode = status;
        context.Response.ContentType = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
        return context.Response.WriteAsync(message, context.RequestAborted);
    }

    [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Warning, Message = "Refused a connection while holding {Connections} connections")]
    private static partial void LogServerFull(ILogger logger, int connections);
}

using Microsoft.JSInterop;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class JsCleanup
{
    public static async ValueTask DisposeAsync(IJSObjectReference? reference, string? method)
    {
        if (reference is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            if (method is not null)
            {
                await reference.InvokeVoidAsync(method).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }

            await reference.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException)
        {
        }
    }

    public static async ValueTask InvokeAsync(IJSObjectReference? reference, string method)
    {
        if (reference is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            await reference.InvokeVoidAsync(method).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException)
        {
        }
    }
}

FILE:     src/Chaos.Web/Infrastructure/KeyedCooldowns.cs
SIZE:     1.50 KB (1536 bytes)
LINES:    63
SHA256:   0864e0362ff44b3fe378068a4b18cfbf161204f3f1f6f33da06b6b115cb95c38
MODIFIED: 2026-10-16 10:24:45 (UTC)

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class KeyedCooldowns(TimeSpan cooldown, TimeProvider time, int maxKeys = 10_000)
{
    private readonly Dictionary<string, long> _stamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();

    public TimeSpan Remaining(string key)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return RemainingAt(key, time.GetTimestamp());
        }
    }

    public bool TryStart(string key, out TimeSpan remaining)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var now = time.GetTimestamp();
            remaining = RemainingAt(key, now);
            if (remaining > TimeSpan.Zero)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (_stamps.Count >= maxKeys && !_stamps.ContainsKey(key))
            {
                Sweep(now);
            }

            _stamps[key] = now;
            return true;
        }
    }

    private TimeSpan RemainingAt(string key, long now)
    {
        if (!_stamps.TryGetValue(key, out var stamp))
        {
            return TimeSpan.Zero;
        }

        var left = cooldown - time.GetElapsedTime(stamp, now);
        return left > TimeSpan.Zero ? left : TimeSpan.Zero;
    }

    private void Sweep(long now)
    {
        foreach (var (key, stamp) in _stamps)
        {
            if (time.GetElapsedTime(stamp, now) >= cooldown)
            {
                _stamps.Remove(key);
            }
        }

        if (_stamps.Count >= maxKeys)
        {
            _stamps.Clear();
        }
    }
}

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class KeyedTokenBuckets(double capacity, double perSecond, TimeProvider time, int maxKeys = 10_000)
{
    private readonly Dictionary<string, TokenBucket> _buckets = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();

    public int Count
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _buckets.Count;
            }
        }
    }

    public bool TryTake(string key, double count = 1)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (!_buckets.TryGetValue(key, out var bucket))
            {
                if (_buckets.Count >= maxKeys)
                {
                    Sweep();
                }

                bucket = new TokenBucket(capacity, perSecond, time);
                _buckets[key] = bucket;
            }

            return bucket.TryTake(count);
        }
    }

    private void Sweep()
    {
        foreach (var (key, bucket) in _buckets)
        {
            if (bucket.IsFull)
            {
                _buckets.Remove(key);
            }
        }

        if (_buckets.Count >= maxKeys)
        {
            _buckets.Clear();
        }
    }
}

FILE:     src/Chaos.Web/Infrastructure/OriginPolicy.cs

SHA256:   3b44e7d51702ba2ee6f628a530f8090d0412ff3f7cee127133cb50766f8324cc

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class OriginPolicy
{
    public static bool IsAllowed(string? origin, string? host, IEnumerable<string> allowed)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(allowed);

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(origin))
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (!Uri.TryCreate(origin, UriKind.Absolute, out var uri)
            || (uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttps && uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttp))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(host) && string.Equals(uri.Authority, host, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return true;
        }

        var normalized = uri.GetLeftPart(UriPartial.Authority);
        return allowed.Any(entry => string.Equals(entry.TrimEnd('/'), normalized, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }
}

using System.Threading.Channels;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class Outbox<T> : IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly Channel<T> _channel;
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stop = new();
    private readonly Func<IReadOnlyList<T>, CancellationToken, Task> _send;
    private readonly int _maxBatch;
    private readonly TimeSpan _timeout;
    private readonly ILogger _logger;
    private readonly Task _pump;
    private volatile bool _faulted;

    public Outbox(int capacity, int maxBatch, TimeSpan timeout, Func<IReadOnlyList<T>, CancellationToken, Task> send, ILogger logger)
    {
        _channel = Channel.CreateBounded<T>(new BoundedChannelOptions(capacity)
        {
            SingleReader = true,
            FullMode = BoundedChannelFullMode.Wait,
        });
        _maxBatch = maxBatch;
        _timeout = timeout;
        _send = send;
        _logger = logger;
        _pump = Task.Run(PumpAsync);
    }

    public bool IsFaulted => _faulted;

    public bool TryPost(T item) => !_faulted && _channel.Writer.TryWrite(item);

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        _channel.Writer.TryComplete();
        await _stop.CancelAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
        await _pump.ConfigureAwait(false);
        _stop.Dispose();
    }

    private async Task PumpAsync()
    {
        var batch = new List<T>(_maxBatch);
        try
        {
            while (await _channel.Reader.WaitToReadAsync(_stop.Token).ConfigureAwait(false))
            {
                while (batch.Count < _maxBatch && _channel.Reader.TryRead(out var item))
                {
                    batch.Add(item);
                }

                using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(_stop.Token);
                timeout.CancelAfter(_timeout);
                await _send([.. batch], timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                batch.Clear();
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            _faulted = true;
            _channel.Writer.TryComplete();
            OutboxLog.SendFailed(_logger, exception);
        }
    }
}

internal static partial class OutboxLog
{
    [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Debug, Message = "Realtime delivery stopped")]
    public static partial void SendFailed(ILogger logger, Exception exception);
}

using System.Threading.RateLimiting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class RequestLimits
{
    public static readonly PathString HealthPath = new("/health");

    public static void Configure(RateLimiterOptions limiter, ChaosOptions chaos)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(limiter);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(chaos);

        limiter.RejectionStatusCode = StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
        limiter.OnRejected = static async (context, cancellationToken) =>
        {
            context.HttpContext.Response.Headers.RetryAfter = "1";
            context.HttpContext.Response.ContentType = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
            await context.HttpContext.Response.WriteAsync("Too many requests.", cancellationToken);
        };
        limiter.GlobalLimiter = PartitionedRateLimiter.Create<HttpContext, string>(context =>
            context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(HealthPath)
                ? RateLimitPartition.GetNoLimiter("health")
                : RateLimitPartition.GetTokenBucketLimiter(
                    ClientAddress.Key(context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress),
                    _ => new TokenBucketRateLimiterOptions
                    {
                        TokenLimit = chaos.RequestBurst,
                        TokensPerPeriod = chaos.RequestsPerSecond,
                        ReplenishmentPeriod = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),
                        QueueLimit = 0,
                        AutoReplenishment = true,
                    }));
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Latency;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class SecurityHeaders
{
    public static readonly string ContentSecurityPolicy =
        "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self' "
        + string.Join(' ', LatencyCatalog.Origins)
        + "; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'";

    public const string PermissionsPolicy = "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(self), payment=(), usb=()";

    public static IApplicationBuilder UseSecurityHeaders(this IApplicationBuilder app) =>
        app.Use((context, next) =>
        {
            context.Response.OnStarting(Apply, context);
            return next(context);
        });

    private static Task Apply(object state)
    {
        var headers = ((HttpContext)state).Response.Headers;
        headers.ContentSecurityPolicy = ContentSecurityPolicy;
        headers.XContentTypeOptions = "nosniff";
        headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer";
        headers["Permissions-Policy"] = PermissionsPolicy;
        headers["Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy"] = "same-origin";
        headers["Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy"] = "same-origin";
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed record ServerTime(long UnixMs, int OffsetMinutes)
{
    public static ServerTime Now(TimeProvider time)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        var local = time.GetLocalNow();
        return new ServerTime(local.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds(), (int)Math.Round(local.Offset.TotalMinutes));
    }

    [JsonIgnore]
    public string Iso => DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(UnixMs).ToOffset(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(OffsetMinutes))
        .ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:sszzz", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    [JsonIgnore]
    public string Label => DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(UnixMs).ToOffset(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(OffsetMinutes))
        .ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " " + Zone(OffsetMinutes);

    public static string Zone(int offsetMinutes)
    {
        if (offsetMinutes == 0)
        {
            return "UTC";
        }

        var sign = offsetMinutes < 0 ? "-" : "+";
        var magnitude = Math.Abs(offsetMinutes);
        return string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"UTC{sign}{magnitude / 60:00}:{magnitude % 60:00}");
    }
}

SHA256:   5fcd089cd8dfb76622bbc8126100708b503d7663d17ae3c773b3fedd5db2f40d

namespace Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class TokenBucket
{
    private readonly double _capacity;
    private readonly double _perSecond;
    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private double _tokens;
    private long _stamp;

    public TokenBucket(double capacity, double perSecond, TimeProvider time)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        _capacity = capacity;
        _perSecond = perSecond;
        _time = time;
        _tokens = capacity;
        _stamp = time.GetTimestamp();
    }

    public bool IsFull
    {
        get
        {
            Refill();
            return _tokens >= _capacity;
        }
    }

    public bool TryTake(double count = 1)
    {
        Refill();
        if (_tokens < count)
        {
            return false;
        }

        _tokens -= count;
        return true;
    }

    private void Refill()
    {
        var now = _time.GetTimestamp();
        var elapsed = _time.GetElapsedTime(_stamp, now).TotalSeconds;
        _stamp = now;
        _tokens = Math.Min(_capacity, _tokens + (elapsed * _perSecond));
    }
}

using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;

namespace Chaos.Web.Latency;

internal sealed class HttpLatencyProbe : ILatencyProbe, IDisposable
{
    public const int Samples = 3;
    public const int Parallelism = 6;
    public const string UserAgent = "chaos-velixir-run/1.0 (+https://github.com/kusl/chaos-velixir-run)";

    private static readonly TimeSpan RequestTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4);

    private readonly SocketsHttpHandler _handler;
    private readonly HttpClient _client;

    public HttpLatencyProbe()
    {
        _handler = new SocketsHttpHandler
        {
            AllowAutoRedirect = true,
            MaxAutomaticRedirections = 3,
            AutomaticDecompression = DecompressionMethods.None,
            UseCookies = false,
            ConnectTimeout = RequestTimeout,
            PooledConnectionLifetime = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1),
            PooledConnectionIdleTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20),
            MaxConnectionsPerServer = 2,
        };
        _client = new HttpClient(_handler, disposeHandler: false)
        {
            Timeout = Timeout.InfiniteTimeSpan,
            DefaultRequestVersion = HttpVersion.Version20,
            DefaultVersionPolicy = HttpVersionPolicy.RequestVersionOrLower,
        };
        _client.DefaultRequestHeaders.UserAgent.ParseAdd(UserAgent);
    }

    public async Task<int?[]> MeasureAsync(IReadOnlyList<LatencyEndpoint> endpoints, TimeSpan budget, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(endpoints);
        var results = new int?[endpoints.Count];
        var targets = new List<(int Index, Uri Uri)>();
        for (var i = 0; i < endpoints.Count; i++)
        {
            if (endpoints[i].FromServer && endpoints[i].Remote is { } uri)
            {
                targets.Add((i, uri));
            }
        }

        using var deadline = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
        deadline.CancelAfter(budget);
        var options = new ParallelOptions { MaxDegreeOfParallelism = Parallelism, CancellationToken = deadline.Token };
        try
        {
            await Parallel.ForEachAsync(targets, options, async (target, token) =>
            {
                results[target.Index] = await MeasureOneAsync(target.Uri, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (deadline.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
        }

        return results;
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        _client.Dispose();
        _handler.Dispose();
    }

    private async Task<int?> MeasureOneAsync(Uri uri, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (await TimeAsync(uri, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false) is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        int? best = null;
        for (var i = 0; i < Samples; i++)
        {
            if (await TimeAsync(uri, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false) is not { } elapsed)
            {
                break;
            }

            best = best is { } current ? Math.Min(current, elapsed) : elapsed;
        }

        return best;
    }

    private async Task<int?> TimeAsync(Uri uri, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
        timeout.CancelAfter(RequestTimeout);
        try
        {
            using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, uri);
            request.Headers.CacheControl = new CacheControlHeaderValue { NoCache = true, NoStore = true };
            var start = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
            using var response = await _client.SendAsync(request, HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead, timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
            var elapsed = Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(start);
            await response.Content.CopyToAsync(Stream.Null, timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
            return (int)Math.Round(elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero);
        }
        catch (Exception exception) when (exception is HttpRequestException or OperationCanceledException or IOException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

internal sealed record LatencyEndpoint(string Key, string Label, string Url, bool FromServer = true)
{

        Uri.TryCreate(Url, UriKind.Absolute, out var uri) && (uri.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttps || uri.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttp)

using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Latency;

internal enum LatencySide
{
    Browser,
    Server,
}

internal sealed record LatencyBoards(
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> FastJackpots,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> SlowJackpots,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> BrowserFastest,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> BrowserSlowest,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> ServerFastest,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> ServerSlowest,
    IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Runs);

internal sealed class LatencyLeaders
{
    public const int FastBelow = 10;
    public const int SlowFrom = 100;

    private LatencyLeaders(
        BoardMessage[] fastJackpots,
        BoardMessage[] slowJackpots,
        BoardMessage[] browserFastest,
        BoardMessage[] browserSlowest,
        BoardMessage[] serverFastest,
        BoardMessage[] serverSlowest)
    {
        FastJackpots = fastJackpots;
        SlowJackpots = slowJackpots;
        BrowserFastest = browserFastest;
        BrowserSlowest = browserSlowest;
        ServerFastest = serverFastest;
        ServerSlowest = serverSlowest;
    }

    public static LatencyLeaders Empty { get; } = new([], [], [], [], [], []);

    public BoardMessage[] FastJackpots { get; }

    public BoardMessage[] SlowJackpots { get; }

    public BoardMessage[] BrowserFastest { get; }

    public BoardMessage[] BrowserSlowest { get; }

    public BoardMessage[] ServerFastest { get; }

    public BoardMessage[] ServerSlowest { get; }

    private IEnumerable<BoardMessage[]> Lists => [FastJackpots, SlowJackpots, BrowserFastest, BrowserSlowest, ServerFastest, ServerSlowest];

    public static int? Of(LatencyRank rank, LatencySide side) => side == LatencySide.Browser ? rank.Browser : rank.Server;

    public static bool IsFast(int? median) => median < FastBelow;

    public static bool IsSlow(int? median) => median >= SlowFrom;

    public static bool IsFastJackpot(LatencyRank rank) => IsFast(rank.Browser) || IsFast(rank.Server);

    public static bool IsSlowJackpot(LatencyRank rank) => IsSlow(rank.Browser) || IsSlow(rank.Server);

    public LatencyLeaders? Merge(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> candidates, int rankCapacity, int jackpotCapacity)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(candidates);
        var scored = candidates.Select(Score).ToList();
        var next = new LatencyLeaders(
            Window(FastJackpots, scored.Where(entry => IsFastJackpot(entry.Rank)), jackpotCapacity),
            Window(SlowJackpots, scored.Where(entry => IsSlowJackpot(entry.Rank)), jackpotCapacity),
            Order(BrowserFastest, scored, LatencySide.Browser, fastest: true, rankCapacity),
            Order(BrowserSlowest, scored, LatencySide.Browser, fastest: false, rankCapacity),
            Order(ServerFastest, scored, LatencySide.Server, fastest: true, rankCapacity),
            Order(ServerSlowest, scored, LatencySide.Server, fastest: false, rankCapacity));
        return next.Lists.Zip(Lists).All(pair => pair.First.Select(run => run.Id).SequenceEqual(pair.Second.Select(run => run.Id))) ? null : next;
    }

    public IReadOnlyList<Guid> Entered(LatencyLeaders previous)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(previous);
        return [.. Lists.Zip(previous.Lists)
            .SelectMany(pair => pair.First.Select(run => run.Id).Except(pair.Second.Select(run => run.Id)))
            .Distinct()];
    }

    public LatencyBoards Boards(LatencyLeaders? previous)
    {
        var known = previous?.Lists.SelectMany(list => list).Select(run => run.Id).ToHashSet() ?? [];
        return new LatencyBoards(
            Ids(FastJackpots),
            Ids(SlowJackpots),
            Ids(BrowserFastest),
            Ids(BrowserSlowest),
            Ids(ServerFastest),
            Ids(ServerSlowest),
            [.. Lists.SelectMany(list => list).DistinctBy(run => run.Id).Where(run => !known.Contains(run.Id))]);
    }

    private static Guid[] Ids(BoardMessage[] runs) => [.. runs.Select(run => run.Id)];

    private static Scored Score(BoardMessage run) => new(run, LatencyRun.Rank(run.Text));

    private static BoardMessage[] Window(BoardMessage[] current, IEnumerable<Scored> candidates, int capacity) =>
        MessageWindow.Merge(current, candidates.Select(entry => entry.Run), capacity)?.Next ?? current;

    private static BoardMessage[] Order(BoardMessage[] current, IEnumerable<Scored> candidates, LatencySide side, bool fastest, int capacity)
    {
        var pool = current.Select(Score)
            .Concat(candidates)
            .Where(entry => Of(entry.Rank, side) is not null)
            .DistinctBy(entry => entry.Run.Id);
        var sorted = fastest
            ? pool.OrderBy(entry => Of(entry.Rank, side))
            : pool.OrderByDescending(entry => Of(entry.Rank, side));
        return [.. sorted.ThenBy(entry => entry.Run.Id).Take(capacity).Select(entry => entry.Run)];
    }

    private sealed record Scored(BoardMessage Run, LatencyRank Rank);
}

using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

namespace Chaos.Web.Latency;

internal sealed record LatencyResult(string Key, int? Browser, int? Server);

internal sealed record LatencySummary(int? Browser, int? Server, int Answered, int Total);

internal sealed record LatencyRank(int? Browser, int? Server)
{
    public static LatencyRank None { get; } = new(null, null);
}

internal static class LatencyRun
{
    public const int MaxMilliseconds = 60_000;
    public const int MaxEntries = 64;
    public const int MaxKeyLength = 32;
    public const int MaxLength = 4096;

    public static string Compose(IReadOnlyList<LatencyEndpoint> endpoints, IReadOnlyList<int?> browser, IReadOnlyList<int?> server)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(endpoints);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(browser);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(server);
        var results = new List<LatencyResult>(endpoints.Count);
        for (var i = 0; i < endpoints.Count; i++)
        {
            results.Add(new LatencyResult(
                endpoints[i].Key,
                i < browser.Count ? Clamp(browser[i]) : null,
                endpoints[i].FromServer && i < server.Count ? Clamp(server[i]) : null));
        }

        return Format(results);
    }

    public static string Format(IEnumerable<LatencyResult> results)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(results);
        var builder = new StringBuilder();
        foreach (var result in results)
        {
            if (builder.Length > 0)
            {
                builder.Append(';');
            }

            builder.Append(result.Key).Append('=');
            if (result.Browser is { } browser)
            {
                builder.Append(browser.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            }

            builder.Append(',');
            if (result.Server is { } server)
            {
                builder.Append(server.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            }
        }

        return builder.ToString();
    }

    public static bool TryParse([NotNullWhen(true)] string? text, [NotNullWhen(true)] out IReadOnlyList<LatencyResult>? results)
    {
        results = null;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || text.Length > MaxLength)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var entries = text.Split(';');
        if (entries.Length > MaxEntries)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var parsed = new List<LatencyResult>(entries.Length);
        var keys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        foreach (var entry in entries)
        {
            var equals = entry.IndexOf('=', StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (equals <= 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var key = entry[..equals];
            var values = entry[(equals + 1)..].Split(',');
            if (!IsKey(key) || !keys.Add(key) || values.Length != 2
                || !TryValue(values[0], out var browser) || !TryValue(values[1], out var server))
            {
                return false;
            }

            parsed.Add(new LatencyResult(key, browser, server));
        }

        results = parsed;
        return true;
    }

    public static bool IsValid([NotNullWhen(true)] string? text) =>
        TryParse(text, out var results) && string.Equals(Format(results), text, StringComparison.Ordinal);

    public static LatencySummary Summarize(IReadOnlyList<LatencyResult> results)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(results);
        return new LatencySummary(
            Median(results.Select(result => result.Browser)),
            Median(results.Select(result => result.Server)),
            results.Count(result => result.Browser is not null || result.Server is not null),
            results.Count);
    }

    public static LatencyRank Rank(IReadOnlyList<LatencyResult> results)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(results);
        var quorum = (results.Count + 1) / 2;
        return new LatencyRank(
            Counted([.. results.Select(result => result.Browser)], quorum),
            Counted([.. results.Select(result => result.Server)], quorum));
    }

    public static LatencyRank Rank(string? text) => TryParse(text, out var results) ? Rank(results) : LatencyRank.None;

    public static int? Median(IEnumerable<int?> values)
    {
        var sorted = values.OfType<int>().Order().ToArray();
        if (sorted.Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var middle = sorted.Length / 2;
        return sorted.Length % 2 == 1 ? sorted[middle] : (int)Math.Round((sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2.0, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero);
    }

    public static string Milliseconds(int? value) =>
        value is { } milliseconds ? milliseconds.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " ms" : "–";

    private static int? Counted(int?[] values, int quorum) =>
        values.Count(value => value is not null) >= quorum ? Median(values) : null;

    private static int? Clamp(int? value) => value is { } milliseconds and >= 0 ? Math.Min(milliseconds, MaxMilliseconds) : null;

    private static bool IsKey(string key)
    {
        if (key.Length > MaxKeyLength)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (var c in key)
        {
            if (!char.IsAsciiLetterLower(c) && !char.IsAsciiDigit(c) && c != '-')
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private static bool TryValue(string value, out int? milliseconds)
    {
        milliseconds = null;
        if (value.Length == 0)
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (value.Length > 5 || !value.All(char.IsAsciiDigit)
            || !int.TryParse(value, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var parsed) || parsed > MaxMilliseconds)
        {
            return false;
        }

        milliseconds = parsed;
        return true;
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Latency;

internal sealed partial class LatencyService : IDisposable
{
    public const int MaxOffers = 50;
    public const int MaxLeaderOffers = 40;

    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private readonly ILatencyProbe _probe;
    private readonly ILogger<LatencyService> _logger;
    private readonly KeyedCooldowns _cooldowns;
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stopping = new();
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly HashSet<ILatencySubscriber> _subscribers = [];
    private readonly bool _serverProbe;
    private readonly TimeSpan _gap;
    private BoardMessage[] _messages = [];
    private LatencyLeaders _leaders = LatencyLeaders.Empty;
    private long _version;
    private Pending? _pending;
    private long? _lastStart;
    private Task _idle = Task.CompletedTask;

    public LatencyService(TimeProvider time, IOptions<ChaosOptions> options, ILatencyProbe probe, ILogger<LatencyService> logger)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(options);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(probe);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(logger);
        var limits = options.Value;
        _time = time;
        _probe = probe;
        _logger = logger;
        Capacity = limits.LatencyCapacity;
        RankCapacity = limits.LatencyRankCapacity;
        JackpotCapacity = limits.LatencyJackpotCapacity;
        Cooldown = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(limits.LatencyCooldownSeconds);
        Budget = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(limits.LatencyBudgetSeconds);
        BrowserWait = Budget + TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15);
        _gap = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(limits.LatencyGapSeconds);
        _serverProbe = limits.LatencyServerProbe;
        _cooldowns = new KeyedCooldowns(Cooldown, time);
        StartedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public int Capacity { get; }

    public int RankCapacity { get; }

    public int JackpotCapacity { get; }

    public TimeSpan Cooldown { get; }

    public TimeSpan Budget { get; }

    public TimeSpan BrowserWait { get; }

    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; }

    public IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Messages => Volatile.Read(ref _messages);

    public LatencyLeaders Leaders => Volatile.Read(ref _leaders);

    public bool Running
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _pending is not null;
            }
        }
    }

    public Task WhenIdle()
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _idle;
        }
    }

    public LatencyStart Begin(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var now = _time.GetTimestamp();
            if (_pending is not null || (_lastStart is { } last && _time.GetElapsedTime(last, now) < _gap))
            {
                return LatencyStart.Busy;
            }

            if (!_cooldowns.TryStart(client, out var remaining))
            {
                return LatencyStart.Wait(Seconds(remaining));
            }

            _lastStart = now;
            var pending = new Pending(Uuid7.After(LatestId(), _time.GetUtcNow()));
            _pending = pending;
            Broadcast(LatencyUpdate.State(_version, true));
            _idle = RunAsync(pending);
            return LatencyStart.Started(pending.Id, Seconds(Cooldown));
        }
    }

    public bool Finish(Guid id, IReadOnlyList<int>? browser)
    {
        if (browser is null || browser.Count != LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Count)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (var value in browser)
        {
            if (value < -1 || value > LatencyRun.MaxMilliseconds)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _pending is { } pending && pending.Id == id && pending.Browser.TrySetResult([.. browser.Select(value => value < 0 ? (int?)null : value)]);
        }
    }

    public int Offer(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered) => Restore(offered, MaxOffers, recent: true);

    public int OfferLeaders(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered) => Restore(offered, MaxLeaderOffers, recent: false);

    public void Subscribe(ILatencySubscriber subscriber)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(subscriber);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (subscriber.Deliver(LatencyUpdate.Snapshot(_version, _messages, _pending is not null, _leaders.Boards(null))))
            {
                _subscribers.Add(subscriber);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Unsubscribe(ILatencySubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _subscribers.Remove(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public bool IsSubscribed(ILatencySubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _subscribers.Contains(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        _stopping.Cancel();
        _stopping.Dispose();
    }

    private static int Seconds(TimeSpan span) => Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(span.TotalSeconds));

    private int Restore(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered, int limit, bool recent)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(offered);
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var valid = offered
            .Take(limit)
            .Where(message => message is not null && MessageWindow.IsRestorable(message.Id, now, StartedAt) && LatencyRun.IsValid(message.Text))
            .ToList();
        if (valid.Count == 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            return Merge(valid, recent);
        }
    }

    private Guid LatestId() => _messages.Length > 0 ? _messages[0].Id : Guid.Empty;

    private async Task RunAsync(Pending pending)
    {
        var endpoints = LatencyCatalog.Endpoints;
        var token = _stopping.Token;
        var server = new int?[endpoints.Count];
        IReadOnlyList<int?> browser = [];
        try
        {
            await Task.Yield();
            if (_serverProbe)
            {
                server = await _probe.MeasureAsync(endpoints, Budget, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }

            browser = await pending.Browser.Task.WaitAsync(BrowserWait, _time, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (TimeoutException)
        {
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (token.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            return;
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            LogProbeFailed(_logger, exception);
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            _pending = null;
            var message = new BoardMessage(pending.Id, LatencyRun.Compose(endpoints, browser, server));
            if (Merge([message], recent: true) == 0)
            {
                Broadcast(LatencyUpdate.State(_version, false));
            }
        }
    }

    private int Merge(IReadOnlyCollection<BoardMessage> candidates, bool recent)
    {
        var change = recent ? MessageWindow.Merge(_messages, candidates, Capacity) : null;
        var leaders = _leaders.Merge(candidates, RankCapacity, JackpotCapacity);
        if (change is null && leaders is null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        var previous = _version;
        _version++;
        if (change is not null)
        {
            Volatile.Write(ref _messages, change.Next);
        }

        LatencyBoards? boards = null;
        IReadOnlyList<Guid> entered = [];
        if (leaders is not null)
        {
            boards = leaders.Boards(_leaders);
            entered = leaders.Entered(_leaders);
            Volatile.Write(ref _leaders, leaders);
        }

        Broadcast(LatencyUpdate.Delta(_version, previous, change?.Added ?? [], change?.Removed ?? [], _pending is not null, boards));
        return (change?.Added ?? []).Select(message => message.Id).Concat(entered).Distinct().Count();
    }

    private void Broadcast(LatencyUpdate update)
    {
        List<ILatencySubscriber>? dropped = null;
        foreach (var subscriber in _subscribers)
        {
            if (!subscriber.Deliver(update))
            {
                (dropped ??= []).Add(subscriber);
            }
        }

        if (dropped is not null)
        {
            _subscribers.ExceptWith(dropped);
        }
    }

    [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Warning, Message = "Server latency probe failed")]
    private static partial void LogProbeFailed(ILogger logger, Exception exception);

    private sealed class Pending(Guid id)
    {
        public Guid Id { get; } = id;

        public TaskCompletionSource<IReadOnlyList<int?>> Browser { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
    }
}

using System.Globalization;

namespace Chaos.Web.Latency;

internal enum StartResult
{
    Accepted,
    Busy,
    Wait,
}

internal sealed record LatencyStart(StartResult Result, Guid Id, int Seconds)
{
    public static LatencyStart Busy { get; } = new(StartResult.Busy, Guid.Empty, 0);

    public static LatencyStart Started(Guid id, int cooldown) => new(StartResult.Accepted, id, cooldown);

    public static LatencyStart Wait(int seconds) => new(StartResult.Wait, Guid.Empty, seconds);

    public string Reply => Result switch
    {
        StartResult.Accepted => string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"ok {Id:D} {Seconds}"),
        StartResult.Busy => "busy",
        _ => string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"wait {Seconds}"),
    };
}
