using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal sealed class BoardService
{
    public const int MaxOffers = 50;
    public const int RepeatWindow = 10;

    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private readonly KeyedTokenBuckets _posts;
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly HashSet<IBoardSubscriber> _subscribers = [];
    private BoardMessage[] _messages = [];
    private long _version;

    public BoardService(TimeProvider time, IOptions<ChaosOptions> options)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(options);
        var limits = options.Value;
        _time = time;
        Capacity = limits.BoardCapacity;
        _posts = new KeyedTokenBuckets(limits.PostBurst, limits.PostsPerSecond, time);
        StartedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public int Capacity { get; }

    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; }

    public IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Messages => Volatile.Read(ref _messages);

    public long Version
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _version;
            }
        }
    }

    public PostResult Post(string? input, string client) => Submit(input, client, null).Result;

    public PostReply Submit(string? input, string client, IEnumerable<MetaEntry?>? meta)
    {
        var text = MessageText.Normalize(input);
        if (text.Length == 0)
        {
            return new PostReply(PostResult.Empty);
        }

        if (!_posts.TryTake(client))
        {
            return new PostReply(PostResult.TooFast);
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            var window = Math.Min(RepeatWindow, _messages.Length);
            for (var i = 0; i < window; i++)
            {
                if (string.Equals(_messages[i].Text, text, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    return new PostReply(PostResult.Repeat);
                }
            }

            var message = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(_time.GetUtcNow()), text, MessageMeta.Normalize(meta));
            Merge([message]);
            return new PostReply(PostResult.Accepted, message);
        }
    }

    public int Offer(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(offered);
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var valid = new List<BoardMessage>();
        foreach (var message in offered.Take(MaxOffers))
        {
            if (IsAcceptable(message, now))
            {
                valid.Add(message);
            }
        }

        if (valid.Count == 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            return Merge(valid);
        }
    }

    public void Subscribe(IBoardSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(subscriber);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (subscriber.Deliver(BoardUpdate.Snapshot(_version, _messages)))
            {
                _subscribers.Add(subscriber);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Unsubscribe(IBoardSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _subscribers.Remove(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public bool IsSubscribed(IBoardSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _subscribers.Contains(subscriber);
        }
    }

    private bool IsAcceptable(BoardMessage message, DateTimeOffset now) =>
        message is not null
        && MessageWindow.IsRestorable(message.Id, now, StartedAt)
        && MessageText.IsNormalized(message.Text)
        && MessageMeta.IsNormalized(message.Meta);

    private int Merge(IReadOnlyCollection<BoardMessage> candidates)
    {
        var change = MessageWindow.Merge(_messages, candidates, Capacity);
        if (change is null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        var previous = _version;
        _version++;
        Volatile.Write(ref _messages, change.Next);
        Broadcast(BoardUpdate.Delta(_version, previous, change.Added, change.Removed));
        return change.Added.Length;
    }

    private void Broadcast(BoardUpdate update)
    {
        List<IBoardSubscriber>? dropped = null;
        foreach (var subscriber in _subscribers)
        {
            if (!subscriber.Deliver(update))
            {
                (dropped ??= []).Add(subscriber);
            }
        }

        if (dropped is not null)
        {
            _subscribers.ExceptWith(dropped);
        }
    }
}

using System.Globalization;

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal static class MessageFormat
{
    public static string Iso(Guid id) =>
        Uuid7.Timestamp(id).UtcDateTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.fff'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    public static string Label(Guid id, DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        var at = Uuid7.Timestamp(id).UtcDateTime;
        return at.Date == now.UtcDateTime.Date
            ? at.ToString("HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
            : at.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE:     src/Chaos.Web/Messages/MessageMeta.cs

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal static class MessageMeta
{
    public const int MaxValueLength = 120;

    public static IReadOnlyList<MetaField> Fields { get; } =
    [
        new("latitude", "Latitude", 32, -90, 90),
        new("longitude", "Longitude", 32, -180, 180),
        new("accuracy", "Accuracy (m)", 32, 0, 1e8),
        new("altitude", "Altitude (m)", 32, -1e5, 1e6),
        new("altitudeAccuracy", "Altitude accuracy (m)", 32, 0, 1e8),
        new("heading", "Heading (°)", 32, 0, 360),
        new("speed", "Speed (m/s)", 32, 0, 1e5),
        new("positionAt", "Position time", 40),
        new("timeZone", "Time zone", 64),
        new("utcOffset", "UTC offset", 8),
        new("localTime", "Local time", 40),
        new("locale", "Locale", 64),
        new("calendar", "Calendar", 32),
        new("numberingSystem", "Numbering system", 32),
        new("language", "Language", 64),
        new("languages", "Languages", 200),
        new("userAgent", "User agent", 512),
        new("brands", "Brands", 200),
        new("platform", "Platform", 64),
        new("mobile", "Mobile", 8),
        new("vendor", "Vendor", 64),
        new("webdriver", "Automated", 8),
        new("doNotTrack", "Do Not Track", 16),
        new("globalPrivacyControl", "Global Privacy Control", 8),
        new("pdfViewer", "PDF viewer", 8),
        new("screen", "Screen", 24),
        new("availableScreen", "Available screen", 24),
        new("viewport", "Viewport", 24),
        new("pixelRatio", "Pixel ratio", 16, 0, 100),
        new("colorDepth", "Color depth", 8, 0, 128),
        new("orientation", "Orientation", 32),
        new("cores", "CPU threads", 8, 0, 4096),
        new("memory", "Memory (GB)", 8, 0, 4096),
        new("touchPoints", "Touch points", 8, 0, 1024),
        new("pointer", "Pointer", 16),
        new("colorScheme", "Color scheme", 16),
        new("reducedMotion", "Reduced motion", 8),
        new("contrast", "Contrast", 16),
        new("online", "Online", 8),
        new("connection", "Connection", 32),
        new("downlink", "Downlink (Mb/s)", 16, 0, 1e6),
        new("rtt", "Round trip (ms)", 16, 0, 1e6),
        new("saveData", "Data saver", 8),
    ];

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, int> Order = Fields
        .Select((field, index) => (field.Key, index))
        .ToDictionary(pair => pair.Key, pair => pair.index, StringComparer.Ordinal);

    public static string LabelOf(string key) =>
        Order.TryGetValue(key, out var index) ? Fields[index].Label : key;

    public static MetaEntry[]? Normalize(IEnumerable<MetaEntry?>? entries)
    {
        if (entries is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var values = new string?[Fields.Count];
        foreach (var entry in entries.Take(Fields.Count * 2))
        {
            if (entry?.Key is not { } key || !Order.TryGetValue(key, out var index) || values[index] is not null)
            {
                continue;
            }

            values[index] = Clean(Fields[index], entry.Value);
        }

        if (values[0] is null || values[1] is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var normalized = new List<MetaEntry>(Fields.Count);
        for (var i = 0; i < Fields.Count; i++)
        {
            if (values[i] is { } value)
            {
                normalized.Add(new MetaEntry(Fields[i].Key, value));
            }
        }

        return [.. normalized];
    }

    public static bool IsNormalized(IReadOnlyList<MetaEntry>? entries)
    {
        if (entries is null)
        {
            return true;
        }

        var normalized = Normalize(entries);
        return normalized is not null && normalized.SequenceEqual(entries);
    }

    private static string? Clean(MetaField field, string? input)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var builder = new StringBuilder(Math.Min(input.Length, field.MaxLength));
        foreach (var c in input)
        {
            if (!char.IsControl(c))
            {
                builder.Append(c);
            }
        }

        var text = builder.ToString().Trim();
        if (text.Length > field.MaxLength)
        {
            var cut = field.MaxLength;
            if (char.IsHighSurrogate(text[cut - 1]))
            {
                cut--;
            }

            text = text[..cut].TrimEnd();
        }

        if (text.Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        if (!field.IsNumber)
        {
            return text;
        }

        return double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var number)
            && double.IsFinite(number)
            && number >= (field.Min ?? double.MinValue)
            && number <= (field.Max ?? double.MaxValue)
            ? text
            : null;
    }
}

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal static partial class MessageText
{
    public const int MaxLength = 500;

    public static string Normalize(string? input)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
        {
            return "";
        }

        var text = string.Concat(input.ReplaceLineEndings("\n").Where(c => c == '\n' || !char.IsControl(c)));
        text = ExtraBlankLines().Replace(text, "\n\n").Trim();

        if (text.Length > MaxLength)
        {
            var cut = 0;
            while (cut < text.Length)
            {
                var next = StringInfo.GetNextTextElementLength(text, cut);
                if (cut + next > MaxLength)
                {
                    break;
                }

                cut += next;
            }

            text = text[..cut].TrimEnd();
        }

        return text.Any(IsVisible) ? text : "";
    }

    public static bool IsNormalized(string? text) =>
        !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && string.Equals(Normalize(text), text, StringComparison.Ordinal);

    private static bool IsVisible(char c) =>
        !char.IsWhiteSpace(c) && char.GetUnicodeCategory(c) != UnicodeCategory.Format;

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\n{3,}")]
    private static partial Regex ExtraBlankLines();
}

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal sealed record WindowChange(BoardMessage[] Next, BoardMessage[] Added, Guid[] Removed);

internal static class MessageWindow
{
    public static WindowChange? Merge(BoardMessage[] current, IEnumerable<BoardMessage> candidates, int capacity)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(current);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(candidates);
        var known = new HashSet<Guid>(current.Length);
        foreach (var message in current)
        {
            known.Add(message.Id);
        }

        var next = current
            .Concat(candidates.Where(message => !known.Contains(message.Id)).DistinctBy(message => message.Id))
            .OrderByDescending(message => message.Id)
            .Take(capacity)
            .ToArray();
        var added = next.Where(message => !known.Contains(message.Id)).ToArray();
        if (added.Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var kept = next.Select(message => message.Id).ToHashSet();
        var removed = current.Where(message => !kept.Contains(message.Id)).Select(message => message.Id).ToArray();
        return new WindowChange(next, added, removed);
    }

    public static bool IsRestorable(Guid id, DateTimeOffset now, DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        if (!Uuid7.IsValid(id))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var at = Uuid7.Timestamp(id);
        return at <= now && at < startedAt;
    }
}

namespace Chaos.Web.Messages;

internal static class Uuid7
{
    public static bool IsValid(Guid id) => id.Version == 7 && (id.Variant & 0b1100) == 0b1000;

    public static long UnixMilliseconds(Guid id)
    {
        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        id.TryWriteBytes(bytes, bigEndian: true, out _);
        long milliseconds = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            milliseconds = (milliseconds << 8) | bytes[i];
        }

        return milliseconds;
    }

    public static DateTimeOffset Timestamp(Guid id) => DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(UnixMilliseconds(id));

    public static Guid After(Guid previous, DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        var candidate = Guid.CreateVersion7(now);
        if (candidate.CompareTo(previous) > 0 || !IsValid(previous))
        {
            return candidate;
        }

        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        previous.TryWriteBytes(bytes, bigEndian: true, out _);
        for (var i = 15; i >= 9; i--)
        {
            if (++bytes[i] != 0)
            {
                return new Guid(bytes, bigEndian: true);
            }
        }

        if ((bytes[8] & 0b0011_1111) != 0b0011_1111)
        {
            bytes[8]++;
            return new Guid(bytes, bigEndian: true);
        }

        return candidate;
    }
}

    .AddInteractiveServerComponents(circuit =>

        circuit.DisconnectedCircuitMaxRetained = 20;
        circuit.DisconnectedCircuitRetentionPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1);

        circuit.PersistedCircuitInMemoryRetentionPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);

});

internal sealed record SlotPress(PressResult Result, Guid Id)
{

using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;
using System.Security.Cryptography;

namespace Chaos.Web.Slots;

internal static class SlotRules
{
    public const int Length = 5;
    public const string Symbols = "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";

    public static string Draw() => Draw(Symbols);

    public static string Draw(string alphabet) => RandomNumberGenerator.GetString(alphabet, Length);

    public static bool IsAlphabet([NotNullWhen(true)] string? alphabet) =>
        !string.IsNullOrEmpty(alphabet) && alphabet.Length <= Symbols.Length && alphabet.All(c => Symbols.Contains(c, StringComparison.Ordinal));

    public static bool IsJackpot(SlotWin win) => win is SlotWin.FourOfAKind or SlotWin.FiveOfAKind or SlotWin.SequenceUp or SlotWin.SequenceDown;

    public static bool IsJackpot(string? text) => IsJackpot(Classify(text));

    public static bool IsValid([NotNullWhen(true)] string? text)
    {
        if (text is null || text.Length != Length)
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (var c in text)
        {
            if (!char.IsAsciiDigit(c) && !char.IsAsciiLetterUpper(c))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    public static SlotWin Classify(string? text)
    {
        if (!IsValid(text))
        {
            return SlotWin.None;
        }

        var most = text.GroupBy(c => c).Max(group => group.Count());
        if (most >= 5)
        {
            return SlotWin.FiveOfAKind;
        }

        if (most == 4)
        {
            return SlotWin.FourOfAKind;
        }

        if (most == 3)
        {
            return SlotWin.ThreeOfAKind;
        }

        if (IsConsecutive(text, 1))
        {
            return SlotWin.SequenceUp;
        }

        if (IsConsecutive(text, -1))
        {
            return SlotWin.SequenceDown;
        }

        if (IsOrdered(text, 1))
        {
            return SlotWin.StraightUp;
        }

        return IsOrdered(text, -1) ? SlotWin.StraightDown : SlotWin.None;
    }

    public static bool[] Hits(string? text)
    {
        var hits = new bool[Length];
        if (!IsValid(text))
        {
            return hits;
        }

        var win = Classify(text);
        if (win is SlotWin.None)
        {
            return hits;
        }

        if (win is SlotWin.StraightUp or SlotWin.StraightDown or SlotWin.SequenceUp or SlotWin.SequenceDown)
        {
            Array.Fill(hits, true);
            return hits;
        }

        var top = text.GroupBy(c => c).MaxBy(group => group.Count())!.Key;
        for (var i = 0; i < Length; i++)
        {
            hits[i] = text[i] == top;
        }

        return hits;
    }

    public static string Label(SlotWin win) => win switch
    {
        SlotWin.ThreeOfAKind => "Three of a kind",
        SlotWin.FourOfAKind => "Four of a kind",
        SlotWin.FiveOfAKind => "Five of a kind",
        SlotWin.StraightUp => "Straight up",
        SlotWin.StraightDown => "Straight down",
        SlotWin.SequenceUp => "Sequence up",
        SlotWin.SequenceDown => "Sequence down",
        _ => "",
    };

    public static char Above(char symbol) => Symbols[(Symbols.IndexOf(symbol, StringComparison.Ordinal) + 1) % Symbols.Length];

    public static char Below(char symbol) => Symbols[(Symbols.IndexOf(symbol, StringComparison.Ordinal) + Symbols.Length - 1) % Symbols.Length];

    private static bool IsConsecutive(string text, int direction)
    {
        for (var i = 1; i < text.Length; i++)
        {
            if (Symbols.IndexOf(text[i], StringComparison.Ordinal) - Symbols.IndexOf(text[i - 1], StringComparison.Ordinal) != direction)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    private static bool IsOrdered(string text, int direction)
    {
        for (var i = 1; i < text.Length; i++)
        {
            if ((text[i] - text[i - 1]) * direction <= 0)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Slots;

internal sealed class SlotService
{
    public const int MaxOffers = 50;
    public const int MaxWinnerOffers = 500;
    public const int MaxJackpotOffers = 500;

    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private readonly KeyedTokenBuckets _presses;
    private readonly string _alphabet;
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly HashSet<ISlotSubscriber> _subscribers = [];
    private BoardMessage[] _messages = [];
    private BoardMessage[] _winners = [];
    private BoardMessage[] _jackpots = [];
    private long _version;
    private Guid _spinId;
    private long? _spinStarted;

    public SlotService(TimeProvider time, IOptions<ChaosOptions> options)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(options);
        var limits = options.Value;
        _time = time;
        Capacity = limits.SlotCapacity;
        WinnerCapacity = limits.SlotWinnerCapacity;
        JackpotCapacity = limits.SlotJackpotCapacity;
        _alphabet = SlotRules.IsAlphabet(limits.SlotDrawSymbols) ? limits.SlotDrawSymbols : SlotRules.Symbols;
        SpinTime = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(limits.SpinMilliseconds);
        _presses = new KeyedTokenBuckets(limits.PressBurst, limits.PressesPerSecond, time);
        StartedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public int Capacity { get; }

    public int WinnerCapacity { get; }

    public int JackpotCapacity { get; }

    public TimeSpan SpinTime { get; }

    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; }

    public IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Messages => Volatile.Read(ref _messages);

    public IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Winners => Volatile.Read(ref _winners);

    public IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Jackpots => Volatile.Read(ref _jackpots);

    public SlotSpin? Spinning
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return CurrentSpin(_time.GetTimestamp());
            }
        }
    }

    public SlotPress Press(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var now = _time.GetTimestamp();
            if (CurrentSpin(now) is not null)
            {
                return SlotPress.Busy;
            }

            if (!_presses.TryTake(client))
            {
                return SlotPress.TooFast;
            }

            var message = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(_time.GetUtcNow()), SlotRules.Draw(_alphabet));
            _spinId = message.Id;
            _spinStarted = now;
            Merge([message], new SlotSpin(message.Id, Milliseconds(SpinTime)), Lists.All, drawn: true);
            return SlotPress.Started(message.Id);
        }
    }

    public int Offer(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered) => Restore(offered, MaxOffers, Lists.All);

    public int OfferWinners(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered) => Restore(offered, MaxWinnerOffers, Lists.Winners | Lists.Jackpots);

    public int OfferJackpots(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered) => Restore(offered, MaxJackpotOffers, Lists.Jackpots);

    public void Subscribe(ISlotSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(subscriber);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (subscriber.Deliver(SlotUpdate.Snapshot(_version, _messages, _winners, _jackpots, CurrentSpin(_time.GetTimestamp()))))
            {
                _subscribers.Add(subscriber);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Unsubscribe(ISlotSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _subscribers.Remove(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public bool IsSubscribed(ISlotSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _subscribers.Contains(subscriber);
        }
    }

    private static int Milliseconds(TimeSpan span) => (int)Math.Ceiling(span.TotalMilliseconds);

    private static bool IsWinner(BoardMessage message) => SlotRules.Classify(message.Text) is not SlotWin.None;

    private static bool IsJackpot(BoardMessage message) => SlotRules.IsJackpot(message.Text);

    private static bool Accepts(BoardMessage message, Lists lists) =>
        lists.HasFlag(Lists.Recent)
        || (lists.HasFlag(Lists.Winners) && IsWinner(message))
        || (lists.HasFlag(Lists.Jackpots) && IsJackpot(message));

    private int Restore(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> offered, int limit, Lists lists)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(offered);
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var valid = offered
            .Take(limit)
            .Where(message => message is not null && MessageWindow.IsRestorable(message.Id, now, StartedAt) && SlotRules.IsValid(message.Text))
            .Where(message => Accepts(message, lists))
            .ToList();
        if (valid.Count == 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            return Merge(valid, null, lists, drawn: false);
        }
    }

    private SlotSpin? CurrentSpin(long now)
    {
        if (_spinStarted is not { } started)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var remaining = SpinTime - _time.GetElapsedTime(started, now);
        return remaining > TimeSpan.Zero ? new SlotSpin(_spinId, Milliseconds(remaining)) : null;
    }

    private WindowChange? Enshrine(IEnumerable<BoardMessage> candidates, bool drawn)
    {
        var known = _jackpots.Select(message => message.Id).ToHashSet();
        var fresh = candidates
            .Where(message => IsJackpot(message) && !known.Contains(message.Id))
            .DistinctBy(message => message.Id)
            .Take(drawn ? int.MaxValue : Math.Max(0, JackpotCapacity - _jackpots.Length))
            .ToList();
        return fresh.Count == 0 ? null : MessageWindow.Merge(_jackpots, fresh, int.MaxValue);
    }

    private int Merge(IReadOnlyCollection<BoardMessage> candidates, SlotSpin? spin, Lists lists, bool drawn)
    {
        var change = lists.HasFlag(Lists.Recent) ? MessageWindow.Merge(_messages, candidates, Capacity) : null;
        var crowned = lists.HasFlag(Lists.Winners) ? MessageWindow.Merge(_winners, candidates.Where(IsWinner), WinnerCapacity) : null;
        var enshrined = lists.HasFlag(Lists.Jackpots) ? Enshrine(candidates, drawn) : null;
        if (change is null && crowned is null && enshrined is null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        var previous = _version;
        _version++;
        if (change is not null)
        {
            Volatile.Write(ref _messages, change.Next);
        }

        if (crowned is not null)
        {
            Volatile.Write(ref _winners, crowned.Next);
        }

        if (enshrined is not null)
        {
            Volatile.Write(ref _jackpots, enshrined.Next);
        }

        Broadcast(SlotUpdate.Delta(
            _version,
            previous,
            change?.Added ?? [],
            change?.Removed ?? [],
            spin,
            crowned?.Added ?? [],
            crowned?.Removed ?? [],
            enshrined?.Added ?? []));
        return (change?.Added ?? [])
            .Concat(crowned?.Added ?? [])
            .Concat(enshrined?.Added ?? [])
            .Select(message => message.Id)
            .Distinct()
            .Count();
    }

    private void Broadcast(SlotUpdate update)
    {
        List<ISlotSubscriber>? dropped = null;
        foreach (var subscriber in _subscribers)
        {
            if (!subscriber.Deliver(update))
            {
                (dropped ??= []).Add(subscriber);
            }
        }

        if (dropped is not null)
        {
            _subscribers.ExceptWith(dropped);
        }
    }

    [Flags]
    private enum Lists
    {
        None = 0,
        Recent = 1,
        Winners = 2,
        Jackpots = 4,
        All = Recent | Winners | Jackpots,
    }
}

using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Slots;

internal sealed record SlotSpin(Guid Id, int Remaining);

internal sealed record SlotUpdate(
    string Kind,
    long Version,
    long Previous,
    IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> Added,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> Removed,
    [property: JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)] SlotSpin? Spin,
    IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> WinnersAdded,
    IReadOnlyList<Guid> WinnersRemoved,
    IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> JackpotsAdded)
{
    public static SlotUpdate Snapshot(
        long version,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> messages,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> winners,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> jackpots,
        SlotSpin? spin) =>
        new("snapshot", version, version, messages, [], spin, winners, [], jackpots);

    public static SlotUpdate Delta(
        long version,
        long previous,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> added,
        IReadOnlyList<Guid> removed,
        SlotSpin? spin,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> winnersAdded,
        IReadOnlyList<Guid> winnersRemoved,
        IReadOnlyList<BoardMessage> jackpotsAdded) =>
        new("delta", version, previous, added, removed, spin, winnersAdded, winnersRemoved, jackpotsAdded);
}

using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed partial class BoardTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    private const string OldId = "016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001";

    private const string StoredTexts = """
        () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-board');
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                const all = db.transaction('messages', 'readonly').objectStore('messages').getAll();
                all.onsuccess = () => { db.close(); resolve(all.result.map(m => m.text)); };
                all.onerror = () => reject(all.error);
            };
            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        })
        """;

    private const string SeedMessage = """
        ([id, text]) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-board', 1);
            request.onupgradeneeded = () => request.result.createObjectStore('messages', { keyPath: 'id' });
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                const transaction = db.transaction('messages', 'readwrite');
                transaction.objectStore('messages').put({ id, text });
                transaction.oncomplete = () => { db.close(); resolve(); };
                transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
            };
            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        })
        """;

    private const string StoredMine = """
        text => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-board');
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                const all = db.transaction('messages', 'readonly').objectStore('messages').getAll();
                all.onsuccess = () => {
                    db.close();
                    const record = all.result.find(m => m.text === text);
                    resolve(record?.mine === true && Array.isArray(record.meta) && record.meta.some(e => e.key === 'latitude'));
                };
                all.onerror = () => reject(all.error);
            };
            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        })
        """;

    [Fact]
    public async Task MessagesReachOtherBrowsersAndTheirLocalHistory()
    {
        await using var first = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var second = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var writer = await OpenBoardAsync(first);
        var reader = await OpenBoardAsync(second);
        var text = $"hello {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await PostAsync(writer, text);

        var item = reader.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(item).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(item).Not.ToHaveClassAsync(Archived());
        await EventuallyAsync(async () => (await reader.EvaluateAsync<string[]>(StoredTexts)).Contains(text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ControlEnterSends()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await OpenBoardAsync(context);
        var text = $"keyboard {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await page.Locator("#compose-text").FillAsync(text);
        await page.Locator("#compose-text").PressAsync("Control+Enter");

        await Expect(page.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-text")).ToHaveValueAsync("");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheCharacterCountFollowsTypingPastingAndSending()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await OpenBoardAsync(context);
        var count = page.Locator("#compose-count");
        await Expect(count).ToHaveTextAsync("0 / 500");

        await page.Locator("#compose-text").PressSequentiallyAsync("abc");
        await Expect(count).ToHaveTextAsync("3 / 500");

        var text = $"counted {Guid.NewGuid():N}";
        await page.Locator("#compose-text").FillAsync(text);
        await Expect(count).ToHaveTextAsync($"{text.Length} / 500");

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Send" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-text")).ToHaveValueAsync("");
        await Expect(count).ToHaveTextAsync("0 / 500");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task NewMessagesNeverLeakIntoOtherPages()
    {
        await using var readerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var writerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var reader = await OpenBoardAsync(readerContext);
        var writer = await OpenBoardAsync(writerContext);

        await reader.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation).GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "Canvas" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(reader.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Reset canvas" })).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        var text = $"leak {Guid.NewGuid():N}";
        await PostAsync(writer, text);
        await reader.WaitForTimeoutAsync(1000);

        await Expect(reader.Locator("main")).Not.ToContainTextAsync(text);
        await Expect(reader.Locator("main .note")).ToHaveTextAsync("Drawing needs a pointer: a mouse, a finger or a stylus. There is no way to draw with a keyboard, but the reset button works from one.");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task MessagesTheServerForgotHaveADottedBorder()
    {
        await using var seededContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var seeded = await seededContext.NewPageAsync();
        await seeded.GotoAsync("/about");
        await seeded.EvaluateAsync(SeedMessage, new[] { OldId, "posted in 2020" });

        await using var otherContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var poster = await OpenBoardAsync(otherContext);
        for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            await PostAsync(poster, $"filler {i} {Guid.NewGuid():N}");
        }

        await seeded.GotoAsync("/board");
        var old = seeded.Locator($"#board-feed .msg[data-id='{OldId}']");
        await Expect(old).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(old).ToHaveClassAsync(Archived());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task LocationDetailsTravelWithMessagesWhenPermitted()
    {
        await using var locatedContext = await browser.NewLocatedContextAsync(27.7172f, 85.324f);
        await using var readerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var poster = await OpenBoardAsync(locatedContext);
        var reader = await OpenBoardAsync(readerContext);
        await Expect(poster.Locator("#compose-share")).ToBeCheckedAsync();
        var text = $"located {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await PostAsync(poster, text);

        var mine = poster.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(mine.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your message");
        var theirs = reader.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(theirs.Locator("details.meta")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(theirs.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        var latitude = theirs.Locator("details.meta dt", new() { HasText = "Latitude" });
        await Expect(latitude).ToBeHiddenAsync();
        await theirs.Locator("details.meta summary").ClickAsync();
        await Expect(latitude).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(theirs.Locator("details.meta dd").First).ToHaveTextAsync(Kathmandu());
        await Expect(theirs.Locator("details.meta dt", new() { HasText = "Time zone" })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await EventuallyAsync(async () => (await poster.EvaluateAsync<bool>(StoredMine, text)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UncheckingStopsDetailsButKeepsYourMessage()
    {
        await using var locatedContext = await browser.NewLocatedContextAsync(40.7128f, -74.006f);
        var poster = await OpenBoardAsync(locatedContext);
        await poster.Locator("#compose-share").UncheckAsync();
        var text = $"unlocated {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await PostAsync(poster, text);

        var item = poster.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(item.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your message");
        await Expect(item.Locator("details.meta")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);

        await poster.ReloadAsync();
        await Expect(poster.Locator("#compose-text")).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        await Expect(poster.Locator("#compose-share")).Not.ToBeCheckedAsync();
        await Expect(poster.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text }).Locator(".yours")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your message");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task WithoutPermissionTheBoardWorksAndAttachesNothing()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await OpenBoardAsync(context);
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-share")).Not.ToBeCheckedAsync();
        var text = $"no permission {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await PostAsync(page, text);

        var item = page.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(item.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your message");
        await Expect(item.Locator("details.meta")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);

        await page.Locator("#compose-share").CheckAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-share")).Not.ToBeCheckedAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-share-status")).ToHaveTextAsync("No location permission, so nothing is attached.");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ClearingLocalHistoryDeletesTheDatabase()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await OpenBoardAsync(context);
        await PostAsync(page, $"to be cleared {Guid.NewGuid():N}");

        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Clear local history" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#clear-status")).ToHaveTextAsync("Local history cleared.");

        var names = await page.EvaluateAsync<string[]>("async () => (await indexedDB.databases()).map(d => d.name)");
        Assert.DoesNotContain("chaos-board", names);
    }

    private static async Task<IPage> OpenBoardAsync(IBrowserContext context)
    {
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/board");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-text")).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        return page;
    }

    private static async Task PostAsync(IPage page, string text)
    {
        await page.Locator("#compose-text").FillAsync(text);
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Send" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#board-feed .msg", new() { HasText = text })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    private static async Task EventuallyAsync(Func<Task<bool>> condition)
    {
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++)
        {
            if (await condition())
            {
                return;
            }

            await Task.Delay(100, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
        }

        Assert.True(await condition());
    }

    [GeneratedRegex("archived")]
    private static partial Regex Archived();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^27\.717")]
    private static partial Regex Kathmandu();
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using Xunit.Sdk;
using Xunit.v3;

[assembly: Parallelization(Mode = ParallelMode.None)]

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed class BrowserFixture : IAsyncLifetime
{
    private IPlaywright? _playwright;
    private IBrowser? _browser;

    public static string BaseUrl { get; } = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("CHAOS_BASE_URL") ?? "http://127.0.0.1:5080/";

    public static string RiggedUrl { get; } = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("CHAOS_RIGGED_URL") ?? "http://127.0.0.1:5081/";

    public async ValueTask InitializeAsync()
    {
        _playwright = await Playwright.CreateAsync();
        _browser = await _playwright.Chromium.LaunchAsync();
    }

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        if (_browser is not null)
        {
            await _browser.DisposeAsync();
        }

        _playwright?.Dispose();
    }

    public Task<IBrowserContext> NewContextAsync(ColorScheme scheme = ColorScheme.Light) => NewContextAsync(BaseUrl, scheme);

    public Task<IBrowserContext> NewLocatedContextAsync(float latitude, float longitude)
    {
        var browser = _browser ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The browser is not running.");
        return browser.NewContextAsync(new BrowserNewContextOptions
        {
            BaseURL = BaseUrl,
            ColorScheme = ColorScheme.Light,
            Geolocation = new Geolocation { Latitude = latitude, Longitude = longitude, Accuracy = 25 },
            Permissions = ["geolocation"],
        });
    }

    public Task<IBrowserContext> NewContextAsync(string baseUrl, ColorScheme scheme = ColorScheme.Light)
    {
        var browser = _browser ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The browser is not running.");
        return browser.NewContextAsync(new BrowserNewContextOptions { BaseURL = baseUrl, ColorScheme = scheme });
    }
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed class CanvasTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    private const string Ink = """
        ([x, y]) => {
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas.paper');
            const pixel = canvas.getContext('2d').getImageData(Math.floor(x * canvas.width), Math.floor(y * canvas.height), 1, 1).data;
            return pixel[3] > 0;
        }
        """;

    private const string Blank = """
        ([x, y]) => {
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas.paper');
            const pixel = canvas.getContext('2d').getImageData(Math.floor(x * canvas.width), Math.floor(y * canvas.height), 1, 1).data;
            return pixel[3] === 0;
        }
        """;

    [Fact]
    public async Task StrokesReachEveryoneAndResetClearsThem()
    {
        await using var firstContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var secondContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var artist = await OpenCanvasAsync(firstContext);
        var viewer = await OpenCanvasAsync(secondContext);

        var box = await artist.Locator("canvas.paper").BoundingBoxAsync() ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The canvas has no size.");
        await artist.Mouse.MoveAsync(box.X + (box.Width * 0.2f), box.Y + (box.Height * 0.2f));
        await artist.Mouse.DownAsync();
        await artist.Mouse.MoveAsync(box.X + (box.Width * 0.8f), box.Y + (box.Height * 0.8f), new() { Steps = 25 });
        await artist.Mouse.UpAsync();

        var centre = new[] { 0.5, 0.5 };
        await viewer.WaitForFunctionAsync(Ink, centre);

        await using var lateContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var late = await OpenCanvasAsync(lateContext);
        await late.WaitForFunctionAsync(Ink, centre);

        await viewer.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Reset canvas" }).ClickAsync();
        await artist.WaitForFunctionAsync(Blank, centre);
        await late.WaitForFunctionAsync(Blank, centre);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FinishedStrokesAreKeptInTheBrowser()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var artist = await OpenCanvasAsync(context);
        var box = await artist.Locator("canvas.paper").BoundingBoxAsync() ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The canvas has no size.");
        await artist.Mouse.MoveAsync(box.X + (box.Width * 0.3f), box.Y + (box.Height * 0.6f));
        await artist.Mouse.DownAsync();
        await artist.Mouse.MoveAsync(box.X + (box.Width * 0.7f), box.Y + (box.Height * 0.6f), new() { Steps = 10 });
        await artist.Mouse.UpAsync();

        await artist.WaitForFunctionAsync(StoredStrokes, 1);

        await artist.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Reset canvas" }).ClickAsync();
        await artist.WaitForFunctionAsync(StoredStrokesAtMost, 0);
    }

    private const string StoredStrokes = """
        async minimum => (await indexedDB.databases()).some(d => d.name === 'chaos-canvas') && await new Promise(resolve => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-canvas');
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('strokes')) { db.close(); resolve(false); return; }
                const count = db.transaction('strokes', 'readonly').objectStore('strokes').count();
                count.onsuccess = () => { db.close(); resolve(count.result >= minimum); };
                count.onerror = () => { db.close(); resolve(false); };
            };
            request.onerror = () => resolve(false);
        })
        """;

    private const string StoredStrokesAtMost = """
        async maximum => (await indexedDB.databases()).some(d => d.name === 'chaos-canvas') && await new Promise(resolve => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-canvas');
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('strokes')) { db.close(); resolve(false); return; }
                const count = db.transaction('strokes', 'readonly').objectStore('strokes').count();
                count.onsuccess = () => { db.close(); resolve(count.result <= maximum); };
                count.onerror = () => { db.close(); resolve(false); };
            };
            request.onerror = () => resolve(false);
        })
        """;

    private static async Task<IPage> OpenCanvasAsync(IBrowserContext context)
    {
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/canvas");
        await Expect(page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Reset canvas" })).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("canvas.paper[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        return page;
    }
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed class LatencyTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task ATestReachesEveryoneAndUnlocksAutoRun()
    {
        await using var firstContext = await OfflineContextAsync();
        await using var secondContext = await OfflineContextAsync();
        var tester = await firstContext.NewPageAsync();
        await tester.GotoAsync("/about");
        await tester.EvaluateAsync("() => localStorage.setItem('chaos-latency-runs', '9')");
        await OpenAsync(tester);
        var watcher = await secondContext.NewPageAsync();
        await OpenAsync(watcher);

        var auto = tester.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Auto run" });
        await Expect(auto).ToBeHiddenAsync();
        await tester.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Run test" }).ClickAsync();

        var mine = tester.Locator("#latency-feed .msg.mine").First;
        await Expect(mine).ToBeVisibleAsync(new() { Timeout = 30_000 });
        await Expect(auto).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(tester.Locator(".probe .status")).ToHaveTextAsync("Done. Your result is at the top of the list.");
        await Expect(tester.Locator(".probe tr[data-key='chaos'] .browser")).ToHaveTextAsync(new System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex("^\\d+ ms$"));

        var id = await mine.GetAttributeAsync("data-id") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The result has no id.");
        var seen = watcher.Locator($"#latency-feed .msg[data-id='{id}']");
        await Expect(seen).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(seen.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        await seen.Locator("summary").ClickAsync();
        await Expect(seen.Locator("tbody tr")).ToHaveCountAsync(await tester.Locator(".probe tbody tr").CountAsync());
        await Expect(watcher.Locator("#latency-browser-fastest-empty")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(watcher.Locator("#latency-jackpot-winners-empty")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    private async Task<IBrowserContext> OfflineContextAsync()
    {
        var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await context.RouteAsync(url => !url.StartsWith(BrowserFixture.BaseUrl, StringComparison.Ordinal), route => route.AbortAsync());
        return context;
    }

    private static async Task OpenAsync(IPage page)
    {
        await page.GotoAsync("/latency");
        await Expect(page.Locator(".probe[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }
}

using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed partial class SiteTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task PagesLoadWithoutErrorsOrCookies()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var errors = new List<string>();
        page.Console += (_, message) =>
        {
            if (message.Type == "error")
            {
                errors.Add(message.Text);
            }
        };
        page.PageError += (_, error) => errors.Add(error);

        foreach (var path in new[] { "/", "/about", "/board", "/slots", "/latency", "/canvas" })
        {
            await page.GotoAsync(path);
        }

        await Expect(page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Reset canvas" })).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        Assert.Empty(errors);
        Assert.Empty(await context.CookiesAsync());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OnlyTheInteractivePagesOpenAWebSocket()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var sockets = new List<string>();
        page.WebSocket += (_, socket) => sockets.Add(socket.Url);

        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.GotoAsync("/");
        await page.WaitForTimeoutAsync(1000);
        Assert.Empty(sockets);

        await page.GotoAsync("/board");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#compose-text")).ToBeEnabledAsync();
        Assert.NotEmpty(sockets);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ThemeChoiceSurvivesReloadAndNavigation()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var root = page.Locator("html");

        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Dark theme" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");

        await page.ReloadAsync();
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation).GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "Canvas" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page).ToHaveURLAsync(CanvasUrl());
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ResponsesCarryAStrictPolicyAndACustomNotFoundPage()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();

        var home = await page.GotoAsync("/") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No response.");
        Assert.StartsWith("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'", home.Headers["content-security-policy"], StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var missing = await page.GotoAsync("/no-such-page") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No response.");
        Assert.Equal(404, missing.Status);
        await Expect(page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Heading, new() { Name = "Nothing here" })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheFooterClockTicksWithTheServerTime()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        var clock = page.Locator("#server-clock");
        await Expect(clock).ToHaveTextAsync(ClockFormat());
        var first = await clock.TextContentAsync();
        await Expect(clock).Not.ToHaveTextAsync(first ?? "", new() { Timeout = 5000 });

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation).GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "Board" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(clock).ToHaveTextAsync(ClockFormat());
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2} UTC([+-]\d{2}:\d{2})?$")]
    private static partial Regex ClockFormat();

    [GeneratedRegex("/canvas$")]
    private static partial Regex CanvasUrl();
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Chaos.E2E;

public sealed class SlotTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task APressSpinsForEveryoneAndLandsInTheirResults()
    {
        await using var firstContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var secondContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var presser = await OpenSlotsAsync(firstContext);
        var watcher = await OpenSlotsAsync(secondContext);

        await SpinButton(presser).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(presser.Locator(".machine .status")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your press worked. This spin is yours.");

        var mine = presser.Locator("#slots-feed .msg.mine").First;
        await Expect(mine).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        var id = await mine.GetAttributeAsync("data-id") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The result has no id.");

        var seen = watcher.Locator($"#slots-feed .msg[data-id='{id}']");
        await Expect(seen).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(seen.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        await Expect(SpinButton(watcher)).ToBeEnabledAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TenPressesUnlockAutoSpinThatKeepsGoingInBackgroundTabs()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await context.AddInitScriptAsync("Object.defineProperty(document, 'visibilityState', { get: () => 'hidden' }); Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', { get: () => true });");
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.EvaluateAsync("() => localStorage.setItem('chaos-slots-presses', '9')");
        await page.GotoAsync("/slots");
        await Expect(page.Locator(".machine[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();

        var auto = page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Auto spin" });
        await Expect(auto).ToBeHiddenAsync();
        await SpinButton(page).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(auto).ToBeVisibleAsync();

        await auto.ClickAsync();
        await Expect(auto).ToHaveAttributeAsync("aria-pressed", "true");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#slots-feed .msg.mine").Nth(2)).ToBeVisibleAsync(new() { Timeout = 20_000 });
        await auto.ClickAsync();
        await Expect(auto).ToHaveAttributeAsync("aria-pressed", "false");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task WinnersAreKeptAndExplainTheirOdds()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        var id = Guid.CreateVersion7(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(-1)).ToString();
        await page.EvaluateAsync(
            """
            ([id, text]) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-slots', 1);
                request.onupgradeneeded = () => request.result.createObjectStore('spins', { keyPath: 'id' });
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
                request.onsuccess = () => {
                    const db = request.result;
                    const transaction = db.transaction('spins', 'readwrite');
                    transaction.objectStore('spins').put({ id, text, mine: false });
                    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
                        db.close();
                        resolve();
                    };
                    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
                };
            })
            """,
            new[] { id, "QQQQ7" });
        await page.GotoAsync("/slots");
        await Expect(page.Locator(".machine[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();

        var winner = page.Locator($"#slots-winners .msg[data-id='{id}']");
        await Expect(winner).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(winner.Locator(".lesson")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        await winner.Locator("summary").ClickAsync();
        await Expect(winner.Locator(".lesson")).ToContainTextAsync("1 in 9,598");
        await Expect(page.Locator($"#slots-jackpots .msg.jackpot[data-id='{id}']")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await KeepsJackpotAsync(page, id);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task JackpotsAreSharedAndNeverForgotten()
    {
        await using var seededContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await seededContext.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        var id = Guid.CreateVersion7(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(-400)).ToString();
        await page.EvaluateAsync(
            """
            ([id, text]) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-slots', 2);
                request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
                    request.result.createObjectStore('spins', { keyPath: 'id' });
                    request.result.createObjectStore('jackpots', { keyPath: 'id' });
                };
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
                request.onsuccess = () => {
                    const db = request.result;
                    const transaction = db.transaction('jackpots', 'readwrite');
                    transaction.objectStore('jackpots').put({ id, text, mine: true });
                    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
                        db.close();
                        resolve();
                    };
                    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
                };
            })
            """,
            new[] { id, "KKKKK" });
        await page.GotoAsync("/slots");
        await Expect(page.Locator(".machine[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        var kept = page.Locator($"#slots-jackpots .msg.jackpot[data-id='{id}']");
        await Expect(kept).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(kept.Locator(".prize")).ToHaveTextAsync("Jackpot");
        await Expect(kept.Locator(".yours")).ToBeVisibleAsync();

        await using var freshContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var fresh = await OpenSlotsAsync(freshContext);
        var shared = fresh.Locator($"#slots-jackpots .msg.jackpot[data-id='{id}']");
        await Expect(shared).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(shared.Locator(".yours")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        await Expect(fresh.Locator("#slots-jackpots-empty")).ToBeHiddenAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AJackpotIsCelebratedByEveryone()
    {
        await using var firstContext = await browser.NewContextAsync(BrowserFixture.RiggedUrl);
        await using var secondContext = await browser.NewContextAsync(BrowserFixture.RiggedUrl);
        var presser = await OpenSlotsAsync(firstContext);
        var watcher = await OpenSlotsAsync(secondContext);

        await SpinButton(presser).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(presser.Locator(".machine .status")).ToHaveTextAsync("Your press worked. This spin is yours.");

        foreach (var page in new[] { presser, watcher })
        {
            await Expect(page.Locator(".machine.jackpot")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
            await Expect(page.Locator(".machine .verdict")).ToHaveTextAsync("Jackpot! Five of a kind");
        }

        var mine = presser.Locator("#slots-jackpots .msg.jackpot.mine").First;
        await Expect(mine).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(mine.Locator(".symbols")).ToHaveTextAsync("77777");
        var id = await mine.GetAttributeAsync("data-id") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The jackpot has no id.");
        await Expect(watcher.Locator($"#slots-jackpots .msg.jackpot[data-id='{id}']")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await KeepsJackpotAsync(watcher, id);
        await Expect(presser.Locator(".machine.jackpot")).ToHaveCountAsync(0, new() { Timeout = 15_000 });
    }

    private static async Task KeepsJackpotAsync(IPage page, string id)
    {
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++)
        {
            if ((await KeptJackpotsAsync(page)).Contains(id, StringComparer.Ordinal))
            {
                return;
            }

            await Task.Delay(100, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
        }

        Assert.Contains(id, await KeptJackpotsAsync(page));
    }

    private static Task<string[]> KeptJackpotsAsync(IPage page) =>
        page.EvaluateAsync<string[]>(
            """
            () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const request = indexedDB.open('chaos-slots');
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
                request.onsuccess = () => {
                    const db = request.result;
                    if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('jackpots')) {
                        db.close();
                        resolve([]);
                        return;
                    }
                    const keys = db.transaction('jackpots', 'readonly').objectStore('jackpots').getAllKeys();
                    keys.onsuccess = () => {
                        db.close();
                        resolve(keys.result);
                    };
                    keys.onerror = () => reject(keys.error);
                };
            })
            """);

    private static ILocator SpinButton(IPage page) =>
        page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Spin", Exact = true });

    private static async Task<IPage> OpenSlotsAsync(IBrowserContext context)
    {
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/slots");
        await Expect(page.Locator(".machine[data-ready]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        return page;
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class BoardServiceTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Start = new(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(Start);

    [Fact]
    public void PostStampsMessagesWithTheServerClock()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("  hello  ", "a"));
        var message = Assert.Single(board.Messages);
        Assert.Equal("hello", message.Text);
        Assert.Equal(Start, Uuid7.Timestamp(message.Id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PostRejectsBlankText()
    {
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Empty, Create().Post(" \n ", "a"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PostIsRateLimitedPerClient()
    {
        var board = Create(o =>
        {
            o.PostBurst = 2;
            o.PostsPerSecond = 0.5;
        });
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("one", "a"));
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("two", "a"));
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.TooFast, board.Post("three", "a"));
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("four", "b"));
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("five", "a"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PostRejectsRecentRepeats()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post("same", "a"));
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Repeat, board.Post("same", "b"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsOnlyTheNewestMessages()
    {
        var board = Create(o => o.BoardCapacity = 3);
        for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        {
            _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
            Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, board.Post($"m{i}", $"c{i}"));
        }

        Assert.Equal(["m4", "m3", "m2"], board.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubscribersGetASnapshotThenDeltas()
    {
        var board = Create(o => o.BoardCapacity = 1);
        board.Post("first", "a");
        var recorder = new BoardRecorder();
        board.Subscribe(recorder);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        board.Post("second", "b");

        Assert.Equal(2, recorder.Updates.Count);
        var snapshot = recorder.Updates[0];
        var delta = recorder.Updates[1];
        Assert.Equal("snapshot", snapshot.Kind);
        Assert.Equal("first", Assert.Single(snapshot.Added).Text);
        Assert.Equal("delta", delta.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(snapshot.Version, delta.Previous);
        Assert.Equal(snapshot.Version + 1, delta.Version);
        Assert.Equal("second", Assert.Single(delta.Added).Text);
        Assert.Equal(snapshot.Added[0].Id, Assert.Single(delta.Removed));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubscribersThatCannotKeepUpAreDropped()
    {
        var board = Create();
        var recorder = new BoardRecorder(accept: false);
        board.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.False(board.IsSubscribed(recorder));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferAcceptsOlderMessagesWhenThereIsRoom()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Equal(2, board.Offer([Old(-2, "older"), Old(-1, "newer")]));
        Assert.Equal(["newer", "older"], board.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferRejectsMessagesFromTheFuture()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(1)), "future")]));
        Assert.Empty(board.Messages);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferRejectsMessagesThisServerShouldAlreadyHave()
    {
        var board = Create();
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(1));
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(5)), "forged")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start), "at start")]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferRejectsInvalidIdsAndText()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer(
        [
            new BoardMessage(Guid.NewGuid(), "v4"),
            Old(-1, " padded"),
            Old(-2, ""),
            Old(-3, new string('x', MessageText.MaxLength + 1)),
        ]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferDoesNothingWhenTheServerIsFullOfNewerMessages()
    {
        var board = Create(o => o.BoardCapacity = 2);
        board.Post("a", "a");
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        board.Post("b", "b");
        var recorder = new BoardRecorder();
        board.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer([Old(-1, "old")]));
        Assert.Single(recorder.Updates);
        Assert.Equal(["b", "a"], board.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferKeepsTheServerCopyOnTies()
    {
        var board = Create();
        var id = Old(-1, "x").Id;
        Assert.Equal(1, board.Offer([new BoardMessage(id, "kept")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer([new BoardMessage(id, "ignored")]));
        Assert.Equal("kept", Assert.Single(board.Messages).Text);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferConsidersAtMostFiftyMessages()
    {
        var board = Create(o => o.BoardCapacity = 100);
        var offered = Enumerable.Range(1, 60).Select(i => Old(-i, $"m{i}")).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(BoardService.MaxOffers, board.Offer(offered));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubmitReturnsTheMessageWithNormalizedDetails()
    {
        var board = Create();
        var reply = board.Submit("located", "a", [new("timeZone", " UTC "), new("longitude", "2"), new("latitude", "1"), new("mystery", "x")]);
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, reply.Result);
        var message = Assert.Single(board.Messages);
        Assert.Same(message, reply.Message);
        Assert.Equal(
            [new MetaEntry("latitude", "1"), new MetaEntry("longitude", "2"), new MetaEntry("timeZone", "UTC")],
            message.Meta);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubmitDropsDetailsWithoutALocation()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Null(board.Submit("no location", "a", [new("timeZone", "UTC")]).Message!.Meta);
        Assert.Null(board.Submit("no details", "b", null).Message!.Meta);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubmitReturnsNoMessageWhenRefused()
    {
        var board = Create();
        Assert.Null(board.Submit(" ", "a", null).Message);
        board.Submit("same", "a", null);
        var repeat = board.Submit("same", "b", [new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2")]);
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Repeat, repeat.Result);
        Assert.Null(repeat.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DetailsReachSubscribers()
    {
        var board = Create();
        var recorder = new BoardRecorder();
        board.Subscribe(recorder);
        board.Submit("shared", "a", [new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2")]);
        Assert.Equal("1", Assert.Single(recorder.Updates[1].Added).Meta![0].Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferKeepsNormalizedDetailsAndRejectsOthers()
    {
        var board = Create();
        MetaEntry[] good = [new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2")];
        Assert.Equal(1, board.Offer([Old(-1, "kept") with { Meta = good }]));
        Assert.Equal(0, board.Offer(
        [
            Old(-2, "reordered") with { Meta = [new("longitude", "2"), new("latitude", "1")] },
            Old(-3, "unknown") with { Meta = [new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2"), new("mystery", "x")] },
            Old(-4, "empty") with { Meta = [] },
            Old(-5, "no location") with { Meta = [new("timeZone", "UTC")] },
        ]));
        Assert.Equal(good, Assert.Single(board.Messages).Meta);
    }

    private BoardService Create(Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null) => new(_time, Limits.Create(configure));

    private static BoardMessage Old(int minutes, string text) =>
        new(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(minutes)), text);
}

using Chaos.Web.Drawing;
using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class CanvasPersistenceTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Start = new(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(Start);

    [Fact]
    public void StrokesAndResetsGetIncreasingIds()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.ResetBurst = 10);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, [1, 1], true);
        var first = session.LastStroke;
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 2, [1, 1], true);
        var second = session.LastStroke;
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(first));
        Assert.True(second.CompareTo(first) > 0);

        Assert.Equal(ResetResult.Accepted, canvas.Reset("a"));
        Assert.True(canvas.ResetId.CompareTo(second) > 0);
        var next = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        canvas.Draw(next, canvas.Epoch, 1, [1, 1], true);
        Assert.True(next.LastStroke.CompareTo(canvas.ResetId) > 0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ResetsAreBroadcastWithTheirId()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        canvas.Reset("a");
        var update = viewer.Updates[^1];
        Assert.Equal("reset", update.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(canvas.ResetId, update.Reset);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SnapshotsCarryTheRestoreContext()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.CanvasMaxPoints = 5000);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(10), true);
        var late = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(late);
        var snapshot = Assert.Single(late.Updates);
        Assert.Null(snapshot.Reset);
        Assert.Equal(Start.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds(), snapshot.Started);
        Assert.Equal(4990, snapshot.Room);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrokesFromBeforeTheRestartAreTakenBack()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        var old = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-5));

        Assert.Equal(1, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(old, [1, 2, 3, 4])]));
        Assert.Equal(1, canvas.StrokeCount);
        Assert.Equal(2, canvas.PointCount);
        var merge = viewer.Updates[^1];
        Assert.Equal("merge", merge.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(old, Assert.Single(merge.Strokes!).Id);
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(old, [1, 2, 3, 4])]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrokesFromThisRunOrTheFutureAreRefused()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1));
        var recent = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddSeconds(1));
        var future = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddHours(1));
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(recent, [1, 1]), new StrokeOffer(future, [1, 1]), new StrokeOffer(Guid.NewGuid(), [1, 1])]));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(new int[0])]
    [InlineData(new[] { 1, 2, 3 })]
    [InlineData(new[] { 4096, 1 })]
    [InlineData(new[] { -1, 1 })]
    public void MalformedStrokesAreRefused(int[] points)
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1)), points)]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OffersNeverEvictAndPreferTheNewest()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.CanvasMaxPoints = CanvasService.MaxStrokePoints);
        var older = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-2));
        var newer = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1));
        var accepted = canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(older, Points(1500)), new StrokeOffer(newer, Points(1500))]);
        Assert.Equal(1, accepted);
        var late = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(late);
        Assert.Equal(newer, Assert.Single(late.Updates[0].Strokes!).Id);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ARememberedResetDropsOlderStrokes()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        var before = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-3));
        var reset = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-2));
        var after = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1));
        canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(before, [1, 1]), new StrokeOffer(after, [2, 2])]);
        var epoch = canvas.Epoch;

        Assert.True(canvas.OfferReset(reset));
        Assert.Equal(reset, canvas.ResetId);
        Assert.Equal(epoch + 1, canvas.Epoch);
        var snapshot = viewer.Updates[^1];
        Assert.Equal("snapshot", snapshot.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(after, Assert.Single(snapshot.Strokes!).Id);
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(before, [1, 1])]));
        Assert.False(canvas.OfferReset(before));
        Assert.False(canvas.OfferReset(reset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ResetsFromThisRunWinOverRememberedOnes()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
        canvas.Reset("a");
        Assert.False(canvas.OfferReset(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1))));
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.OfferStrokes([new StrokeOffer(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1)), [1, 1])]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ResetsFromAfterTheRestartAreRefused()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1));
        Assert.False(canvas.OfferReset(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddSeconds(10))));
        Assert.False(canvas.OfferReset(Guid.NewGuid()));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DrawRepliesCarryTheStrokeId()
    {
        var id = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start);
        Assert.Equal($"0 {id:D}", DrawReply.Format(DrawResult.Accepted, id));
        Assert.Equal("2", DrawReply.Format(DrawResult.TooFast, id));
        Assert.Equal("0", DrawReply.Format(DrawResult.Accepted, Guid.Empty));
    }

    private CanvasService Create(Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null) => new(_time, Limits.Create(configure));

    private static int[] Points(int count) => [.. Enumerable.Range(0, count * 2).Select(i => i % 4096)];
}
