using Chaos.Web.Drawing;
using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class CanvasServiceTests
{
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero));

    [Fact]
    public void StrokesReachEveryoneButTheArtist()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var artist = new CanvasRecorder();
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(artist);
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(artist);

        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, [10, 10, 20, 20], false));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, [30, 30], true));

        Assert.Single(artist.Updates);
        Assert.Equal(3, viewer.Updates.Count);
        Assert.Equal(0, viewer.Updates[1].Offset);
        Assert.Equal(2, viewer.Updates[2].Offset);
        Assert.True(viewer.Updates[2].Done);
        Assert.Equal(3, canvas.PointCount);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StaleEpochsAreRejected()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Stale, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch + 1, 1, [1, 1], true));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(new[] { 4096, 1 })]
    [InlineData(new[] { -1, 1 })]
    [InlineData(new[] { 1, 1, 1 })]
    [InlineData(new int[0])]
    public void InvalidBatchesAreRejected(int[] points)
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Invalid, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, points, false));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OversizedBatchesAreRejected()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        var points = new int[(CanvasService.MaxBatchPoints + 1) * 2];
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Invalid, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, points, false));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrokeIdsMustMoveForward()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 5, [1, 1], true));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Invalid, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 5, [2, 2], true));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Invalid, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 4, [2, 2], true));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PointsAreRateLimited()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o =>
        {
            o.StrokePointBurst = 300;
            o.StrokePointsPerSecond = 1;
        });
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        var batch = new int[CanvasService.MaxBatchPoints * 2];
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, batch, false));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.TooFast, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, batch, false));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OverlongStrokesEnd()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.StrokePointBurst = 100_000);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        var batch = new int[CanvasService.MaxBatchPoints * 2];
        for (var i = 0; i < CanvasService.MaxStrokePoints / CanvasService.MaxBatchPoints; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, batch, false));
        }

        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.TooLong, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, batch, false));
        Assert.Null(session.Open);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OldFinishedStrokesMakeRoomForNewOnes()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.CanvasMaxPoints = 10);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(6), true));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 2, Points(6), true));
        Assert.Equal(1, canvas.StrokeCount);
        Assert.Equal(6, canvas.PointCount);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OpenStrokesAreNeverEvicted()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.CanvasMaxPoints = 10);
        var first = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        var second = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Accepted, canvas.Draw(first, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(6), false));
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Full, canvas.Draw(second, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(6), false));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ResetStartsANewEpochForEveryone()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.ResetBurst = 10);
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(viewer);
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(2), false);
        var epoch = canvas.Epoch;

        Assert.Equal(ResetResult.Accepted, canvas.Reset("a"));
        Assert.Equal(epoch + 1, canvas.Epoch);
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.StrokeCount);
        Assert.Equal(0, canvas.PointCount);
        Assert.Equal("reset", viewer.Updates[^1].Kind);
        Assert.Equal(DrawResult.Stale, canvas.Draw(session, epoch, 1, Points(2), true));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ResetsHaveACooldown()
    {
        var canvas = Create(o => o.ResetBurst = 10);
        Assert.Equal(ResetResult.Accepted, canvas.Reset("a"));
        Assert.Equal(ResetResult.TooFast, canvas.Reset("b"));
        _time.Advance(CanvasService.ResetCooldown);
        Assert.Equal(ResetResult.Accepted, canvas.Reset("b"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LateJoinersGetEveryStroke()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(new CanvasRecorder());
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, [1, 2, 3, 4], true);
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 2, [5, 6], false);
        var late = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(late);

        var snapshot = Assert.Single(late.Updates);
        Assert.Equal("snapshot", snapshot.Kind);
        Assert.NotNull(snapshot.Strokes);
        Assert.Equal(2, snapshot.Strokes.Count);
        Assert.Equal([1, 2, 3, 4], snapshot.Strokes[0].Points);
        Assert.True(snapshot.Strokes[0].Done);
        Assert.False(snapshot.Strokes[1].Done);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AbandonFinishesTheOpenStroke()
    {
        var canvas = Create();
        var artist = new CanvasRecorder();
        var viewer = new CanvasRecorder();
        canvas.Subscribe(viewer);
        var session = canvas.OpenSession(artist);
        canvas.Draw(session, canvas.Epoch, 1, Points(2), false);
        canvas.Abandon(session);

        var last = viewer.Updates[^1];
        Assert.True(last.Done);
        Assert.Empty(last.Points);
        Assert.Equal(2, last.Offset);
    }

    private CanvasService Create(Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null) => new(_time, Limits.Create(configure));

    private static int[] Points(int count) => [.. Enumerable.Range(0, count * 2).Select(i => i % 4096)];
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class ServerTimeTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0, "UTC")]
    [InlineData(330, "UTC+05:30")]
    [InlineData(-240, "UTC-04:00")]
    public void ZonesAreLabelled(int minutes, string expected) => Assert.Equal(expected, ServerTime.Zone(minutes));

    [Fact]
    public void LabelsUseTheServerOffset()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 16, 8, 10, 39, 500, TimeSpan.Zero).ToUnixTimeMilliseconds();
        Assert.Equal("2026-10-16 08:10:39 UTC", new ServerTime(at, 0).Label);
        Assert.Equal("2026-10-16 13:40:39 UTC+05:30", new ServerTime(at, 330).Label);
        Assert.Equal("2026-10-16T13:40:39+05:30", new ServerTime(at, 330).Iso);
    }
}

public sealed class KeyedCooldownTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void EachKeyWaitsOutItsOwnCooldown()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var cooldowns = new KeyedCooldowns(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60), time, maxKeys: 2);
        Assert.True(cooldowns.TryStart("a", out _));
        Assert.False(cooldowns.TryStart("a", out var remaining));
        Assert.Equal(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60), remaining);
        Assert.True(cooldowns.TryStart("b", out _));
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(45));
        Assert.Equal(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15), cooldowns.Remaining("a"));
        Assert.True(cooldowns.TryStart("c", out _));
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
        Assert.True(cooldowns.TryStart("a", out _));
    }
}

public sealed class Uuid7OrderTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void AfterIsAlwaysLater()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var previous = Guid.Empty;
        for (var i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
        {
            var next = Uuid7.After(previous, at);
            Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(next));
            Assert.True(next.CompareTo(previous) > 0);
            Assert.True(string.CompareOrdinal(next.ToString(), previous.ToString()) > 0);
            previous = next;
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AfterSurvivesTheClockGoingBack()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var later = Guid.CreateVersion7(at.AddMinutes(5));
        var next = Uuid7.After(later, at);
        Assert.True(next.CompareTo(later) > 0);
        Assert.Equal(Uuid7.Timestamp(later), Uuid7.Timestamp(next));
    }
}

public sealed class LatencyOptionsTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void LatencyLimitsAreChecked()
    {
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyCooldownSeconds = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyGapSeconds = -1 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyBudgetSeconds = 1 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyRankCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { LatencyJackpotCapacity = 1_001 }.IsValid());
        Assert.True(new ChaosOptions { LatencyCooldownSeconds = 1, LatencyGapSeconds = 0 }.IsValid());
    }
}

using System.Net;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Latency;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Chaos.Web.Slots;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class ChaosFactory : WebApplicationFactory<Program>
{
    public const string AllowedOrigin = "https://chaos.example";

    protected override void ConfigureWebHost(IWebHostBuilder builder)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(builder);
        builder.UseSetting("Chaos:AllowedOrigins:0", AllowedOrigin);
    }
}

public sealed class HttpPipelineTests(ChaosFactory factory) : IClassFixture<ChaosFactory>
{
    private static CancellationToken Cancellation => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("/", "Chaos")]
    [InlineData("/board", "Chaos board")]
    [InlineData("/canvas", "Chaos canvas")]
    [InlineData("/slots", "Chaos slots")]
    [InlineData("/latency", "Chaos latency")]
    [InlineData("/about", "About chaos")]
    public async Task PagesRenderWithoutSettingCookies(string path, string title)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync(path, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.False(response.Headers.Contains("Set-Cookie"));
        Assert.Contains($"<title>{title}</title>", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task BoardPageRendersServerMessages()
    {
        var text = $"rendered {Guid.NewGuid():N}";
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, factory.Services.GetRequiredService<BoardService>().Post(text, "integration"));
        using var client = CreateClient();
        Assert.Contains(text, await client.GetStringAsync("/board", Cancellation));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task BoardPageRendersMessageDetailsCollapsed()
    {
        var text = $"located {Guid.NewGuid():N}";
        var reply = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<BoardService>().Submit(text, $"integration {Guid.NewGuid():N}", [new("latitude", "27.7172"), new("longitude", "85.324"), new("timeZone", "Asia/Kathmandu")]);
        Assert.Equal(PostResult.Accepted, reply.Result);
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/board", Cancellation);
        var start = html.IndexOf($"data-id=\"{reply.Message!.Id}\"", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.True(start >= 0);
        var item = html[start..html.IndexOf("</li>", start, StringComparison.Ordinal)];
        var details = Regex.Match(item, "<details class=\"meta\"[^>]*>");
        Assert.True(details.Success);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(" open", details.Value, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Matches(">Latitude</dt><dd[^>]*>27.7172</dd>", item);
        Assert.Matches(">Time zone</dt><dd[^>]*>Asia/Kathmandu</dd>", item);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"compose-share\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task LatencyPageListsJackpotsLast()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/latency", Cancellation);
        var results = html.IndexOf("id=\"runs-title\"", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        var leaders = html.IndexOf("id=\"leaders-title\"", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        var jackpots = html.IndexOf("id=\"jackpots-title\"", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.True(results >= 0 && results < leaders && leaders < jackpots);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task SlotsPageRendersSettledResults()
    {
        var slots = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<SlotService>();
        var press = slots.Press($"integration {Guid.NewGuid():N}");
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, press.Result);
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/slots", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"slots-feed\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain($"data-id=\"{press.Id}\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task SlotsPageRendersWinnersWithTheirOdds()
    {
        var slots = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<SlotService>();
        var winner = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddHours(-1)), "QQQQ7");
        slots.OfferWinners([winner]);
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/slots", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"slots-winners\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("data-text=\"QQQQ7\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task SlotsPageRendersJackpots()
    {
        var slots = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<SlotService>();
        var jackpot = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(-30)), "KKKKK");
        Assert.Equal(1, slots.OfferJackpots([jackpot]));
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/slots", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"slots-jackpots\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains($"class=\"msg win jackpot\" data-id=\"{jackpot.Id}\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("class=\"confetti\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ResponsesCarrySecurityHeaders()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(SecurityHeaders.ContentSecurityPolicy, response.Headers.GetValues("Content-Security-Policy").Single());
        Assert.Equal("nosniff", response.Headers.GetValues("X-Content-Type-Options").Single());
        Assert.Equal("no-referrer", response.Headers.GetValues("Referrer-Policy").Single());
        Assert.Equal(SecurityHeaders.PermissionsPolicy, response.Headers.GetValues("Permissions-Policy").Single());
        Assert.Contains("geolocation=(self)", SecurityHeaders.PermissionsPolicy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("camera=()", SecurityHeaders.PermissionsPolicy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task PagesShowTheServerClock()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/about", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"server-clock\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("data-now=\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"announcer\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TimeEndpointReportsTheServerClock()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var before = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/time", Cancellation);
        var after = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds();
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.True(response.Headers.CacheControl?.NoStore);
        using var json = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation));
        var now = json.RootElement.GetProperty("unixMs").GetInt64();
        Assert.InRange(now, before, after);
        Assert.InRange(json.RootElement.GetProperty("offsetMinutes").GetInt32(), -14 * 60, 14 * 60);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task LatencyPageListsEveryEndpoint()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/latency", Cancellation);
        foreach (var endpoint in LatencyCatalog.Endpoints)
        {
            Assert.Contains($"data-key=\"{endpoint.Key}\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task LatencyPageHasEmptyLeaderboardsBelowTheResults()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/latency", Cancellation);
        var feed = html.IndexOf("id=\"latency-feed\"", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.True(feed > 0);
        foreach (var id in new[] { "latency-jackpot-winners", "latency-jackpot-losers", "latency-browser-fastest", "latency-browser-slowest", "latency-server-fastest", "latency-server-slowest" })
        {
            Assert.True(html.IndexOf($"id=\"{id}\"", StringComparison.Ordinal) > feed, id);
            Assert.Contains($"id=\"{id}-empty\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ThePolicyAllowsTheLatencyEndpoints()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/", Cancellation);
        var policy = response.Headers.GetValues("Content-Security-Policy").Single();
        Assert.StartsWith("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'", policy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("https://dynamodb.us-east-1.amazonaws.com", policy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("object-src 'none'", policy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HealthEndpointReportsHealthy()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        Assert.Equal("Healthy", await client.GetStringAsync("/health", Cancellation));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnknownPathsGetTheNotFoundPage()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/no-such-page", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Contains("Nothing here", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("https://evil.example", HttpStatusCode.Forbidden)]
    [InlineData(ChaosFactory.AllowedOrigin, HttpStatusCode.OK)]
    public async Task HubNegotiationChecksTheOrigin(string origin, HttpStatusCode expected)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, "/_blazor/negotiate?negotiateVersion=1");
        request.Headers.Add("Origin", origin);
        using var response = await client.SendAsync(request, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(expected, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HubRefusesTransportsOtherThanWebSockets()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/_blazor?id=abc", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task PagesAreCompressedWhenAsked()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, "/about");
        request.Headers.AcceptEncoding.ParseAdd("br");
        using var response = await client.SendAsync(request, Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("br", response.Content.Headers.ContentEncoding);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FormPostsAreRejected()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var content = new FormUrlEncodedContent([new KeyValuePair<string, string>("text", "hi")]);
        using var response = await client.PostAsync("/board", content, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("/app.css")]
    [InlineData("/theme-init.js")]
    [InlineData("/favicon.svg")]
    public async Task StaticAssetsAreServed(string path)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync(path, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
    }

    private HttpClient CreateClient() =>
        factory.CreateClient(new WebApplicationFactoryClientOptions { AllowAutoRedirect = false, HandleCookies = false });
}

using System.Net;
using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class TokenBucketTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void TakesUntilEmptyThenRefillsOverTime()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var bucket = new TokenBucket(2, 1, time);
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NeverHoldsMoreThanItsCapacity()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var bucket = new TokenBucket(2, 1, time);
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(1));
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake(2));
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeyedBucketsAreIndependentAndBounded()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var buckets = new KeyedTokenBuckets(1, 1, time, maxKeys: 3);
        Assert.True(buckets.TryTake("a"));
        Assert.False(buckets.TryTake("a"));
        Assert.True(buckets.TryTake("b"));
        for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
            buckets.TryTake($"k{i}");
        }

        Assert.InRange(buckets.Count, 1, 3);
    }
}

public sealed class ConnectionTrackerTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void LimitsConnectionsPerClientAndOverall()
    {
        var tracker = new ConnectionTracker(Limits.Create(o =>
        {
            o.MaxConnectionsPerClient = 2;
            o.MaxConnections = 3;
        }));

        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out var first));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out var second));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.ClientFull, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out _));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("b", out var third));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull, tracker.TryAdmit("c", out _));

        first?.Dispose();
        first?.Dispose();
        Assert.Equal(2, tracker.Total);
        Assert.Equal(1, tracker.CountFor("a"));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("c", out var fourth));

        second?.Dispose();
        third?.Dispose();
        fourth?.Dispose();
        Assert.Equal(0, tracker.Total);
    }
}

public sealed class OriginPolicyTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(null, "chaos.velixir.run", true)]
    [InlineData("https://chaos.velixir.run", "chaos.velixir.run", true)]
    [InlineData("http://localhost:5216", "localhost:5216", true)]
    [InlineData("https://chaos.velixir.run", "internal:8080", true)]
    [InlineData("https://CHAOS.velixir.run/", "internal:8080", true)]
    [InlineData("https://evil.example", "chaos.velixir.run", false)]
    [InlineData("null", "chaos.velixir.run", false)]
    [InlineData("file:///etc", "chaos.velixir.run", false)]
    public void AllowsOnlyThisSite(string? origin, string host, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, OriginPolicy.IsAllowed(origin, host, ["https://chaos.velixir.run"]));
    }
}

public sealed class ClientAddressTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("203.0.113.7", "203.0.113.7")]
    [InlineData("::ffff:203.0.113.7", "203.0.113.7")]
    [InlineData("2001:db8:1:2:3:4:5:6", "2001:db8:1:2::/64")]
    public void GroupsAddressesSensibly(string address, string expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, ClientAddress.Key(IPAddress.Parse(address)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MissingAddressesShareOneKey()
    {
        Assert.Equal(ClientAddress.Unknown, ClientAddress.Key(null));
    }
}

public sealed class CookielessAntiforgeryTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void NeverIssuesTokens()
    {
        var tokens = new CookielessAntiforgery().GetAndStoreTokens(new DefaultHttpContext());
        Assert.Null(tokens.RequestToken);
        Assert.Null(tokens.CookieToken);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RejectsUnsafeRequests()
    {
        var antiforgery = new CookielessAntiforgery();
        var get = new DefaultHttpContext();
        get.Request.Method = HttpMethods.Get;
        var post = new DefaultHttpContext();
        post.Request.Method = HttpMethods.Post;

        Assert.True(await antiforgery.IsRequestValidAsync(get));
        Assert.False(await antiforgery.IsRequestValidAsync(post));
        await antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync(get);
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<AntiforgeryValidationException>(() => antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync(post));
    }
}

public sealed class ModulePathTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Components/App.razor.js", "./Components/App.razor.js")]
    [InlineData("./Components/App.razor.js", "./Components/App.razor.js")]
    [InlineData("/Components/App.razor.js", "/Components/App.razor.js")]
    public void MakesModulePathsRelative(string asset, string expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, ModulePath.Of(asset));
    }
}

public sealed class ChaosOptionsTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreValid()
    {
        Assert.True(new ChaosOptions().IsValid());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsImpossibleLimits()
    {
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { MaxConnections = 1, MaxConnectionsPerClient = 2 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { BoardCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { CanvasMaxPoints = 10 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SlotCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SlotWinnerCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SlotJackpotCapacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SlotDrawSymbols = "" }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SlotDrawSymbols = "abc" }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { PressBurst = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SpinMilliseconds = 500 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new ChaosOptions { SpinMilliseconds = 60_000 }.IsValid());
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Latency;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class LatencyRunTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("google=12,3;aws-us-east-1=40,")]
    [InlineData("chaos=,")]
    [InlineData("a=0,60000")]
    public void ValidRunsRoundTrip(string text)
    {
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.IsValid(text));
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.TryParse(text, out var results));
        Assert.Equal(text, LatencyRun.Format(results));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData(null)]
    [InlineData("google")]
    [InlineData("google=1")]
    [InlineData("google=01,2")]
    [InlineData("Google=1,2")]
    [InlineData("a=1,2;a=3,4")]
    [InlineData("a=60001,1")]
    [InlineData("a=-1,1")]
    [InlineData("a=1,2,3")]
    [InlineData("=1,2")]
    [InlineData("a=1.5,2")]
    public void InvalidRunsAreRejected(string? text) => Assert.False(LatencyRun.IsValid(text));

    [Fact]
    public void SummariesUseMediansAndCountAnswers()
    {
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.TryParse("a=10,1;b=30,;c=,5;d=,", out var results));
        Assert.Equal(new LatencySummary(20, 3, 3, 4), LatencyRun.Summarize(results));
        Assert.Equal(2, LatencyRun.Median([1, 2]));
        Assert.Equal(2, LatencyRun.Median([3, null, 1, 2]));
        Assert.Null(LatencyRun.Median([null]));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("a=10,1;b=30,;c=,5;d=,", 20, 3)]
    [InlineData("a=5,;b=,;c=,", null, null)]
    [InlineData("a=5,200;b=7,150;c=,", 6, 175)]
    [InlineData("chaos=3,", 3, null)]
    public void RanksNeedHalfTheEndpointsToAnswer(string text, int? browser, int? server)
    {
        Assert.Equal(new LatencyRank(browser, server), LatencyRun.Rank(text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InvalidRunsHaveNoRank() => Assert.Equal(LatencyRank.None, LatencyRun.Rank("nope"));

    [Fact]
    public void ComposeSkipsServerTimingsForBrowserOnlyEndpoints()
    {
        LatencyEndpoint[] endpoints = [new("self", "Self", "/time", FromServer: false), new("x", "X", "https://x.example/")];
        Assert.Equal("self=5,;x=6,7", LatencyRun.Compose(endpoints, [5, 6], [9, 7]));
        Assert.Equal("self=,;x=60000,", LatencyRun.Compose(endpoints, [null, 99_999], []));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheCatalogIsUsableInRunsAndThePolicy()
    {
        Assert.Equal(LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Count, LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Select(endpoint => endpoint.Key).Distinct().Count());
        Assert.True(LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Count <= LatencyRun.MaxEntries);
        var text = LatencyRun.Compose(LatencyCatalog.Endpoints, [.. LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Select(_ => (int?)60_000)], [.. LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Select(_ => (int?)60_000)]);
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.IsValid(text));
        Assert.All(LatencyCatalog.Origins, origin => Assert.StartsWith("https://", origin, StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Equal("Google", LatencyCatalog.LabelOf("google"));
        Assert.Equal("unknown-key", LatencyCatalog.LabelOf("unknown-key"));
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Latency;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class LatencyServiceTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Start = new(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(Start);

    private static int Count => LatencyCatalog.Endpoints.Count;

    [Fact]
    public async Task ARunCombinesBrowserAndServerTimings()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => [.. endpoints.Select(_ => (int?)7)]));
        var viewer = new LatencyRecorder();
        latency.Subscribe(viewer);

        var start = latency.Begin("a");
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Accepted, start.Result);
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(start.Id));
        Assert.True(latency.Running);
        Assert.Equal("state", viewer.Updates[^1].Kind);
        Assert.True(viewer.Updates[^1].Running);

        Assert.True(latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(12, Count)]));
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        Assert.False(latency.Running);
        var run = Assert.Single(latency.Messages);
        Assert.Equal(start.Id, run.Id);
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.IsValid(run.Text));
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.TryParse(run.Text, out var results));
        Assert.Equal(Count, results.Count);
        Assert.All(results, result => Assert.Equal(12, result.Browser));
        Assert.Null(results.Single(result => result.Key == "chaos").Server);
        Assert.Equal(7, results.Single(result => result.Key == "google").Server);
        var delta = viewer.Updates[^1];
        Assert.Equal("delta", delta.Kind);
        Assert.False(delta.Running);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OnlyOneRunAtATime()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]), o => o.LatencyGapSeconds = 0);
        var first = latency.Begin("a");
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Busy, latency.Begin("b").Result);
        latency.Finish(first.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(-1, Count)]);
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Accepted, latency.Begin("b").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RunsAreSpacedOutAndEachClientWaitsItsTurn()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]));
        var first = latency.Begin("a");
        latency.Finish(first.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(-1, Count)]);
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Busy, latency.Begin("b").Result);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10));
        var again = latency.Begin("a");
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Wait, again.Result);
        Assert.Equal("wait 290", again.Reply);
        var other = latency.Begin("b");
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Accepted, other.Result);
        Assert.StartsWith($"ok {other.Id:D} 300", other.Reply, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        latency.Finish(other.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(-1, Count)]);
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(290));
        Assert.Equal(StartResult.Accepted, latency.Begin("a").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FinishChecksTheRunAndTheValues()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]));
        var start = latency.Begin("a");
        Assert.False(latency.Finish(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start), [.. Enumerable.Repeat(1, Count)]));
        Assert.False(latency.Finish(start.Id, [1, 2]));
        Assert.False(latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(-2, Count)]));
        Assert.False(latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(LatencyRun.MaxMilliseconds + 1, Count)]));
        Assert.True(latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(1, Count)]));
        Assert.False(latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(1, Count)]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheServerProbeCanBeTurnedOff()
    {
        var probe = new FakeProbe(endpoints => [.. endpoints.Select(_ => (int?)1)]);
        using var latency = Create(probe, o => o.LatencyServerProbe = false);
        var start = latency.Begin("a");
        latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(5, Count)]);
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
        Assert.Equal(0, probe.Calls);
        Assert.True(LatencyRun.TryParse(Assert.Single(latency.Messages).Text, out var results));
        Assert.All(results, result => Assert.Null(result.Server));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OffersAreValidatedLikeBoardMessages()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]));
        var old = Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1));
        Assert.Equal(1, latency.Offer([new BoardMessage(old, "google=12,3")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, latency.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-2)), "google=012,3")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, latency.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(1)), "google=12,3")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, latency.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-3)), "hello")]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SnapshotsSayWhetherARunIsGoing()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]));
        latency.Begin("a");
        var late = new LatencyRecorder();
        latency.Subscribe(late);
        Assert.True(Assert.Single(late.Updates).Running);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AMeasuredRunJoinsTheLeaderboards()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => [.. endpoints.Select(_ => (int?)150)]));
        var viewer = new LatencyRecorder();
        latency.Subscribe(viewer);
        Assert.Empty(viewer.Updates[0].Boards!.Runs);

        var start = latency.Begin("a");
        latency.Finish(start.Id, [.. Enumerable.Repeat(8, Count)]);
        await latency.WhenIdle().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        var leaders = latency.Leaders;
        Assert.Equal(start.Id, Assert.Single(leaders.FastJackpots).Id);
        Assert.Equal(start.Id, Assert.Single(leaders.SlowJackpots).Id);
        Assert.Single(leaders.BrowserFastest);
        Assert.Single(leaders.ServerSlowest);
        var boards = viewer.Updates[^1].Boards!;
        Assert.Equal(start.Id, Assert.Single(boards.Runs).Id);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { start.Id }, boards.BrowserSlowest);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LeaderOffersFillTheBoardsButNotTheResults()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]), o => o.LatencyRankCapacity = 2);
        var viewer = new LatencyRecorder();
        latency.Subscribe(viewer);
        var offered = Enumerable.Range(1, 5)
            .Select(i => new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-i)), $"a={i * 10},;b=,"))
            .ToList();

        Assert.Equal(4, latency.OfferLeaders(offered));
        Assert.Empty(latency.Messages);
        var leaders = latency.Leaders;
        Assert.Equal(new[] { offered[0].Id, offered[1].Id }, leaders.BrowserFastest.Select(run => run.Id));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { offered[4].Id, offered[3].Id }, leaders.BrowserSlowest.Select(run => run.Id));
        Assert.Empty(leaders.ServerFastest);
        Assert.Empty(leaders.FastJackpots);
        Assert.Empty(leaders.SlowJackpots);
        Assert.Equal(0, latency.OfferLeaders(offered));
        Assert.Equal(4, viewer.Updates[^1].Boards!.Runs.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RunsWithoutAQuorumAreNotRanked()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]));
        Assert.Equal(0, latency.OfferLeaders([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1)), "a=1,;b=,;c=,")]));
        Assert.Equal(1, latency.Offer([new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-2)), "a=1,;b=,;c=,")]));
        Assert.Empty(latency.Leaders.BrowserFastest);
        Assert.Empty(latency.Leaders.FastJackpots);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void JackpotsKeepTheNewestAndRanksBreakTiesByAge()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]), o => o.LatencyJackpotCapacity = 2);
        var older = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-3)), "a=5,");
        var old = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-2)), "a=5,");
        var recent = new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-1)), "a=9,");
        latency.OfferLeaders([old, recent, older]);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { recent.Id, old.Id }, latency.Leaders.FastJackpots.Select(run => run.Id));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { older.Id, old.Id, recent.Id }, latency.Leaders.BrowserFastest.Select(run => run.Id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LeaderOffersAreLimitedPerCall()
    {
        using var latency = Create(new FakeProbe(endpoints => new int?[endpoints.Count]), o => o.LatencyRankCapacity = 100);
        var offered = Enumerable.Range(1, LatencyService.MaxLeaderOffers + 5)
            .Select(i => new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(-i)), $"a={i},"));
        Assert.Equal(LatencyService.MaxLeaderOffers, latency.OfferLeaders(offered));
    }

    private LatencyService Create(ILatencyProbe probe, Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null) =>
        new(_time, Limits.Create(configure), probe, NullLogger<LatencyService>.Instance);
}

using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class MessageMetaTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Keys =
    [
        "latitude", "longitude", "accuracy", "altitude", "altitudeAccuracy", "heading", "speed", "positionAt",
        "timeZone", "utcOffset", "localTime", "locale", "calendar", "numberingSystem", "language", "languages",
        "userAgent", "brands", "platform", "mobile", "vendor", "webdriver", "doNotTrack", "globalPrivacyControl",
        "pdfViewer", "screen", "availableScreen", "viewport", "pixelRatio", "colorDepth", "orientation", "cores",
        "memory", "touchPoints", "pointer", "colorScheme", "reducedMotion", "contrast", "online", "connection",
        "downlink", "rtt", "saveData",
    ];

    [Fact]
    public void TheCatalogMatchesTheBrowserModule()
    {
        Assert.Equal(Keys, MessageMeta.Fields.Select(field => field.Key));
        Assert.Equal(Keys.Length, MessageMeta.Fields.Select(field => field.Label).Distinct().Count());
        Assert.Equal("Latitude", MessageMeta.LabelOf("latitude"));
        Assert.Equal("CPU threads", MessageMeta.LabelOf("cores"));
        Assert.Equal("mystery", MessageMeta.LabelOf("mystery"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizeKeepsKnownKeysInCatalogOrder()
    {
        var normalized = MessageMeta.Normalize(
        [
            new("timeZone", " Asia/Kathmandu "),
            new("mystery", "dropped"),
            new("longitude", "85.324"),
            new("latitude", "27.7172"),
            new("latitude", "1"),
            null,
        ]);

        Assert.NotNull(normalized);
        Assert.Equal(
            [new MetaEntry("latitude", "27.7172"), new MetaEntry("longitude", "85.324"), new MetaEntry("timeZone", "Asia/Kathmandu")],
            normalized);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizeNeedsLatitudeAndLongitude()
    {
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize(null));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "1")]));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "91"), new("longitude", "1")]));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "NaN")]));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "north"), new("longitude", "1")]));
        Assert.NotNull(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "-90"), new("longitude", "180")]));
        Assert.NotNull(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", "1e-7"), new("longitude", "-0.5")]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizeDropsOutOfRangeNumbersAndBlankText()
    {
        var normalized = MessageMeta.Normalize(
        [
            new("latitude", "1"),
            new("longitude", "2"),
            new("accuracy", "-1"),
            new("heading", "361"),
            new("cores", "8"),
            new("language", "   "),
            new("platform", "\u0000\u0007"),
        ]);

        Assert.Equal(["latitude", "longitude", "cores"], normalized!.Select(entry => entry.Key));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizeStripsControlCharactersAndCutsLongValues()
    {
        var agent = new string('a', 600);
        var normalized = MessageMeta.Normalize(
        [
            new("latitude", "1"),
            new("longitude", "2"),
            new("userAgent", agent),
            new("vendor", "Goo\u0000gle\nInc."),
            new("utcOffset", "+05:45:00:00"),
            new("timeZone", new string('x', 63) + "😀"),
        ]);

        var values = normalized!.ToDictionary(entry => entry.Key, entry => entry.Value);
        Assert.Equal(512, values["userAgent"].Length);
        Assert.Equal("GoogleInc.", values["vendor"]);
        Assert.Equal("+05:45:0", values["utcOffset"]);
        Assert.Equal(new string('x', 63), values["timeZone"]);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizeIgnoresNullValuesAndEndlessInput()
    {
        var flood = Enumerable.Range(0, 10_000).Select(i => new MetaEntry($"k{i}", "v")).Prepend(new MetaEntry("latitude", "1"));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize(flood.Append(new MetaEntry("longitude", "2"))));
        Assert.Null(MessageMeta.Normalize([new("latitude", null!), new("longitude", "2")]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void IsNormalizedAcceptsOnlyTheCanonicalForm()
    {
        MetaEntry[] canonical = [new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2"), new("timeZone", "UTC")];
        Assert.True(MessageMeta.IsNormalized(null));
        Assert.True(MessageMeta.IsNormalized(canonical));
        Assert.False(MessageMeta.IsNormalized([]));
        Assert.False(MessageMeta.IsNormalized([new("longitude", "2"), new("latitude", "1")]));
        Assert.False(MessageMeta.IsNormalized([new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2"), new("timeZone", " UTC")]));
        Assert.False(MessageMeta.IsNormalized([new("latitude", "1"), new("longitude", "2"), new("mystery", "x")]));
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class MessageTextTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(null)]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData("   \n\t ")]
    [InlineData("\u200B\u200B")]
    public void BlankInputBecomesEmpty(string? input)
    {
        Assert.Equal("", MessageText.Normalize(input));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StripsControlCharactersButKeepsNewlines()
    {
        Assert.Equal("a\nb", MessageText.Normalize("a\u0007\r\nb\u0000"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CollapsesRunsOfBlankLines()
    {
        Assert.Equal("a\n\nb", MessageText.Normalize("a\n\n\n\n\nb"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TrimsSurroundingWhitespace()
    {
        Assert.Equal("hi", MessageText.Normalize("  hi \n"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsTextAtTheLimit()
    {
        var input = new string('x', MessageText.MaxLength);
        Assert.Equal(input, MessageText.Normalize(input));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TruncatesOnGraphemeBoundaries()
    {
        var prefix = new string('a', MessageText.MaxLength - 3);
        Assert.Equal(prefix, MessageText.Normalize(prefix + "👨‍👩‍👧‍👦"));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("hello", true)]
    [InlineData(" hello", false)]
    [InlineData("a\n\n\nb", false)]
    [InlineData("", false)]
    public void RecognisesNormalizedText(string text, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, MessageText.IsNormalized(text));
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class OutboxTests
{
    private static CancellationToken Cancellation => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Fact]
    public async Task DeliversEverythingInOrder()
    {
        var received = new List<int>();
        var gate = new Lock();
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(1000, 16, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), (batch, _) =>
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                received.AddRange(batch);
            }

            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }, NullLogger.Instance);

        for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++)
        {
            Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(i));
        }

        await Eventually(() =>
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                return received.Count == 200;
            }
        });

        Assert.Equal(Enumerable.Range(0, 200), received);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RefusesItemsWhenFull()
    {
        var started = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        var release = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(2, 1, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30), async (_, _) =>
        {
            started.TrySetResult();
            await release.Task;
        }, NullLogger.Instance);

        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), Cancellation);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(2));
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(3));
        Assert.False(outbox.TryPost(4));
        release.TrySetResult();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FaultsWhenSendingFails()
    {
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(10, 1, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("gone"), NullLogger.Instance);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await Eventually(() => outbox.IsFaulted);
        Assert.False(outbox.TryPost(2));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FaultsWhenTheClientIsTooSlow()
    {
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(10, 1, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(50), (_, token) => Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, token), NullLogger.Instance);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await Eventually(() => outbox.IsFaulted);
    }

    private static async Task Eventually(Func<bool> condition)
    {
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 200 && !condition(); attempt++)
        {
            await Task.Delay(25, Cancellation);
        }

        Assert.True(condition());
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Slots;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class SlotRulesTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("7Q7A7", nameof(SlotWin.ThreeOfAKind))]
    [InlineData("XXXXB", nameof(SlotWin.FourOfAKind))]
    [InlineData("KKKKK", nameof(SlotWin.FiveOfAKind))]
    [InlineData("13AKZ", nameof(SlotWin.StraightUp))]
    [InlineData("ZKA80", nameof(SlotWin.StraightDown))]
    [InlineData("ABCDF", nameof(SlotWin.StraightUp))]
    [InlineData("ABCDE", nameof(SlotWin.SequenceUp))]
    [InlineData("789AB", nameof(SlotWin.SequenceUp))]
    [InlineData("VWXYZ", nameof(SlotWin.SequenceUp))]
    [InlineData("43210", nameof(SlotWin.SequenceDown))]
    [InlineData("BA987", nameof(SlotWin.SequenceDown))]
    [InlineData("YZ012", nameof(SlotWin.None))]
    [InlineData("ZK8A0", nameof(SlotWin.None))]
    [InlineData("AABBC", nameof(SlotWin.None))]
    [InlineData("ABCDD", nameof(SlotWin.None))]
    [InlineData("abcde", nameof(SlotWin.None))]
    public void ClassifiesSpins(string text, string expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, SlotRules.Classify(text).ToString());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("A1B2C", true)]
    [InlineData("a1b2c", false)]
    [InlineData("ABCD", false)]
    [InlineData("ABCDEF", false)]
    [InlineData("AB-CD", false)]
    [InlineData(null, false)]
    public void ValidatesSymbols(string? text, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, SlotRules.IsValid(text));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("XXXXB", true)]
    [InlineData("KKKKK", true)]
    [InlineData("7Q7A7", false)]
    [InlineData("ABCDE", true)]
    [InlineData("43210", true)]
    [InlineData("VWXYZ", true)]
    [InlineData("13AKZ", false)]
    [InlineData("YZ012", false)]
    [InlineData("ZK8A0", false)]
    [InlineData("kkkkk", false)]
    [InlineData(null, false)]
    public void KnowsJackpots(string? text, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, SlotRules.IsJackpot(text));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("7", true)]
    [InlineData("0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ", true)]
    [InlineData("", false)]
    [InlineData(null, false)]
    [InlineData("a", false)]
    [InlineData("0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0", false)]
    public void ValidatesDrawAlphabets(string? alphabet, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, SlotRules.IsAlphabet(alphabet));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DrawsFromTheGivenAlphabet()
    {
        Assert.Equal("77777", SlotRules.Draw("7"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DrawsValidSpins()
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++)
        {
            Assert.True(SlotRules.IsValid(SlotRules.Draw()));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MarksWinningSymbols()
    {
        Assert.Equal([true, false, true, false, true], SlotRules.Hits("7Q7A7"));
        Assert.Equal([true, true, true, true, true], SlotRules.Hits("ABCDE"));
        Assert.Equal([true, true, true, true, true], SlotRules.Hits("43210"));
        Assert.Equal([false, false, false, false, false], SlotRules.Hits("AABBC"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NeighboursWrapAroundTheStrip()
    {
        Assert.Equal('0', SlotRules.Above('Z'));
        Assert.Equal('Z', SlotRules.Below('0'));
        Assert.Equal('A', SlotRules.Above('9'));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryWinHasALabel()
    {
        foreach (var win in Enum.GetValues<SlotWin>())
        {
            Assert.Equal(win is SlotWin.None, SlotRules.Label(win).Length == 0);
        }
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Chaos.Web.Slots;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class SlotServiceTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Start = new(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(Start);

    [Fact]
    public void PressStartsASpinStampedWithTheServerClock()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        var press = slots.Press("a");
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, press.Result);
        var result = Assert.Single(slots.Messages);
        Assert.Equal(press.Id, result.Id);
        Assert.True(SlotRules.IsValid(result.Text));
        Assert.Equal(Start, Uuid7.Timestamp(result.Id));
        Assert.Equal(press.Id.ToString(), press.Reply);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NobodyCanPressUntilTheSpinSettles()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Busy, slots.Press("b").Result);
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Busy, slots.Press("a").Result);
        Assert.Equal("busy", slots.Press("b").Reply);
        _time.Advance(slots.SpinTime - TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1));
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Busy, slots.Press("b").Result);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1));
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("b").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BusyPressesDoNotSpendTheClientsAllowance()
    {
        var slots = Create(o =>
        {
            o.PressBurst = 1;
            o.PressesPerSecond = 0.01;
        });
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("a").Result);
        for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(PressResult.Busy, slots.Press("b").Result);
        }

        _time.Advance(slots.SpinTime);
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("b").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PressIsRateLimitedPerClient()
    {
        var slots = Create(o =>
        {
            o.PressBurst = 1;
            o.PressesPerSecond = 0.1;
        });
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("a").Result);
        _time.Advance(slots.SpinTime);
        var press = slots.Press("a");
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.TooFast, press.Result);
        Assert.Equal("slow", press.Reply);
        Assert.Equal(PressResult.Accepted, slots.Press("b").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SpinningReportsTheTimeLeft()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SpinMilliseconds = 4000);
        Assert.Null(slots.Spinning);
        var press = slots.Press("a");
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1500));
        var spin = Assert.IsType<SlotSpin>(slots.Spinning);
        Assert.Equal(press.Id, spin.Id);
        Assert.Equal(2500, spin.Remaining);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(2500));
        Assert.Null(slots.Spinning);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubscribersSeeTheSpinAndLateJoinersSeeWhatIsLeft()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SpinMilliseconds = 4000);
        var early = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(early);
        var press = slots.Press("a");

        var delta = early.Updates[1];
        Assert.Equal("delta", delta.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(press.Id, Assert.Single(delta.Added).Id);
        Assert.Equal(new SlotSpin(press.Id, 4000), delta.Spin);

        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        var late = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(late);
        var snapshot = Assert.Single(late.Updates);
        Assert.Equal("snapshot", snapshot.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(new SlotSpin(press.Id, 3000), snapshot.Spin);

        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3));
        var settled = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(settled);
        Assert.Null(Assert.Single(settled.Updates).Spin);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsOnlyTheNewestResults()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotCapacity = 2);
        var ids = new List<Guid>();
        for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            ids.Add(slots.Press($"c{i}").Id);
            _time.Advance(slots.SpinTime);
        }

        Assert.Equal([ids[2], ids[1]], slots.Messages.Select(m => m.Id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferAcceptsOlderValidResults()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.Equal(2, slots.Offer([Old(-2, "ABCDE"), Old(-1, "ZZZ12")]));
        Assert.Equal(["ZZZ12", "ABCDE"], slots.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
        Assert.Null(recorder.Updates[1].Spin);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferRejectsForgedResults()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(1));
        Assert.Equal(0, slots.Offer(
        [
            new BoardMessage(Guid.NewGuid(), "ABCDE"),
            new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(5)), "ABCDE"),
            new BoardMessage(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddHours(2)), "ABCDE"),
            Old(-1, "abcde"),
            Old(-2, "ABCDEF"),
            Old(-3, "hello world"),
        ]));
        Assert.Empty(slots.Messages);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferConsidersAtMostFiftyResults()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotCapacity = 100);
        var offered = Enumerable.Range(1, 60).Select(i => Old(-i, "ABCDE")).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(SlotService.MaxOffers, slots.Offer(offered));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WinnersOutliveTheRecentResults()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotCapacity = 2);
        Assert.Equal(3, slots.Offer([Old(-3, "AAAB1"), Old(-2, "ZK8A0"), Old(-1, "ZK8A1")]));
        Assert.Equal(["ZK8A1", "ZK8A0"], slots.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
        Assert.Equal("AAAB1", Assert.Single(slots.Winners).Text);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsOnlyTheNewestWinners()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 2);
        slots.Offer([Old(-3, "AAAB1"), Old(-2, "13AKZ"), Old(-1, "ZKA80"), Old(-4, "ZK8A0")]);
        Assert.Equal(["ZKA80", "13AKZ"], slots.Winners.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferedWinnersSkipTheRecentResults()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.Equal(1, slots.OfferWinners([Old(-2, "ZK8A0"), Old(-1, "KKKKK")]));
        Assert.Empty(slots.Messages);
        Assert.Equal("KKKKK", Assert.Single(slots.Winners).Text);
        var delta = recorder.Updates[1];
        Assert.Empty(delta.Added);
        Assert.Equal("KKKKK", Assert.Single(delta.WinnersAdded).Text);
        Assert.Equal(0, slots.OfferWinners([Old(-2, "ZK8A0")]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DeltasReportWinnersThatFallOff()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 1);
        var first = Old(-2, "AAAB1");
        slots.Offer([first]);
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.Equal(first.Id, Assert.Single(recorder.Updates[0].WinnersAdded).Id);
        slots.Offer([Old(-1, "13AKZ")]);
        Assert.Equal(first.Id, Assert.Single(recorder.Updates[1].WinnersRemoved));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WinnerOffersConsiderFiveHundred()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 1000);
        var offered = Enumerable.Range(1, 600).Select(i => Old(-i, "13AKZ")).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(SlotService.MaxWinnerOffers, slots.OfferWinners(offered));
        Assert.Equal(500, slots.Winners.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void JackpotsOutliveEveryOtherList()
    {
        var slots = Create(o =>
        {
            o.SlotCapacity = 1;
            o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 1;
        });
        slots.Offer([Old(-5, "XXXXB"), Old(-4, "KKKKK"), Old(-3, "13AKZ"), Old(-2, "AAAB1"), Old(-1, "ZK8A0")]);
        Assert.Equal(["ZK8A0"], slots.Messages.Select(m => m.Text));
        Assert.Equal(["AAAB1"], slots.Winners.Select(m => m.Text));
        Assert.Equal(["KKKKK", "XXXXB"], slots.Jackpots.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferedJackpotsOnlyTouchTheJackpots()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        var jackpot = Old(-1, "QQQQ7");
        Assert.Equal(1, slots.OfferJackpots([Old(-3, "AAAB1"), Old(-2, "13AKZ"), jackpot]));
        Assert.Empty(slots.Messages);
        Assert.Empty(slots.Winners);
        Assert.Equal("QQQQ7", Assert.Single(slots.Jackpots).Text);
        var delta = recorder.Updates[1];
        Assert.Empty(delta.Added);
        Assert.Empty(delta.WinnersAdded);
        Assert.Equal("QQQQ7", Assert.Single(delta.JackpotsAdded).Text);
        Assert.Equal(0, slots.OfferJackpots([jackpot]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SequencesAreKeptAsJackpots()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        Assert.Equal(2, slots.OfferJackpots([Old(-3, "13AKZ"), Old(-2, "ABCDE"), Old(-1, "43210")]));
        Assert.Equal(["43210", "ABCDE"], slots.Jackpots.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OfferedJackpotsStopAtCapacityWithoutEvicting()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotJackpotCapacity = 2);
        var oldest = Old(-9, "KKKKK");
        Assert.Equal(2, slots.OfferJackpots([oldest, Old(-8, "XXXXB")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, slots.OfferJackpots([Old(-1, "77777")]));
        Assert.Contains(slots.Jackpots, m => m.Id == oldest.Id);
        Assert.Equal(2, slots.Jackpots.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DrawnJackpotsAreAlwaysKept()
    {
        var slots = Create(o =>
        {
            o.SlotDrawSymbols = "7";
            o.SlotCapacity = 1;
            o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 1;
            o.SlotJackpotCapacity = 1;
        });
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        var first = slots.Press("a");
        _time.Advance(slots.SpinTime);
        var second = slots.Press("a");
        Assert.Equal("77777", slots.Messages[0].Text);
        Assert.Equal([second.Id, first.Id], slots.Jackpots.Select(m => m.Id));
        Assert.Equal(second.Id, Assert.Single(recorder.Updates[^1].JackpotsAdded).Id);
        Assert.Equal(second.Id, recorder.Updates[^1].Spin!.Id);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SnapshotsCarryEveryJackpot()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotWinnerCapacity = 1);
        slots.Offer([Old(-2, "XXXXB"), Old(-1, "KKKKK")]);
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder();
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        var snapshot = Assert.Single(recorder.Updates);
        Assert.Single(snapshot.WinnersAdded);
        Assert.Equal(["KKKKK", "XXXXB"], snapshot.JackpotsAdded.Select(m => m.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnInvalidDrawAlphabetFallsBackToEverySymbol()
    {
        var slots = Create(o => o.SlotDrawSymbols = "abc");
        slots.Press("a");
        Assert.True(SlotRules.IsValid(slots.Messages[0].Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubscribersThatCannotKeepUpAreDropped()
    {
        var slots = Create();
        var recorder = new SlotRecorder(accept: false);
        slots.Subscribe(recorder);
        Assert.False(slots.IsSubscribed(recorder));
    }

    private SlotService Create(Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null) => new(_time, Limits.Create(configure));

    private static BoardMessage Old(int minutes, string text) =>
        new(Guid.CreateVersion7(Start.AddMinutes(minutes)), text);
}

using Chaos.Web.Drawing;
using Chaos.Web.Infrastructure;
using Chaos.Web.Latency;
using Chaos.Web.Messages;
using Chaos.Web.Slots;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

internal sealed class ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset start) : TimeProvider
{
    private DateTimeOffset _now = start;
    private long _timestamp;

    public override long TimestampFrequency => TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond;

    public override DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => _now;

    public override long GetTimestamp() => _timestamp;

    public void Advance(TimeSpan by)
    {
        _now += by;
        _timestamp += by.Ticks;
    }
}

internal static class Limits
{
    public static IOptions<ChaosOptions> Create(Action<ChaosOptions>? configure = null)
    {
        var options = new ChaosOptions();
        configure?.Invoke(options);
        return Options.Create(options);
    }
}

internal sealed class BoardRecorder(bool accept = true) : IBoardSubscriber
{
    public List<BoardUpdate> Updates { get; } = [];

    public bool Deliver(BoardUpdate update)
    {
        if (accept)
        {
            Updates.Add(update);
        }

        return accept;
    }
}

internal sealed class SlotRecorder(bool accept = true) : ISlotSubscriber
{
    public List<SlotUpdate> Updates { get; } = [];

    public bool Deliver(SlotUpdate update)
    {
        if (accept)
        {
            Updates.Add(update);
        }

        return accept;
    }
}

internal sealed class CanvasRecorder(bool accept = true) : ICanvasSubscriber
{
    public List<CanvasUpdate> Updates { get; } = [];

    public bool Deliver(CanvasUpdate update)
    {
        if (accept)
        {
            Updates.Add(update);
        }

        return accept;
    }
}

internal sealed class LatencyRecorder(bool accept = true) : ILatencySubscriber
{
    public List<LatencyUpdate> Updates { get; } = [];

    public bool Deliver(LatencyUpdate update)
    {
        if (accept)
        {
            lock (Updates)
            {
                Updates.Add(update);
            }
        }

        return accept;
    }
}

internal sealed class FakeProbe(Func<IReadOnlyList<LatencyEndpoint>, int?[]> measure) : ILatencyProbe
{
    public int Calls { get; private set; }

    public Task<int?[]> MeasureAsync(IReadOnlyList<LatencyEndpoint> endpoints, TimeSpan budget, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        Calls++;
        return Task.FromResult(measure(endpoints));
    }
}

using Chaos.Web.Messages;

namespace Chaos.Web.Tests;

public sealed class Uuid7Tests
{
    [Fact]
    public void ReadsTheTimestampBack()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 23, 18, 42, 123, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var id = Guid.CreateVersion7(at);
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(id));
        Assert.Equal(at, Uuid7.Timestamp(id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ReadsAHandWrittenId()
    {
        var id = Guid.Parse("016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001");
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(id));
        Assert.Equal(new DateTimeOffset(2020, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero), Uuid7.Timestamp(id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsOtherVersions()
    {
        Assert.False(Uuid7.IsValid(Guid.NewGuid()));
        Assert.False(Uuid7.IsValid(Guid.Empty));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GuidOrderMatchesStringOrder()
    {
        var start = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var ids = Enumerable.Range(0, 300).Select(i => Guid.CreateVersion7(start.AddMilliseconds(i * 7919 % 1000))).ToList();
        var byGuid = ids.Order().Select(id => id.ToString()).ToList();
        var byString = ids.Select(id => id.ToString()).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(byString, byGuid);
    }
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    MAX_RETRIES,
    THEME_KEY,
    better,
    clockLabel,
    reconnectDelay,
    sample,
    storedTheme,
    toggledTheme,
    uncertainty,
    untilNextSecond,
    zoneLabel,
} from '../../src/Chaos.Web/Components/App.razor.js';

describe('reconnectDelay', () => {
    it('retries the first attempt at once', () => {
        expect(reconnectDelay(0)).toBe(0);
    });

    it('doubles the ceiling and jitters inside its upper half', () => {
        expect(reconnectDelay(1, () => 0)).toBe(500);
        expect(reconnectDelay(1, () => 1)).toBe(1000);
        expect(reconnectDelay(3, () => 0.5)).toBe(3000);
    });

    it('caps the wait at thirty seconds', () => {
        expect(reconnectDelay(20, () => 1)).toBe(30000);
        expect(reconnectDelay(20, () => 0)).toBe(15000);
    });

    it('keeps trying for more than five minutes', () => {
        let total = 0;
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_RETRIES; attempt += 1) {
            total += reconnectDelay(attempt, () => 0);
        }
        expect(total).toBeGreaterThan(5 * 60 * 1000);
    });
});

describe('theme', () => {
    const storage = value => ({ getItem: key => (key === THEME_KEY ? value : null) });

    it('reads only known themes', () => {
        expect(storedTheme(storage('dark'))).toBe('dark');
        expect(storedTheme(storage('light'))).toBe('light');
        expect(storedTheme(storage('purple'))).toBeNull();
        expect(storedTheme(undefined)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('survives storage that throws', () => {
        expect(storedTheme({ getItem() { throw new Error('denied'); } })).toBeNull();
    });

    it('flips the system preference when nothing is stored', () => {
        expect(toggledTheme(null, true)).toBe('light');
        expect(toggledTheme(null, false)).toBe('dark');
    });

    it('flips a stored choice regardless of the system', () => {
        expect(toggledTheme('dark', false)).toBe('light');
        expect(toggledTheme('light', true)).toBe('dark');
    });
});

describe('server clock', () => {
    it('labels the server time in the server zone', () => {
        const at = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 16, 8, 10, 39, 500);
        expect(clockLabel(at, 0)).toBe('2026-10-16 08:10:39 UTC');
        expect(clockLabel(at, 330)).toBe('2026-10-16 13:40:39 UTC+05:30');
        expect(clockLabel(at, -240)).toBe('2026-10-16 04:10:39 UTC-04:00');
    });

    it('formats zones', () => {
        expect(zoneLabel(0)).toBe('UTC');
        expect(zoneLabel(60)).toBe('UTC+01:00');
        expect(zoneLabel(-570)).toBe('UTC-09:30');
    });

    it('takes the middle of the round trip', () => {
        expect(sample(10_000, 100, 300)).toEqual({ offset: 9800, error: 100, at: 300, zone: 0 });
    });

    it('prefers tighter samples but lets old ones age out', () => {
        const old = { offset: 0, error: 10, at: 0, zone: 0 };
        expect(better({ error: 50 }, old, 1000)).toBe(false);
        expect(better({ error: 50 }, old, 1_000_000)).toBe(true);
        expect(better({ error: 50 }, null, 0)).toBe(true);
        expect(uncertainty(null, 0)).toBe(Infinity);
    });

    it('wakes just after the next second', () => {
        expect(untilNextSecond(1000)).toBe(1005);
        expect(untilNextSecond(1999)).toBe(6);
        expect(untilNextSecond(1500.7)).toBe(505);
    });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    LOCAL_CAP,
    META_FIELDS,
    OFFER_LIMIT,
    SHARE_KEY,
    chunkOffers,
    collectMeta,
    countText,
    excessOf,
    formatOffset,
    isoOf,
    labelOf,
    localStamp,
    metaLabel,
    needsResync,
    newestFirst,
    readShare,
    shareDefault,
    timestampOf,
    toRecord,
    upgrade,
    writeShare,
} from '../../src/Chaos.Web/Components/Islands/BoardIsland.razor.js';

const OLD_ID = '016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001';

describe('message ids', () => {
    it('reads the UUIDv7 timestamp', () => {
        expect(timestampOf(OLD_ID)).toBe(Date.UTC(2020, 0, 1));
        expect(isoOf(OLD_ID)).toBe('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
    });

    it('labels today with the time and other days with the date', () => {
        expect(labelOf(OLD_ID, Date.UTC(2020, 0, 1, 12))).toBe('00:00:00');
        expect(labelOf(OLD_ID, Date.UTC(2026, 9, 12))).toBe('2020-01-01 00:00');
    });

    it('sorts newest first', () => {
        const messages = [{ id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000' }, { id: '0199b000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000' }, { id: OLD_ID }];
        expect(messages.sort(newestFirst).map(message => message.id)).toEqual([
            '0199b000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000',
            '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000',
            OLD_ID,
        ]);
    });
});

describe('chunkOffers', () => {
    const message = (index, text = 'hi') => ({ id: `0199a000-0000-7000-8000-${String(index).padStart(12, '0')}`, text });

    it('keeps small offers in one chunk and stops at the limit', () => {
        const messages = Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, index) => message(index));
        const chunks = chunkOffers(messages);
        expect(chunks).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(chunks[0]).toHaveLength(OFFER_LIMIT);
    });

    it('splits by size without reordering', () => {
        const messages = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, index) => message(index, 'x'.repeat(400)));
        const chunks = chunkOffers(messages, 1000);
        expect(chunks.every(chunk => chunk.length === 2)).toBe(true);
        expect(chunks.flat()).toEqual(messages);
    });

    it('skips a message that can never fit', () => {
        expect(chunkOffers([message(1, 'x'.repeat(2000)), message(2)], 1000)).toEqual([[message(2)]]);
    });

    it('counts details toward the size', () => {
        const meta = [{ key: 'userAgent', value: 'x'.repeat(300) }];
        const messages = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, index) => ({ ...message(index, 'x'.repeat(100)), meta }));
        expect(chunkOffers(messages, 1000).every(chunk => chunk.length === 2)).toBe(true);
        expect(chunkOffers(messages.map(({ meta: _, ...rest }) => rest), 1000)).toHaveLength(1);
    });
});

describe('bookkeeping', () => {
    it('asks for a resync when a delta skips a version', () => {
        expect(needsResync(4, { previous: 4 })).toBe(false);
        expect(needsResync(4, { previous: 5 })).toBe(true);
    });

    it('trims only beyond the local cap', () => {
        expect(excessOf(LOCAL_CAP)).toBe(0);
        expect(excessOf(LOCAL_CAP + 7)).toBe(7);
        expect(excessOf(3, 2)).toBe(1);
    });
});

describe('schema migrations', () => {
    it('creates the message store on a fresh database', () => {
        const created = [];
        upgrade({ createObjectStore: (name, options) => created.push([name, options]) }, 0);
        expect(created).toEqual([['messages', { keyPath: 'id' }]]);
    });

    it('runs only the steps after the stored version', () => {
        const ran = [];
        upgrade({}, 1, 3, [() => ran.push(1), () => ran.push(2), () => ran.push(3)]);
        expect(ran).toEqual([2, 3]);
    });
});

describe('countText', () => {
    it('shows the length against the limit', () => {
        expect(countText(0, 500)).toBe('0 / 500');
        expect(countText(137, 500)).toBe('137 / 500');
        expect(countText(3, -1)).toBe('3');
    });
});

describe('message details', () => {
    const keys = [
        'latitude', 'longitude', 'accuracy', 'altitude', 'altitudeAccuracy', 'heading', 'speed', 'positionAt',
        'timeZone', 'utcOffset', 'localTime', 'locale', 'calendar', 'numberingSystem', 'language', 'languages',
        'userAgent', 'brands', 'platform', 'mobile', 'vendor', 'webdriver', 'doNotTrack', 'globalPrivacyControl',
        'pdfViewer', 'screen', 'availableScreen', 'viewport', 'pixelRatio', 'colorDepth', 'orientation', 'cores',
        'memory', 'touchPoints', 'pointer', 'colorScheme', 'reducedMotion', 'contrast', 'online', 'connection',
