    ];

    const position = (coords = {}) => ({
        timestamp: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 30, 12),
        coords: { latitude: 27.7172, longitude: 85.324, accuracy: 35, altitude: null, altitudeAccuracy: null, heading: Number.NaN, speed: null, ...coords },
    });

    const browser = {
        navigator: {
            language: 'en-US',
            languages: ['en-US', 'ne'],
            userAgent: 'TestAgent/1.0',
            userAgentData: { brands: [{ brand: 'Chromium', version: '140' }], platform: 'Linux', mobile: false },
            platform: 'Linux x86_64',
            vendor: 'Google Inc.',
            webdriver: true,
            doNotTrack: '1',
            pdfViewerEnabled: true,
            hardwareConcurrency: 8,
            deviceMemory: 4,
            maxTouchPoints: 0,
            onLine: true,
            connection: { effectiveType: '4g', downlink: 10, rtt: 50, saveData: false },
        },
        screen: { width: 1920, height: 1080, availWidth: 1920, availHeight: 1040, colorDepth: 24, orientation: { type: 'landscape-primary' } },
        innerWidth: 1280,
        innerHeight: 720,
        devicePixelRatio: 1.5,
        matchMedia: query => ({ matches: ['(pointer: fine)', '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'].includes(query) }),
        Intl: { DateTimeFormat: () => ({ resolvedOptions: () => ({ timeZone: 'Asia/Kathmandu', locale: 'en-US', calendar: 'gregory', numberingSystem: 'latn' }) }) },
    };

    const now = { getTimezoneOffset: () => -345, getFullYear: () => 2026, getMonth: () => 9, getDate: () => 30, getHours: () => 17, getMinutes: () => 45, getSeconds: () => 3 };

    it('uses the same keys in the same order as the server', () => {
        expect(META_FIELDS.map(([key]) => key)).toEqual(keys);
        expect(new Set(META_FIELDS.map(([, label]) => label)).size).toBe(keys.length);
    });

    it('labels known keys and falls back to the key', () => {
        expect(metaLabel('latitude')).toBe('Latitude');
        expect(metaLabel('cores')).toBe('CPU threads');
        expect(metaLabel('mystery')).toBe('mystery');
    });

    it('needs a position', () => {
        expect(collectMeta(null, browser)).toBeNull();
        expect(collectMeta({ coords: { latitude: Number.NaN, longitude: 1 } }, browser)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('collects what the browser reports, in catalog order, without empty values', () => {
        const entries = collectMeta(position(), browser, now);
        const values = Object.fromEntries(entries.map(entry => [entry.key, entry.value]));
        expect(entries.map(entry => entry.key)).toEqual(keys.filter(key => key in values));
        expect(values).toMatchObject({
            latitude: '27.7172',
            longitude: '85.324',
            accuracy: '35',
            positionAt: '2026-10-30T12:00:00.000Z',
            timeZone: 'Asia/Kathmandu',
            utcOffset: '+05:45',
            localTime: '2026-10-30 17:45:03',
            languages: 'en-US, ne',
            brands: 'Chromium 140',
            platform: 'Linux',
            mobile: 'no',
            webdriver: 'yes',
            screen: '1920x1080',
            availableScreen: '1920x1040',
            viewport: '1280x720',
            pixelRatio: '1.5',
            orientation: 'landscape-primary',
            cores: '8',
            memory: '4',
            touchPoints: '0',
            pointer: 'fine',
            colorScheme: 'dark',
            reducedMotion: 'no',
            contrast: 'no preference',
            connection: '4g',
            downlink: '10',
            rtt: '50',
            saveData: 'no',
        });
        expect(values).not.toHaveProperty('altitude');
        expect(values).not.toHaveProperty('heading');
        expect(values).not.toHaveProperty('speed');
    });

    it('works in a bare browser', () => {
        const entries = collectMeta(position({ altitude: 1400, heading: 90, speed: 0 }), {}, now);
        const values = Object.fromEntries(entries.map(entry => [entry.key, entry.value]));
        expect(values).toMatchObject({ altitude: '1400', heading: '90', speed: '0', utcOffset: '+05:45' });
        expect(values).not.toHaveProperty('pointer');
        expect(values).not.toHaveProperty('userAgent');
    });

    it('formats UTC offsets', () => {
        expect(formatOffset(0)).toBe('+00:00');
        expect(formatOffset(345)).toBe('+05:45');
        expect(formatOffset(-240)).toBe('-04:00');
        expect(formatOffset(-570)).toBe('-09:30');
        expect(formatOffset(Number.NaN)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('stamps local time', () => {
        expect(localStamp(new Date(2026, 0, 2, 3, 4, 5))).toBe('2026-01-02 03:04:05');
    });

    it('stores details and ownership only when present', () => {
        const meta = [{ key: 'latitude', value: '1' }, { key: 'longitude', value: '2' }];
        expect(toRecord({ id: 'a', text: 't', meta }, true)).toEqual({ id: 'a', text: 't', meta, mine: true });
        expect(toRecord({ id: 'a', text: 't', meta: [] }, false)).toEqual({ id: 'a', text: 't' });
        expect(toRecord({ id: 'a', text: 't' }, false)).toEqual({ id: 'a', text: 't' });
    });
});

describe('sharing choice', () => {
    const storage = () => {
        const values = new Map();
        return { getItem: key => values.get(key) ?? null, setItem: (key, value) => values.set(key, value) };
    };

    it('follows the permission unless turned off', () => {
        expect(shareDefault(null, 'granted')).toBe(true);
        expect(shareDefault('on', 'granted')).toBe(true);
        expect(shareDefault('off', 'granted')).toBe(false);
        expect(shareDefault('on', 'denied')).toBe(false);
        expect(shareDefault('on', 'prompt')).toBe(false);
        expect(shareDefault(null, 'prompt')).toBe(false);
        expect(shareDefault('on', null)).toBe(true);
        expect(shareDefault(null, null)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('remembers only on and off', () => {
        const store = storage();
        expect(readShare(store)).toBeNull();
        writeShare(store, 'on');
        expect(store.getItem(SHARE_KEY)).toBe('on');
        expect(readShare(store)).toBe('on');
        store.setItem(SHARE_KEY, 'maybe');
        expect(readShare(store)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('survives storage that throws', () => {
        const broken = { getItem: () => { throw new Error('denied'); }, setItem: () => { throw new Error('denied'); } };
        expect(readShare(broken)).toBeNull();
        expect(() => writeShare(broken, 'on')).not.toThrow();
        expect(readShare(undefined)).toBeNull();
    });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    LOCAL_CAP,
    OFFER_LIMIT,
    PALM_SIZE,
    PEN_GRACE_MS,
    RESOLUTION,
    Result,
    backingSize,
    brushWidth,
    chunkStrokes,
    drawPath,
    farEnough,
    isPalm,
    isUuid7,
    later,
    parseDrawReply,
    pickOffers,
    quantize,
    shouldOfferReset,
    timeKey,
    toPoint,
    unseen,
} from '../../src/Chaos.Web/Components/Islands/CanvasIsland.razor.js';

describe('coordinates', () => {
    it('quantizes and clamps to the shared grid', () => {
        expect(quantize(0)).toBe(0);
        expect(quantize(1)).toBe(RESOLUTION);
        expect(quantize(-0.5)).toBe(0);
        expect(quantize(1.5)).toBe(RESOLUTION);
        expect(quantize(0.5)).toBe(2048);
    });

    it('maps client coordinates into the canvas box', () => {
        expect(toPoint(150, 250, { left: 100, top: 200, width: 100, height: 100 })).toEqual([2048, 2048]);
    });

    it('drops points that are too close together', () => {
        expect(farEnough([], 0, 0)).toBe(true);
        expect(farEnough([10, 10], 11, 11)).toBe(false);
        expect(farEnough([10, 10], 13, 10)).toBe(true);
    });
});

describe('palm rejection', () => {
    it('never rejects a pen or a mouse', () => {
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'pen', width: 99, height: 99 }, 0, 0)).toBe(false);
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'mouse', width: 99, height: 99 }, 0, 0)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('rejects broad touches', () => {
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'touch', width: PALM_SIZE + 1, height: 5 }, -Infinity, 0)).toBe(true);
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'touch', width: 10, height: 10 }, -Infinity, 0)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('rejects touches right after a pen was used', () => {
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'touch', width: 1, height: 1 }, 1000, 1000 + PEN_GRACE_MS - 1)).toBe(true);
        expect(isPalm({ pointerType: 'touch', width: 1, height: 1 }, 1000, 1000 + PEN_GRACE_MS)).toBe(false);
    });
});

describe('sizing', () => {
    it('uses device pixels for the backing store', () => {
        expect(backingSize(300, 2)).toBe(600);
        expect(backingSize(0.2, 1)).toBe(1);
    });

    it('scales the brush with the canvas', () => {
        expect(brushWidth(1000)).toBe(6);
        expect(brushWidth(10)).toBe(1);
    });
});

describe('remote strokes', () => {
    it('keeps only points not seen before', () => {
        expect(unseen(0, 0, [1, 2, 3, 4])).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4]);
        expect(unseen(1, 0, [1, 2, 3, 4])).toEqual([3, 4]);
        expect(unseen(2, 0, [1, 2, 3, 4])).toEqual([]);
        expect(unseen(2, 2, [5, 6])).toEqual([5, 6]);
    });

    it('reports a gap', () => {
        expect(unseen(1, 3, [5, 6])).toBeNull();
    });

    it('matches the server result codes', () => {
        expect(Result).toEqual({ Accepted: 0, Stale: 1, TooFast: 2, Invalid: 3, Full: 4, TooLong: 5 });
    });
});

describe('drawPath', () => {
    const recorder = () => {
        const calls = [];
        return {
            calls,
            lineWidth: 4,
            beginPath: () => calls.push(['begin']),
            arc: (x, y, r) => calls.push(['arc', x, y, r]),
            fill: () => calls.push(['fill']),
            moveTo: (x, y) => calls.push(['move', x, y]),
            lineTo: (x, y) => calls.push(['line', x, y]),
            stroke: () => calls.push(['stroke']),
        };
    };

    it('draws a dot for a single point', () => {
        const context = recorder();
        drawPath(context, RESOLUTION, [10, 20], 0);
        expect(context.calls).toEqual([['begin'], ['arc', 10, 20, 2], ['fill']]);
    });

    it('connects new points to the previous one', () => {
        const context = recorder();
        drawPath(context, RESOLUTION, [0, 0, 10, 10, 20, 20], 4);
        expect(context.calls).toEqual([['begin'], ['move', 10, 10], ['line', 20, 20], ['stroke']]);
    });

    it('does nothing without new points', () => {
        const context = recorder();
        drawPath(context, RESOLUTION, [1, 1, 2, 2], 4);
        expect(context.calls).toEqual([]);
    });
});

describe('persistence', () => {
    const id = (ms, tail = 1) => {
        const hex = ms.toString(16).padStart(12, '0');
        return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8)}-7000-8000-${String(tail).padStart(12, '0')}`;
    };

    it('reads draw replies', () => {
        expect(parseDrawReply('0 0199e1aa-bbbb-7ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee')).toEqual({ result: Result.Accepted, stroke: '0199e1aa-bbbb-7ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee' });
        expect(parseDrawReply('2')).toEqual({ result: Result.TooFast, stroke: null });
        expect(parseDrawReply('0 not-an-id')).toEqual({ result: Result.Accepted, stroke: null });
        expect(parseDrawReply(7)).toEqual({ result: Result.Invalid, stroke: null });
    });

    it('builds a key bound that sorts before every id from that millisecond', () => {
        const at = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 16);
        expect(id(at - 1) < timeKey(at)).toBe(true);
        expect(id(at) > timeKey(at)).toBe(true);
        expect(isUuid7(id(at))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('picks the later reset', () => {
        expect(later(null, null)).toBeNull();
        expect(later(id(5), null)).toBe(id(5));
        expect(later(id(5), id(9))).toBe(id(9));
    });

    it('offers a remembered reset only when it is newer and from before the restart', () => {
        expect(shouldOfferReset(id(5), null, 10)).toBe(true);
        expect(shouldOfferReset(id(5), id(4), 10)).toBe(true);
        expect(shouldOfferReset(id(5), id(6), 10)).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldOfferReset(id(12), null, 10)).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldOfferReset(null, null, 10)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('picks strokes after the reset, before the restart, within the room left', () => {
        const strokes = [
            { id: id(30), points: [1, 1] },
            { id: id(9), points: [1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3] },
            { id: id(8), points: [1, 1] },
            { id: id(7), points: [1, 1] },
            { id: id(3), points: [1, 1] },
        ];
        const picked = pickOffers(strokes, new Set([id(7)]), id(5), 20, 2);
        expect(picked.map(stroke => stroke.id)).toEqual([id(8)]);
        expect(pickOffers(strokes, new Set(), null, 20, 100, 2).map(stroke => stroke.id)).toEqual([id(9), id(8)]);
    });

    it('chunks offers under the message limit', () => {
        const stroke = { id: id(1), points: Array(1000).fill(4095) };
        const chunks = chunkStrokes([stroke, stroke, stroke, stroke, stroke, stroke], 12000);
        expect(chunks.length).toBe(3);
        expect(chunks.every(chunk => chunk.length === 2)).toBe(true);
        expect(chunkStrokes([{ id: id(1), points: Array(5000).fill(1) }], 1000)).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('keeps local history bounded', () => {
        expect(LOCAL_CAP).toBe(10000);
        expect(OFFER_LIMIT).toBe(500);
    });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const RUNS = [
    ['google=12,3;aws-us-east-1=40,', [{ key: 'google', browser: 12, server: 3 }, { key: 'aws-us-east-1', browser: 40, server: null }]],
    ['chaos=,', [{ key: 'chaos', browser: null, server: null }]],
    ['a=0,60000', [{ key: 'a', browser: 0, server: 60000 }]],
];

const INVALID = ['', 'google', 'google=1', 'google=01,2', 'Google=1,2', 'a=1,2;a=3,4', 'a=60001,1', 'a=-1,1', 'a=1,2,3', '=1,2', 'a=1.5,2'];

describe('run text', () => {
    it.each(RUNS)('parses %s', (text, expected) => {
        expect(parseRun(text)).toEqual(expected);
    });

    it.each(INVALID)('rejects %s', text => {
        expect(parseRun(text)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('summarizes with medians and answer counts', () => {
        const results = parseRun('a=10,1;b=30,;c=,5;d=,');
        expect(summarize(results)).toEqual({ browser: 20, server: 3, answered: 3, total: 4 });
    });

    it('rounds even medians half up', () => {
        expect(median([1, 2])).toBe(2);
        expect(median([3, null, 1, 2])).toBe(2);
        expect(median([null])).toBeNull();
    });

    it('formats milliseconds', () => {
        expect(ms(12)).toBe('12 ms');
        expect(ms(null)).toBe('–');
    });
});

const RANKS = [
    ['a=10,1;b=30,;c=,5;d=,', { browser: 20, server: 3 }],
    ['a=5,;b=,;c=,', { browser: null, server: null }],
    ['a=5,200;b=7,150;c=,', { browser: 6, server: 175 }],
    ['chaos=3,', { browser: 3, server: null }],
];

function run(index, browser, server = '') {
    return { id: `0199e1aa-0000-7000-8000-${String(index).padStart(12, '0')}`, text: `a=${browser},${server}` };
}

describe('leaderboards', () => {
    it.each(RANKS)('ranks %s only with a quorum', (text, expected) => {
        expect(rank(parseRun(text))).toEqual(expected);
    });

    it('draws the jackpot lines at 10 and 100 ms', () => {
        expect([isFast(9), isFast(10), isFast(null)]).toEqual([true, false, false]);
        expect([isSlow(99), isSlow(100), isSlow(null)]).toEqual([false, true, false]);
        expect([qualifies(0, 'fast'), qualifies(0, 'slow'), qualifies(250, 'slow')]).toEqual([true, false, true]);
    });

    it('offers the fastest and slowest runs on each side, once each', () => {
        const records = [run(1, 50, 9), run(2, 40), run(3, 40), run(4, 70), run(5, 60, 300), { id: 'x', text: 'bad' }, { text: 'a=1,1' }];
        const ids = leadersOf(records, 2).map(record => record.id);
        expect(ids).toEqual([records[1].id, records[2].id, records[3].id, records[4].id, records[0].id]);
        expect(leadersOf([])).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('reads board ids defensively', () => {
        const ids = boardIds({ fastJackpots: ['a'], serverSlowest: 'nope' });
        expect(Object.keys(ids)).toEqual(BOARDS.map(board => board.key));
        expect(ids.fastJackpots).toEqual(['a']);
        expect(ids.serverSlowest).toEqual([]);
        expect(boardIds(undefined).browserFastest).toEqual([]);
    });
});

describe('start replies', () => {
    it('reads every reply', () => {
        expect(parseStartReply('ok 0199e1aa-bbbb-7ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee 300')).toEqual({ kind: 'ok', id: '0199e1aa-bbbb-7ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee', seconds: 300 });
        expect(parseStartReply('busy')).toEqual({ kind: 'busy' });
        expect(parseStartReply('wait 42')).toEqual({ kind: 'wait', seconds: 42 });
        expect(parseStartReply('wait x')).toEqual({ kind: 'error' });
        expect(parseStartReply('ok nope 300')).toEqual({ kind: 'error' });
        expect(parseStartReply(null)).toEqual({ kind: 'error' });
    });
});

describe('text helpers', () => {
    it('counts down in minutes and seconds', () => {
        expect(clockText(0)).toBe('0:00');
        expect(clockText(299_001)).toBe('5:00');
        expect(clockText(61_000)).toBe('1:01');
    });

    it('counts towards auto run', () => {
        expect(progressText(0)).toBe(`${AUTO_UNLOCK} more tests unlock auto run.`);
        expect(progressText(AUTO_UNLOCK - 1)).toBe('1 more test unlocks auto run.');
        expect(progressText(12)).toBe('12 tests from this browser.');
    });

    it('reads stored numbers defensively', () => {
        expect(readNumber({ getItem: () => '7' }, 'k')).toBe(7);
        expect(readNumber({ getItem: () => 'x' }, 'k')).toBe(0);
        expect(readNumber({ getItem() { throw new Error('denied'); } }, 'k')).toBe(0);
    });
});

describe('requests', () => {
    it('sends opaque requests without credentials to other sites', () => {
        expect(fetchOptions('https://example.com/', null)).toMatchObject({ mode: 'no-cors', cache: 'no-store', credentials: 'omit', referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer' });
        expect(fetchOptions('/time', null).mode).toBeUndefined();
        expect(cacheBusted('/time', 3)).toBe('/time?n=3');
        expect(cacheBusted('https://example.com/a?b=1', 3)).toBe('https://example.com/a?b=1');
    });

    const fake = (durations, failAfter = Infinity) => {
        let clock = 0;
        let calls = 0;
        return {
            calls: () => calls,
            deps: {
                now: () => clock,
                setTimeout: () => 0,
                clearTimeout: () => {},
                AbortController,
                fetch: async () => {
                    calls += 1;
                    if (calls > failAfter) {
                        throw new Error('down');
                    }
                    clock += durations[(calls - 1) % durations.length];
                    return {};
                },
            },
        };
    };

    it('warms up and keeps the fastest of three', async () => {
        const probe = fake([100, 30, 20, 25]);
        expect(await measure('https://example.com/', probe.deps)).toBe(20);
        expect(probe.calls()).toBe(4);
    });

    it('gives up when the warm up fails', async () => {
        const probe = fake([5], 0);
        expect(await measure('https://example.com/', probe.deps)).toBeNull();
        expect(probe.calls()).toBe(1);
    });

    it('keeps what it has when a later sample fails', async () => {
        const probe = fake([50, 9], 2);
        expect(await measure('https://example.com/', probe.deps)).toBe(9);
    });

    it('measures every endpoint and reports each one', async () => {
        const probe = fake([4]);
        const seen = [];
        const results = await measureAll(['a', 'b', 'c'], probe.deps, 1000, (index, value) => seen.push([index, value]), 1);
        expect(results).toEqual([4, 4, 4]);
        expect(seen.map(entry => entry[0]).sort()).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);
    });
});

describe('winners', () => {
    it.each([
        ['7Q7A7', 'three'],
        ['XXXXB', 'four'],
        ['KKKKK', 'five'],
        ['13AKZ', 'up'],
        ['ZKA80', 'down'],
        ['ABCDE', 'sequenceUp'],
        ['789AB', 'sequenceUp'],
        ['VWXYZ', 'sequenceUp'],
        ['43210', 'sequenceDown'],
        ['BA987', 'sequenceDown'],
        ['YZ012', 'none'],
        ['ABCDF', 'up'],
        ['ZK8A0', 'none'],
        ['AABBC', 'none'],
        ['ABCDD', 'none'],
    ])('classifies %s as %s', (text, expected) => {
        expect(classify(text)).toBe(expected);
    });

    it('labels every kind of win', () => {
        expect(Object.keys(LABELS).sort()).toEqual(['down', 'five', 'four', 'sequenceDown', 'sequenceUp', 'three', 'up']);
    });

    it('marks the symbols that won', () => {
        expect(hits('7Q7A7')).toEqual([true, false, true, false, true]);
        expect(hits('ABCDE')).toEqual([true, true, true, true, true]);
        expect(hits('43210')).toEqual([true, true, true, true, true]);
        expect(hits('AABBC')).toEqual([false, false, false, false, false]);
    });
});

describe('reels', () => {
    it('stops left to right inside the spin', () => {
        const stops = stopTimes(4000);
        expect(stops).toHaveLength(5);
        expect([...stops].sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual(stops);
        expect(stops[4]).toBeLessThan(4000);
        expect(stopTimes(0)).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
    });

    it('builds a strip that starts on the current face and ends on the target', () => {
        const strip = stripFor('A', 'Q', 4, () => 0);
        expect(strip).toEqual(['R', 'Q', 'P', '0', '0', '0', '0', 'B', 'A', '9']);
    });

    it('moves the strip from its last window to its first', () => {
        const frames = spinFrames(10);
        expect(frames[0].transform).toBe('translateY(-70%)');
        expect(frames.at(-1).transform).toBe('translateY(0%)');
    });
});

describe('presses', () => {
    const storage = value => ({ getItem: key => (key === PRESS_KEY ? value : null) });

    it('reads a stored count', () => {
        expect(readPresses(storage('12'))).toBe(12);
        expect(readPresses(storage('nope'))).toBe(0);
        expect(readPresses(storage(null))).toBe(0);
        expect(readPresses({ getItem() { throw new Error('denied'); } })).toBe(0);
    });

    it('counts down to auto spin', () => {
        expect(progressText(0)).toBe(`${AUTO_UNLOCK} more successful presses unlock auto spin.`);
        expect(progressText(AUTO_UNLOCK - 1)).toBe('1 more successful press unlocks auto spin.');
        expect(progressText(AUTO_UNLOCK)).toContain('successful presses from this browser');
    });

    it('understands server replies', () => {
        expect(parseReply('busy')).toEqual({ kind: 'busy', id: null });
        expect(parseReply('slow')).toEqual({ kind: 'slow', id: null });
        expect(parseReply('0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000')).toEqual({ kind: 'accepted', id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000' });
        expect(parseReply(undefined).kind).toBe('error');
    });

    it('waits a little before an automatic press', () => {
        expect(autoDelay(() => 0)).toBe(200);
        expect(autoDelay(() => 1)).toBe(1000);
    });
});

describe('odds', () => {
    it('counts every kind the same way an exhaustive search does', () => {
        const symbols = '89ABCD';
        const counts = { none: 0, three: 0, four: 0, five: 0, up: 0, down: 0, sequenceUp: 0, sequenceDown: 0 };
        const total = symbols.length ** 5;
        for (let index = 0; index < total; index += 1) {
            let text = '';
            for (let rest = index, reel = 0; reel < 5; reel += 1, rest = Math.floor(rest / symbols.length)) {
                text += symbols[rest % symbols.length];
            }
            counts[classify(text)] += 1;
        }
        for (const kind of Object.keys(LABELS)) {
            expect(counts[kind]).toBe(waysOf(kind, symbols.length));
        }
    });

    it('knows the real totals', () => {
        expect(OUTCOMES).toBe(60_466_176);
        expect(waysOf('five')).toBe(36);
        expect(waysOf('four')).toBe(6_300);
        expect(waysOf('three')).toBe(441_000);
        expect(waysOf('up')).toBe(376_960);
        expect(waysOf('down')).toBe(376_960);
        expect(waysOf('sequenceUp')).toBe(32);
        expect(waysOf('sequenceDown')).toBe(32);
        expect(SEQUENCES).toBe(64);
        expect(JACKPOT_WAYS).toBe(6_400);
        expect(waysOf('none')).toBe(0);
        expect(WINNING).toBe(1_201_320);
        expect(FULL_HOUSE).toBe(12_600);
        expect(ALL_DIFFERENT).toBe(45_239_040);
        expect(choose(36, 5)).toBe(376_992);
        expect(choose(5, 6)).toBe(0);
    });

    it('formats chances for people', () => {
        expect(oneIn(WINNING)).toBe('50.3');
        expect(oneIn(waysOf('four'))).toBe('9,598');
        expect(oneIn(SEQUENCES)).toBe('944,784');
        expect(oneIn(JACKPOT_WAYS)).toBe('9,448');
        expect(percent(waysOf('three') / OUTCOMES)).toBe('0.729%');
        expect(percent(waysOf('five') / OUTCOMES)).toBe('0.0000595%');
        expect(duration(4000)).toBe('4 seconds');
        expect(duration(1000)).toBe('1 second');
        expect(duration(548_000)).toBe('9.1 minutes');
        expect(duration(36 ** 4 * 4000)).toBe('78 days');
    });

    it('separates the median wait from the average', () => {
        expect(medianWait(0.5)).toBe(1);
        expect(medianWait(waysOf('three') / OUTCOMES)).toBe(95);
        expect(headsInARow(1 / 8192)).toBe(13);
        expect(headsInARow(0.9)).toBe(1);
    });

    it('spots three with a pair', () => {
        expect(isFullHouse('77QQ7')).toBe(true);
        expect(isFullHouse('7Q7A7')).toBe(false);
        expect(isFullHouse('ABCDE')).toBe(false);
    });

    it('explains only winners', () => {
        expect(lesson('ZK8A0')).toBeNull();
        const four = lesson('XXXXB', 4000);
        expect(four.kind).toBe('four');
        expect(four.headline).toBe('1 in 9,598');
        expect(four.facts.map(([term]) => term)).toContain('Half the time within');
        expect(four.sections.some(([, text]) => text.includes('XXXXB'))).toBe(true);
        expect(lesson('77QQ7').facts.at(-1)[1]).toBe('12,600 ways, 1 in 4,799');
        expect(lesson('ZKA80').sections[0][1]).toContain('falls');
        const run = lesson('ABCDE');
        expect(run.kind).toBe('sequenceUp');
        expect(run.facts).toContainEqual(['Either direction', '64 ways, 1 in 944,784']);
        expect(run.facts).toContainEqual(['Any jackpot', '6,400 ways, 1 in 9,448']);
        expect(lesson('43210').sections[0][1]).toContain('43210');
        expect(lesson('13AKZ').facts.map(([term]) => term)).not.toContain('Any jackpot');
    });

    it('offers the newest winners from local history', () => {
        const records = [
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000001', text: 'AAAB1' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000002', text: 'ZK8A0' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000003', text: '13AKZ' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000004', text: 'bad' },
        ];
        expect(newestWinners(records).map(record => record.text)).toEqual(['13AKZ', 'AAAB1']);
        expect(newestWinners(records, 1)).toHaveLength(1);
    });
});

describe('jackpots', () => {
    it.each([
        ['XXXXB', true],
        ['KKKKK', true],
        ['7Q7A7', false],
        ['ABCDE', true],
        ['43210', true],
        ['VWXYZ', true],
        ['13AKZ', false],
        ['YZ012', false],
        ['ZK8A0', false],
        ['kkkkk', false],
        [null, false],
    ])('knows whether %s is a jackpot', (text, expected) => {
        expect(isJackpot(text)).toBe(expected);
    });

    it('shouts about jackpots in the verdict', () => {
        expect(verdictText('KKKKK')).toBe('Jackpot! Five of a kind');
        expect(verdictText('XXXXB')).toBe('Jackpot! Four of a kind');
        expect(verdictText('ABCDE')).toBe('Jackpot! Sequence up');
        expect(verdictText('43210')).toBe('Jackpot! Sequence down');
        expect(verdictText('13AKZ')).toBe('Straight up');
        expect(verdictText('7Q7A7')).toBe('Three of a kind');
        expect(verdictText('ZK8A0')).toBe('');
    });

    it('keeps jackpots in their own store added by the second schema version', () => {
        expect(DB_VERSION).toBe(2);
        expect(migrations).toHaveLength(2);
        const created = [];
        upgrade({ createObjectStore: (name, options) => created.push([name, options]) }, 1, DB_VERSION, migrations);
        expect(created).toEqual([[JACKPOTS, { keyPath: 'id' }]]);
        expect(JACKPOTS).not.toBe(STORE);
    });

    it('offers every jackpot newest first', () => {
        const records = [
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000001', text: 'XXXXB' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000002', text: 'AAAB1' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000003', text: 'KKKKK' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000004', text: 'kkkkk' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000005', text: '13AKZ' },
            { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000006', text: '43210' },
        ];
        expect(newestJackpots(records).map(record => record.text)).toEqual(['43210', 'KKKKK', 'XXXXB']);
        expect(newestJackpots(records, 1)).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(JACKPOT_OFFERS).toBe(500);
        expect(WINNER_OFFERS).toBe(500);
    });

    it('celebrates for a few seconds', () => {
        expect(CELEBRATE_MS).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3000);
        expect(CELEBRATE_MS).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15000);
    });

    it('reads and writes whole stores', async () => {
        const rows = new Map();
        const db = {
            transaction(store, mode) {
                const transaction = {
                    objectStore(name) {
                        expect(name).toBe(store);
                        return {
                            put(record) {
                                expect(mode).toBe('readwrite');
                                rows.set(record.id, record);
                            },
                            getAll() {
                                const request = {};
                                queueMicrotask(() => {
                                    request.result = [...rows.values()];
                                    request.onsuccess();
                                });
                                return request;
                            },
                        };
                    },
                };
                queueMicrotask(() => transaction.oncomplete?.());
                return transaction;
            },
        };
        await putAll(db, JACKPOTS, []);
        expect(rows.size).toBe(0);
        await putAll(db, JACKPOTS, [{ id: 'a', text: 'KKKKK' }, { id: 'b', text: 'XXXXB' }]);
        expect((await readAll(db, JACKPOTS)).map(record => record.text)).toEqual(['KKKKK', 'XXXXB']);
    });
});
